# Project Memory — Master Log

Full record of every task done on this project. Quick-scan titles live in [INDEX.md](./INDEX.md).

## How this works
- Every completed task gets **one entry here** (full detail) and **one line in INDEX.md** (title only).
- IDs are sequential 4-digit (`0001`, `0002`, …). The INDEX row and the master heading share the same ID.
- Newest entries go at the **bottom**.
- **Rollover rule:** when this file passes ~500 lines, the oldest entries move into `docs/memory/archive/MASTER_MEMORY_<range>.md` and INDEX rows are updated to point there. This honors the CLAUDE.md "files under 500 lines" rule and keeps the active log small enough to read into context.

## Entry template
```
## [NNNN] Title — YYYY-MM-DD (branch)
**What:** what was actually done
**Why:** the reason / goal
**Files:** paths touched
**Verified:** how it was confirmed (command run + result), or "not verified — <reason>"
**Notes:** decisions, tradeoffs, follow-ups, links to [[related]]
```

---

## [0001] Set up project memory system (INDEX + MASTER_MEMORY) — 2026-05-29 (Nggaev)
**What:** Created `docs/memory/INDEX.md` (titles only) and `docs/memory/MASTER_MEMORY.md` (full per-task detail), with a defined entry template and a 500-line rollover-to-archive rule.
**Why:** User wants a persistent, project-scoped "memory of the project" — a worklog separate from Claude Code's personal `.claude` memory — so any future session can see what was done.
**Files:** `docs/memory/INDEX.md`, `docs/memory/MASTER_MEMORY.md`
**Verified:** Both files created on disk (Write tool confirmed paths). Not yet committed to git.
**Notes:** Placed under `docs/` because CLAUDE.md forbids working files in repo root. Two-file design per user request; added rollover rule to prevent the master file growing past the 500-line limit and becoming unreadable into context.

## [0002] Document memory system in CLAUDE.md for other sessions — 2026-05-29 (Nggaev)
**What:** Added a `## Project Memory (read at session start)` section to `CLAUDE.md`, instructing future sessions to read `INDEX.md` first, read `MASTER_MEMORY.md` for detail, and append an entry + index row (shared sequential ID) when completing a real task.
**Why:** User wants other Claude sessions in this directory to know how to use the memory system. CLAUDE.md is auto-loaded into every session's context, so it's the only place instructions reliably reach other sessions (a README in `docs/memory/` would go unread).
**Files:** `CLAUDE.md`
**Verified:** Edit applied successfully (tool confirmed). Section placed directly after the Rules block. Not committed to git.
**Notes:** Restated the honest-`Verified`-field rule and the 500-line rollover rule inside CLAUDE.md so the convention survives without this log being read first.

A fresh branch off DaddysBranch avoids force-pushing the shared Nggaev and avoids a messy 4-commit rebase that would have dragged in a stale test suite (tests for the now-deleted `gemini.py`) and add-then-delete CLAUDE.md churn.

CLI router present (`app/services/providers/{base,claude,codex,gemini,kimi}.py`, `app/services/agent.py`); old `app/services/gemini.py` absent. Branch upstream detached (won't push to DaddysBranch). Not pushed to a remote yet.
**Notes:** Decisions confirmed with user: (a) keep DaddysBranch's tracked architecture CLAUDE.md rather than re-applying the earlier gitignore-CLAUDE.md chore; (b) the old-Gemini `tests/unit/*` suite from Nggaev was intentionally dropped (it tests deleted code). **Phase 1 plan must be adapted: providers are CLI-based (subprocess), not API/SDK** — the gateway/task→model/fallback design targets the CLI router in `app/services/agent.py`, not `google-genai`. See [[0001]] for the worklog system.

## [0004] Phase 1 — Flow v2 schemas + provider fallback + task→model policy — 2026-05-29 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Implemented (TDD, test-first) the adapted Phase 1 from the pure-content-automation roadmap §2. (1) New `app/schemas/flow_v2.py`: `GenerationProfile` (difficulty as metadata, no phase-skipping), `DecisionProcessExplanation` (`options` pinned to None so it can never be an MCQ; `expected_components` ∈ concept/method/mistake), `CaseBasedPreview` + submodels (`CaseSetup`, `CaseCheckpoint`, `CaseSimulation`, `FeedbackSummary`, `CompletionRules`) enforcing exactly-3 checkpoints + required DPE + simulation correct/wrong paths + non-empty source_concept_ids, `WeakPointSignal`, and a lean `SourceMap`/`SourceConcept` mock contract with `concept_ids()`. Registered all in `app/schemas/__init__.py`. (2) Added `ProviderSpec` + `run_phase_with_fallback` to `app/services/agent.py` — ordered provider chain, each link keeping its own one-shot repair retry (via existing `run_phase`), advancing on spawn error / non-zero rc / validation-exhaustion, raising once if all fail. (3) Added `resolve_provider_chain` task→model policy: extract-family tasks pinned to `settings.extract_provider/extract_model`; other tasks → job provider primary + remaining CLIs as fallbacks (no dup, job_model on primary only).
**Why:** Roadmap Phase 1 = "AI Gateway + Flow v2 schemas". On DaddysBranch the gateway already existed (providers/ + agent.py with structured-validate + one repair retry), so the only real code gaps were **provider fallback** (acceptance #3) and the Flow v2 content schemas; the other two acceptance criteria (structured validates, repair retry) were already covered by `tests/services/test_agent.py`.
**Files:** new `app/schemas/flow_v2.py`, `tests/schemas/__init__.py`, `tests/schemas/test_flow_v2_schemas.py`, `tests/services/test_agent_fallback.py`, `tests/services/test_agent_policy.py`; edited `app/schemas/__init__.py`, `app/services/agent.py`.
**Verified:** `uv run python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **67 passed** (was 43 baseline; +15 schema, +5 fallback, +4 policy). Each new test watched fail first (RED) before implementing (import errors / ValidationErrors), then pass (GREEN). Env: had to bootstrap toolchain — installed `uv` via pip, `uv sync --extra dev --python 3.13` (system Python is 3.14, too new for some wheels). **Smoke test (zero LLM quota):** full-app import (main/FastAPI + agent + schemas) clean; REAL fallback path (codex+kimi not on PATH) drives actual `_spawn`/`_resolve_binary` and aggregates to the right RuntimeError. This surfaced a Windows bug the mocked unit tests missed — the fallback error message used a Unicode arrow `→` that crashes cp1252 stdout with `UnicodeEncodeError`; fixed to ASCII `->`. **Real-LLM smoke via the `claude` CLI (user logged in): all PASS** — (A) direct `run_phase(schema=Ping)` → validated; (B) `run_phase_with_fallback([codex→claude])` real fallback recovery → validated; (C) real claude generated a full `CaseBasedPreview` that passed the new schema (exactly 3 checkpoints w/ correct intents, DPE no-options, simulation both paths). Stress/concurrency test not run (codex+kimi not installed; low marginal value over A/B/C).
**Notes:** Code folded into existing `app/` layout (schemas in `app/schemas/`, gateway logic in `app/services/agent.py`) per user decision, superseding the earlier "new app/flow_v2/ package" idea. Committed as `f6978ca` and pushed to `origin/Nggaev-v2` (along with the docs commit `056bb70`); branch now tracks `origin/Nggaev-v2`. Out of scope (Phases 2-7): real ingestion/SourceMap builder, the actual CBP/flashcards/games generators, content QA, Markdown assembly. Pipeline wiring of `run_phase_with_fallback`/`resolve_provider_chain` into `pipeline.py` is deferred (current pipeline still calls `run_phase` directly). See [[0003]]. **Superseded in part by [[0001]]→entry 0005: the fallback + policy + WeakPointSignal added here were pruned when the updated plan landed.**

## [0006] PR-1 — source map (extract → structured SourceMap) — 2026-05-29 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Implemented PR-1 on the CLI-router line (NOT reusing alphaq, which is on the old gemini-SDK base). Chose a **refined Option B** over the plan's recommended Option A: instead of rewiring the markdown `extract` + cross-job cache, added a **second, text-only structured call** that derives the SourceMap **from the already-extracted `lesson_context`** (no PDF re-read; cheap; pinned to `settings.extract_provider/model`; non-breaking to the markdown contract every downstream phase consumes). Changes: (1) added required `statement` field to `SourceConcept` (the atomic textbook fact); (2) `agent.extract_source_map(...)` — wraps `run_phase(schema=SourceMap)`, new `_SOURCE_MAP_PROMPT`; (3) persistence: `source_map_json` JSONB col on `homework_jobs` (alembic `0012`, rev `e3a7c1d9b4f2` ← `c8e1d4b27a91`), repo `set_source_map_json`, `JobOut.source_map_json`; (4) pipeline: after `extract` captures `lesson_context`, call `extract_source_map`, persist, emit `source_map_ready` event — **best-effort (logged, non-fatal)** since no downstream phase consumes the map yet.
**Why:** Plan §4 — every Flow v2 phase must cite source-map concept IDs; the flat extract summary isn't structured. Option B keeps the working extract + cache untouched and is cleanly testable; can collapse to one-call Option A later purely for cost.
**Files:** `app/schemas/flow_v2.py`, `tests/schemas/test_flow_v2_schemas.py`, `app/services/agent.py`, `tests/services/test_agent_source_map.py` (new), `app/models/homework_job.py`, `alembic/versions/0012_homework_source_map_json.py` (new), `app/repositories/jobs.py`, `app/schemas/job.py`, `app/services/pipeline.py`.
**Verified:** `pytest tests/ -q` → **58 passed** (was 55; +1 schema, +2 agent). Each new test watched fail first (RED) then pass. **Real `claude` CLI smoke:** `extract_source_map` produced a valid `SourceMap` from a sample lesson_context — 5 concepts, stable kebab IDs, varied kinds (term/formula/process/skill/fact), faithful statements. Import smoke: pipeline/main/JobOut/model column all load. **NOT verified:** the alembic migration is NOT applied here (no DB) — run `uv run alembic upgrade head` on a real DB; and the pipeline wiring itself has no integration test on this branch (no DB harness) — logic is unit+smoke-tested, the glue is import-checked + reviewed.
**Notes:** Best-effort persistence is intentional for PR-1 (downstream consumption is PR-2+). When PR-2 makes phases cite concept IDs, consider promoting source-map failure to fatal (plan: "extraction grounded wrong → re-extract"). See [[0005]] for plan context; alphaq's `SkillRegistry` (entry context in 0005 / branch audit) remains a reference for adding target-skills later.

## [0007] PR-4 — Boss Arena (Why→How→What) content phase — 2026-05-29 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Implemented Boss Arena as **content only** (option A), per user: a static set of reasoning questions, NOT a runtime/dynamic generator (dynamic adaptation belongs to the downstream homework-builder platform, out of scope). (1) New `app/schemas/boss_arena.py`: `BossArena` + `BossQuestion` — each question has `concept_ids` (≥1, references SourceMap), `difficulty` (easy/medium/hard), `scenario`, and **`why`/`how`/`what` all required & non-empty** (the defining rule), plus bloom/pisa, base_damage, hints, correct/partial/wrong feedback. **No `options` field at all** → structurally cannot be MCQ. `questions` min_length 4. (2) Authored one spec-derived `prompts/<subject>/boss-arena.md` and applied it to **all 7 subjects** (subject-general; nuance comes from lesson_context). (3) Registered `STRUCTURED_PHASE_SCHEMAS["boss-arena"]` + added to `_SVG_PHASES`. (4) `flows.py`: swapped `final-challenge`→`boss-arena` in every `SUBJECT_FLOWS` sequence, added boss-arena `PHASE_DEPS` (preview+flashcards+memory-sprint), repointed `reflection`'s dep → boss-arena. (5) Persistence/assembly: `boss_arena_json` JSONB column (alembic `0013`, rev `f4b8d2e6a9c1` ← `e3a7c1d9b4f2`), repo `set_boss_arena_json`, `JobOut.boss_arena_json`, pipeline `_JSON_COLUMN_SETTERS["boss-arena"]`, and a Why→How→What markdown summary branch.
**Why:** PR-4 — Boss is reasoning content (Why→How→What), replacing the quiz-style `final-challenge`. Content-only scope confirmed by user (no runtime, no deletion of old final-challenge, no frontend work).
**Files:** new `app/schemas/boss_arena.py`, `tests/schemas/test_boss_arena_schemas.py`, `alembic/versions/0013_homework_boss_arena_json.py`, `prompts/{biology,english,geometriya-g7-11,history,kimyo-g7-11,math-algebra,physics}/boss-arena.md`; edited `app/schemas/__init__.py`, `app/services/agent.py`, `app/services/flows.py`, `app/models/homework_job.py`, `app/repositories/jobs.py`, `app/schemas/job.py`, `app/services/pipeline.py`.
**Verified:** `pytest tests/ -q` → **64 passed** (+6 boss schema, TDD red-first). **Real `claude` CLI smoke** (via the subprocess router, no SDK): generated a 6-question Uzbek Boss pack — every Q had non-empty why/how/what, ≥1 concept_id, valid difficulty, 3 hints. Import smoke: schema/model col/JobOut/pipeline setter all load. **NOT verified:** migration `0013` not applied (no DB) — run `uv run alembic upgrade head`; pipeline wiring has no integration test (no DB harness) — logic is unit+smoke-tested, glue reviewed.
**Notes:** `final-challenge` left **dormant** per decision — its schema/column/prompts and the React `boss-fight` frontend stay, but it's no longer in any flow, so new jobs produce `boss_arena_json` not `final_challenge_json`; the preview's boss section will be empty until the QA console is pointed at the new shape (frontend out of scope per plan). "Referenced questions" = each Q carries `concept_ids`; currently model-generated slugs grounded in `lesson_context`. Strict alignment to the actual SourceMap IDs (threading the source map into the boss phase context) is a deferred light follow-up. See [[0006]] for the source map it references.

## [0009] PR-3 Practice Arc — six typed games built + wired (TDD, 168 green, claude smoke OK) — 2026-05-29 (flow-v2-integration)
**What:** Built the Practice Arc (the "still feels like old flow" cleanup from [[0008]]). Replaced the generic `game-breaks` + standalone `real-life` + `consolidation` with six typed, source-traced conceptual games. Did NOT wholesale-merge alphaq (verified-not-usable in [[0008]]) — built ourselves against OUR `flow_v2.SourceMap`, using `docs/Infra_prompts/Gamified Practices/*` as the spec reference.

**Key design call (specs vs plan §6):** the plan said "six games, each its own schema." The specs don't support that — **4 of 6** (Memory Matching, Jigsaw, Sentence Filling, TicTacToe) are literally titled *"Case-Based Preview Interaction Mode"* and share the EXACT CBP contract (3 MCQ checkpoints → DPE → correct/wrong simulation). So **6 phases, but only 3 schemas**: `RealLifeChallenge` + `ErrorDetection` (genuinely distinct) + one shared `CbpModeGame` (= `CaseBasedPreview` subclass + `interaction_mode` discriminator). User approved this fork explicitly ("separate phases / 3 schemas").

**Schemas** (`app/schemas/practice_games.py`, 22 unit tests): `RealLifeChallenge` (concept_ids≥1, role/task/context/prediction required, 2-4 `decisions` each MC+why+confidence, per-decision `correct_option`-in-range validator); `ErrorDetection` (pattern Literal math_equation/grammar_sentence/science_diagram, blocks≥3 with **exactly-one-`is_error`** validator, typed correction + accepted_variants + hint, **why_prompt mandatory for math/science** validator); `CbpModeGame` (subclass CaseBasedPreview + `interaction_mode` Literal memory_match/jigsaw/sentence_fill/tictactoe).

**Phases/wiring:** 6 phases `practice-rlc · practice-error-detection · practice-memory-match · practice-tictactoe · practice-jigsaw · practice-sentence`. Registered in `STRUCTURED_PHASE_SCHEMAS` + `_SVG_PHASES` (agent.py). `flows.SUBJECT_FLOWS` rewritten — **curated 2-3 game arc per subject** (NOT all six everywhere — New_Flow forbids "random disconnected games / tasks must match target skill"), sitting between learning sections and Boss Arena; easy = lighter 1-game no-boss, hard = full arc→boss. `game-breaks`/`real-life`/`consolidation` removed from every flow + from `PHASE_DEPS`. New `PHASE_DEPS` make each game depend on the learning sections. Per-subject roster: biology=rlc/error/memory-match; physics=rlc/error/ttt; kimyo=rlc/error/ttt; math-algebra=error/ttt/jigsaw; geometriya=error/jigsaw/ttt; history=rlc/jigsaw/memory-match; english=sentence/error/memory-match.

**Persistence:** 6 JSONB columns on `homework_jobs` (`practice_rlc_json`, `practice_error_detection_json`, `practice_memory_match_json`, `practice_tictactoe_json`, `practice_jigsaw_json`, `practice_sentence_json`) + 6 setters (jobs.py) + 6 JobOut fields + `_JSON_COLUMN_SETTERS` entries + 3 synth-render branches in `pipeline._synth_md_for_structured` (rlc / error-detection / shared CBP-mode via `_CBP_MODE_PHASES`) + download-zip entries (api/v1/jobs.py). Alembic **0015** (`b6d2f8a4c3e9` ← `a1c7e9d3b5f8`) adds all 6 columns. Chain now …0014 learning → 0015 practice_arc; **next free = 0016**.

**Prompts:** 21 per-subject prompt files (`prompts/<subject>/practice-*.md`), authored by 6 fanned-out subagents (one per game type), each grounded in its Gamified-Practices spec, literals verified (interaction_mode + error pattern), no schema drift (`concept_tag`/`diagram_svg` from spec samples correctly NOT used — schema uses `concept_ids`, SVG inline in block content). `test_prompt_coverage.py` enforces the fail-fast rule (every flow phase has a prompt).

## [0012] Live end-to-end smoke on a REAL textbook + provider/env findings + fixes — 2026-05-30 (Nggaev-v2)
**Context:** User drove a real run (uploaded an 8th-grade Uzbek geometry textbook via Swagger) + I ran a controlled full pipeline. Surfaced several real-world constraints and required several fixes. Commits this session (all pushed to `Nggaev-v2`): `364accd` config, `46ce1c0` gitignore+opencode-positional, `155ac93` opencode-stdin-revert, `255993f` gemini -p fix. Tip ~`255993f`.

**FIXES committed:**
1. **`gemini_api_key` made optional** (`364accd`) — was a REQUIRED Settings field but **nothing reads it** (no SDK; CLIs use own login). It forced a phantom key into every `.env` and made it look like the app calls an LLM API. Now optional → **only `DATABASE_URL` is required**. (User confusion: "why is API still used?" → answered: FastAPI HTTP server ≠ LLM API; no SDK anywhere.)
2. **`.gitignore` inline-comment bug** (`46ce1c0`) — lines `var/ # ...`, `*.log # ...`, `.playwright-cli/ # ...`, `ruvector.db # ...` had **trailing inline comments, which .gitignore does NOT support** → those patterns silently never matched. Result: a `git add -A` staged runtime PDFs incl. the **4 MB copyrighted textbook**. Fixed (comments on own lines), `git rm --cached -r var/`, amended before push so **PDFs never reached origin**. LESSON: never put inline comments in .gitignore.
3. **opencode prompt routing** — docs said `opencode run` takes a POSITIONAL prompt, so I added `Provider.prompt_on_stdin`+positional routing (`46ce1c0`). **It broke on Windows**: TOC prompts embed ~60k chars of page text → exceed the ~32k command-line limit (`WinError 206`). Live test proved opencode ALSO reads stdin (`"msg" | opencode run --format json` works), so **reverted to stdin** (`155ac93`). opencode now uses stdin like all providers. W1 stdin-vs-positional risk RESOLVED → stdin.
4. **gemini headless flag** (`255993f`) — user's gemini-cli is a NEWER version that **rejects empty `-p ""`** ("Not enough arguments following: p"). Our provider used `-p ""` + prompt-on-stdin. Fixed to `-p "."` (non-empty no-op trigger; verified stdin remains the effective prompt — a stdin math question answered "56"). Regression test added.

**PROVIDER REALITY (the big findings — extraction of REAL copyrighted textbooks):**
- **claude → REFUSES.** `claude CLI exited rc=1 :: API Error ... violate our Usage Policy`. claude Code's copyright/IP filter blocks reading/reproducing a published textbook. (Synthetic lessons are fine — only real copyrighted books trip it.) So claude is fine for GENERATING derived content, NOT for extracting textbooks.
- **opencode/deepseek-v4-flash-free → runs but TOO WEAK.** No refusal, stdin works, no crash — but returned **0 TOC entries** (42s) on the real book. Free zen tier also requires **billing details** at opencode.ai/auth (sign in → add card → API key → TUI `/connect` → OpenCode Zen). Model IDs confirmed: deepseek-v4-flash-free / big-pickle / mimo-v2.5-free / nemotron-3-super-free.
- **gemini → the right extractor.** Free with personal Google login (no card). Headless works + reads PDFs natively + no copyright refusal. Caveat: this gemini-cli version **crashes in its interactive TUI** (`ProjectRegistry.getShortId` "Cannot read properties of undefined") — but **headless (`-o json -p .`) is unaffected**. Extracted 5527-char lesson + 30-concept source map from the real book pages.
- **`settings.extract_provider` governs BOTH toc.extract AND lesson.extract** (toc_extractor.py + pipeline). So for real textbooks: `EXTRACT_PROVIDER=gemini` (extract), job `provider=claude` (content). Set per-run via `$env:` (overrides .env; doesn't modify it).

**APP LIMITATION found:** **TOC extraction only reads the first ~10 pages of text** (`_extract_toc_source_text` caps at `_TOC_TEXT_MAX_CHARS` ≈ 60k chars; when local text succeeds it CLEARS the PDF attachment — `extract_toc` lines ~1009-1018 — so even gemini only sees those pages). Both opencode AND gemini returned **0 entries** on this book → its "Mundarija" (contents) isn't in the first 10 pages (common in Uzbek textbooks: TOC at the back). **Not a model issue.** FOLLOW-UP candidate: widen/scan more pages, or also read tail pages, for TOC. Workaround used: seed a section manually.

**Live smoke (book a4fa550f, job c6457a80, `real_book_smoke.py` throwaway):** gemini TOC=0 → fell back to seeded section (pp 8-16, "Kesma va burchaklar"). pipeline.run direct (status=running+claimed so the user's worker can't race). extract(gemini, 5527 chars) ✓ → source-map 30 concepts ✓ → classify=HARD ✓ → content phases on claude (CBP/flashcards/memory-check/error-detection/jigsaw/tictactoe/boss/reflection) **[running at time of writing]**. Export to user's Downloads via `export_homework.py` (md + zip) on completion. **UPDATE WITH FINAL RESULT.**

**DB:** docker `edu-postgres` (postgres:16-alpine, :5433, edu/edu_homework) created + `alembic upgrade head` applied LIVE (see [[0011]]). Up 15h+. Throwaway scripts (real_book_smoke/export_homework/full_smoke) are gitignored under repo root? NO — at repo root, must `rm` before any `git add -A` (they're untracked; don't commit). reportlab added to venv via `uv pip` (not pyproject; dev-only).

**Server-run cheatsheet:** `$env:EXTRACT_PROVIDER='gemini'; $env:EXTRACT_MODEL='gemini-2.5-flash'; $env:ENABLE_DOCS='true'; .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m uvicorn main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000` then browse **http://localhost:8000/docs** (NOT 0.0.0.0; root `/` 404s — no web/dist built). uv NOT on user's PATH → use `.venv\Scripts\python.exe` directly. Flow: POST /books (file+subject) → poll GET /books/{id} for toc → POST /books/{id}/sections/{toc}/generate {provider:claude} → poll GET /jobs/{id} → GET /jobs/{id}/download. Upload **dedupes by content hash** → to re-extract, DELETE the book first. See [[0011]] [[0010]].

## [0013] Flashcards 32k-SVG failure + deep per-phase plan verification + teacher-note labeling + CBP slot reconciliation + HOW_IT_WORKS.md — 2026-05-30 (Nggaev-v2)
**Context:** Continued the [[0012]] live real-textbook run (geometry, job `c6457a80`, seeded pp8-16, extract=gemini / content=claude). A concurrent claude session was integrating the frontend (`web/`), so all work this session was kept **backend/content/docs only** and **nothing was committed** (no `git add`) to avoid colliding. Suite went 201 → 203 green.

**THE FAILURE — flashcards blew the claude CLI 32k output-token ceiling.** First full run failed: `flashcards: claude CLI exited rc=1 :: API Error: Claude's response exceeded the 32000 output token maximum`, after a **29-minute** single call. Root cause: `flashcards` was wrongly in `agent._SVG_PHASES` (so the ~370-line VISUAL/SVG RULES block was injected) AND `prompts/geometriya-g7-11/flashcards.md` had a self-contradictory "must be actual SVG code" line (its own examples use bracket `[Diagram: ...]` notation). Claude generated a full inline `<svg>` per card → >32k tokens. This is a **deviation from the plan** — §3/§5 say flashcards is "keep, add stable IDs", a "simple reference tool"; `FlashcardsPack` has **no SVG field**.

**FIX (plan-faithful, TDD):**
1. Removed `"flashcards"` from `_SVG_PHASES` (`agent.py`) + explanatory comment. New test `tests/services/test_flashcards_no_svg.py` (RED→GREEN): flashcards prompt must not contain the SVG-rules block; a real SVG phase (case-based-preview) still must.
2. Rewrote the contradictory line in `prompts/geometriya-g7-11/flashcards.md` → bracket `[Diagram: ...]`, "do NOT emit raw inline <svg>".
3. **Propagated** to the two other subjects the audit caught with the same bug class: `prompts/biology/flashcards.md` (had a whole "## SVG guidance" section + "include an SVG") and `prompts/english/flashcards.md` ("inline SVG only where it aids recall") → both converted to bracket notation. (history/kimyo/physics flashcards were already clean.)
**Live-validated:** after the fix, flashcards generated a clean 3,726-char deck, **0 `<svg>`** in the rendered output.

**DEEP PER-PHASE VERIFICATION (6 parallel read-only audit subagents vs plan §4-§10).** Verdict: largely on-track and plan-faithful. ✅ source-map/extract (5/5: SourceMap id/label/statement/kind, digest threaded into every content phase, extract pinned + cross-job reuse, `## Source Map` rendered), ✅ practice arc (6 games / 3 schemas, curated per-subject arcs never all-six, source-concept tracing), ✅ boss-arena (genuine Why→How→What, no options/correct_index), ✅ reflection (last in every flow; no consolidation anywhere), ✅ classify, ✅ assembly order, ✅ fail-fast (`get_prompt` raises on missing). Gaps found: (a) the flashcards-SVG bug in biology+english [fixed above]; (b) CBP "slot 7" prose inconsistency [fixed below]; (c) teacher-note labeling not implemented [fixed below]; (d) minor: DPE "no one-word / min 60 chars" is a generator hint, not validated — left as-is (core non-MCQ invariant IS enforced via `options: None`).

**TEACHER-NOTE LABELING (plan §8 content convention, user-approved, TDD).** Added `_TEACHER_MARK = "🔑 TEACHER NOTE:"` + `_teacher()` helper in `pipeline.py`. Routed every answer-revealing element through it across all 8 answer-emitting synth branches (memory-check, memory-sprint, final-challenge, boss-arena why/how/what, reading, case-based-preview correct/wrong path, practice-rlc correct action, practice-error-detection flawed-block+correction, CBP-mode correct/wrong path). Student-visible lines now render clean lettered options (a/b/c) with NO `✓` flag; the answer/explanation/model-answer moves to a machine-detectable teacher line. New test `tests/services/test_teacher_note_labeling.py` (the answer must be absent from student lines, present in teacher lines); updated 2 existing `test_practice_arc_synth.py` tests that pinned the old leaky `← broken`/`✓` render. Verified live: final packet has **40 TEACHER NOTE markers**.

**CBP SLOT-7 RECONCILIATION (user accepted my recommendation: keep the collapsed model).** Decision rationale: the literal plan §5 10-slot model with `learning_block_1/2` would push *teaching the method before the student commits*, contradicting §5's own principles ("method stays unnamed, student commits first; reasoning lives only in the DPE"). The collapsed 3-checkpoint + DPE schema is MORE faithful to those principles, and the one load-bearing invariant (DPE required, no options, before consequence) IS schema-enforced. So: kept the model, only fixed the inconsistent prose — the 6 CBP prompts (english/history/biology/physics/geometriya/kimyo) said "DPE in slot 7" while their own list numbered it item 5; reworded to "after Checkpoint 3, before the final simulation (canonical CBP slot 7)" (math-algebra already had this clarity). Dropped the bare "slot 7" labels from the `pipeline.py` CBP synth render too.

**HOW_IT_WORKS.md** — created at repo root: a plain-English, new-coder walkthrough of the whole system (CLI-not-SDK core idea, end-to-end flow diagram, data model, queue/worker, pipeline 3 stages, subjects/flows/phases, provider router, web API + SSE, frontend, usage tracking, PDF gotchas, run cheatsheet, don'ts, where-to-look table). Grounded by reading the actual source, not memory.

**FINAL DELIVERY:** Re-ran the job IN PLACE (same id `c6457a80`) twice via `rerun_job.py` — first to validate the SVG fix (done, 39,230 chars, but rendered by the pre-teacher-note code since the process loaded old code at startup), then a FRESH process to pick up teacher-note + CBP changes. Final packet: **done, 41,717 chars, 10 phases, 40 teacher notes, flashcards 0 SVG** (the 1 `<svg>` is in practice-error-detection — a legitimate SVG phase, small). Exported to `C:\Users\Recruiter\Downloads\homework-c6457a80-c4d7-44ce-a079-1395323752b6.{md,zip}` (zip = homework.md + 8 structured JSONs).

## [0014] TOC extraction fix (glyph-decode + front/tail) + plan-conformance hardening — 2026-05-30 (Nggaev-v2)
**Context:** Same session as [[0013]]. User had me (a) fix TOC extraction (was returning 0 entries on the real geometry book), then (b) verify an external "Plan Conformance Audit" and implement its genuine findings. Suite 207 → **214 green**.

**TOC EXTRACTION FIX (agent.py + tests/services/test_toc_source_text.py, TDD).** Original symptom (from [[0012]]): real geometry book → 0 TOC entries; assumed "TOC at the back, only first ~10 pages read." Live diagnosis revealed the REAL root cause: the PDF uses a **glyph-subset font with no ToUnicode map**, so pypdf emits glyph names like `/G55/G6D/G75` on EVERY page — pure garbage, no readable text anywhere → model can't find a TOC. Three-part fix:
1. **Glyph decode** — `_decode_glyph_text()`: when `/G<hex>` tokens dominate a page (≥20), decode each as a cp1252 byte (`/G55/G6D/G75/G6D/G69/G79` → "Umumiy"). Guarded so normal text with an incidental `/G` is untouched.
2. **Front + tail scan** — `_extract_toc_source_text` now reads the first pages AND the last `_TOC_TAIL_PAGES`(=15) pages, reserving `_TOC_TAIL_MAX_CHARS`(=20k) of the 60k budget for the tail (the Mundarija was on p157 of 161). Extracted into a shared `_read_pdf_pages` helper.
3. **Reverse tail** — read the tail from the LAST page backward (then sort chunks ascending) so the very end of the book is captured first when budget is tight.
Plus **Part 2**: gemini (which reads PDFs natively) keeps the whole PDF attached alongside the text excerpt when under `_GEMINI_PDF_MAX_BYTES`(=20MB). **Live-validated on the real book: 0 → 14 → 81 TOC entries** (decode+front/tail → reverse-tail reaching the actual Mundarija on p157-158). 4 unit tests (back-of-book capture, glyph decode, normal-text-not-mangled, short-book no-dup).

**PLAN-CONFORMANCE AUDIT — integrity-checked an external audit, then fixed the real findings.** The audit cited "198 tests" → it ran on a pre-session snapshot, so ~40% of its 8 deviations were stale/wrong: **#3** ("plan §9 still says Reflection dropped") FALSE — plan §9 already says "kept (Corrected 2026-05-29)"; **#6** ("§6 says each its own schema") FALSE — plan §6 already says "Schemas — three, not six"; **#4** (§8 answer-key labeling) already shipped in [[0013]]; **#1**'s slot-7 tell-tale already reconciled in [[0013]]. Implemented the genuinely-accurate, necessary ones:
1. **#2 source-fidelity validation** (the substantive one) — `_unknown_concept_ids`/`_emitted_concept_ids` in pipeline.py (7 TDD tests): collects emitted ids (top-level `concept_ids`, `source_concept_ids`, nested boss `questions[].concept_ids`) and flags any NOT in the job's SourceMap. Threaded `source_map_ids` from run() into `_run_content_phases_parallel`; on an invented id it logs a §10 violation warning + publishes a `concept_fidelity_warning` event. **Non-fatal by design** (a single hallucinated id shouldn't discard a good 10-phase run) — detected+surfaced where before it was silently trusted. Source-map extraction left non-fatal (the extract itself is the grounding; the map is a derived enhancement).
2. **#5 RLC reverse-test variant (§6)** — added a required "reverse-test" final decision to all 4 subjects running practice-rlc (biology/history/kimyo/physics): same scenario, key numbers/evidence changed, method stays unnamed, student infers the governing concept (tests transfer).
3. **Docstring nit** — flow_v2.py SourceMap "mock contract / real builder is Phase 2" → updated to reflect PR-1 reality.
**Skipped (with reason):** #1 (deliberate collapsed-CBP decision, [[0013]]), #3/#6 (audit wrong), #4 (done), #7 (plan says "ideally", soft), #8 (conforms — Option B allowed).

**State:** `Nggaev-v2`. Suite **214 green**. This entry's work committed+pushed together (TOC fix + conformance fixes + worklog). Open follow-ups: TOC fix is general but image-only/OCR PDFs still rely on gemini's native read; #2 fidelity is detect-only (could escalate to hard-fail/retry if desired). See [[0013]] [[0012]].

## [0015] CBP Completeness — Learning Blocks added (Flow v2 reshape, workstream 1) — 2026-05-30 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** First workstream of the Flow v2 content reshape. Brainstormed → spec'd → planned → executed via subagent-driven development (fresh subagent per task; controller independently verified every task by reading the committed diff + re-running the suite). Spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-30-cbp-completeness-design.md · Plan: docs/superpowers/plans/2026-05-30-cbp-completeness.md.

**The gap fixed:** the CBP standard (§5) mandates two interleaved Learning Blocks (LB1 after Checkpoint 1, LB2 after Checkpoint 2 — short teaching moments). Our CBP omitted them (quiz → quiz → quiz, no teaching between). NOTE: this REVERSES the [[0013]] "keep collapsed model" call — that decision was made before reading the actual standard; the standard clearly requires the blocks. (mistake_provenance deferred to a later fidelity pass; not in this workstream.)

**Commits (all on Nggaev-v2):**
1. `0bfebc5` — schema: new `LearningBlock` model (explanation required; optional title/visual_svg/source_concept_id); `learning_block_1`+`learning_block_2` required on CaseBasedPreview (CbpModeGame inherits — the 4 CBP-mode game specs also mandate them); `CaseSimulation.why_wrong_fails` now required (was `=""`). +3 tests, 2 fixtures updated.
2. `e38f117` — `pipeline._emitted_concept_ids` now collects learning-block `source_concept_id`, so the source-fidelity check flags an invented one.
3. `42c879f` — synth render: new `_render_checkpoints_and_blocks` helper interleaves C1→LB1→C2→LB2→C3 in both the case-based-preview and CBP-mode branches.
4. `6a8a184` — 7 CBP subject prompts: structure renumbered to insert LB1/LB2, "## Learning Blocks" section + "## Final simulation rules" (why_wrong_fails REQUIRED).
5. `8734e2b` — 11 CBP-mode game prompts (memory-match/jigsaw/sentence/tictactoe across subjects): same Learning Blocks + why_wrong_fails instruction.

**Tests:** 219 green throughout (each task verified independently — diff + full suite).

**Live smoke (real claude CBP on geometry, throwaway verify_cbp.py, now removed):** SUCCESS attempt=1, no 32k crash. Both learning blocks + why_wrong_fails populated with real Uzbek geometry content; 3 checkpoints; `visual_svg` empty on both (text-first prompt guidance worked → no SVG bloat). **⚠ WATCH-ITEM:** the call reported **46,251 output tokens and took ~12.4 min** — CBP is the heaviest phase and got heavier. It did NOT trip the response ceiling (likely the 46k includes thinking tokens; the visible JSON response stayed under the cap, unlike the SVG-laden flashcards that failed in [[0013]]). If CBP ever starts failing on output size, the lever is the text-first/no-SVG guidance in the LB + case prompts, or raising the claude output cap.

**Remaining Flow v2 content workstreams (each its own spec→plan→build):** flashcards 8-field active-recall shape; memory-check WHY apparatus + distractor tagging + FITB normalization; practice-game interaction payloads (pairs/grid/pieces/chips); Uzbek language contract. See [[0013]] [[0014]] and the gap analysis. Frontend (web/) is old-flow and out of scope here (separate student app).

## [0016] Flashcards Completeness — 8-field active-recall card (Flow v2 reshape, WS2) — 2026-05-30 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Workstream 2 of the Flow v2 content reshape. Brainstormed → spec'd → planned → subagent-driven execution with per-task independent verification (controller read every diff + re-ran the suite). Spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-30-flashcards-completeness-design.md · Plan: docs/superpowers/plans/2026-05-30-flashcards-completeness.md. Scope: the card DECK only (the 5 runtime study games are student-app/Memory-Check territory — out of scope).

**The gap fixed:** flashcards were a 3-field "reference tool" (id/front/back/hint + non-spec cluster). Spec requires an 8-field active-recall card. Now: `front, back` (required, min_length≥1), `type` + `difficulty` (required closed enums), `hint, explanation, example, misconception` (optional, prompt-encouraged), `cluster` kept. Word limits + count stay prompt-governed (brittle as schema rules).

**Commits (Nggaev-v2):**
1. `d6ea8e1` — schema: `FlashcardType` (10-value) + `FlashcardDifficulty` enums; type/difficulty required; explanation/example/misconception optional; front/back min_length. +4 tests, 4 fixtures fixed.
2. `d95983f` — synth: flashcards branch renders `type·difficulty` tag + explanation/example/misconception (no teacher-note wrap — both card sides are student-facing).
3. `4878801` — rewrote 7 subject flashcards prompts to the 8-field card (per-subject `type` subset + word limits).
4. `9a1a061` — **fix:** the WS3 subagent left contradictory legacy rules in 6 of 7 prompts ("NO explanations" / "Back = … Nothing else"; biology listed `formula` despite "NO formulas"). Controller verification caught it; reconciled all — removed the contradictions, dropped `formula` from biology's type list. (history was already clean.)

**Tests:** 224 green.

**Live smoke (real claude flashcards on geometry, throwaway removed):** SUCCESS attempt=1. 12 cards, **every card has type+difficulty**, **no raw `<svg>`**, all 12 have `explanation` + 7 have `misconception`; valid geometry types (definition/formula/image_label/term_to_meaning); real Uzbek content. Lightweight: 13,781 output tokens / ~3.5 min (vs CBP's ~46k/~12min — flashcards are small text cards, no token concern).

**Remaining Flow v2 content workstreams (in order):** 3) Memory Check (WHY apparatus + distractor tagging + FITB normalization + 4-option lock); 4) Practice-game interaction payloads (pairs/grid/pieces/chips); 5) Uzbek language contract. See [[0015]] [[0013]] [[0014]].

## [0017] Memory Check Completeness — per-type item model (Flow v2 reshape, WS3) — 2026-05-30 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Workstream 3 of the Flow v2 content reshape. Brainstorm → spec → plan → subagent-driven execution with per-task independent verification (controller read every diff + re-ran suite). Spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-30-memory-check-completeness-design.md · Plan: docs/superpowers/plans/2026-05-30-memory-check-completeness.md. Scope: content only (items + answers + reasons; runtime scoring/retry/adaptation = student app).

**The gap fixed:** Memory Check items were a flat `options: list[str]` + `correct_index`. Spec wants per-type structure. Now: `MemoryCheckOption {text, is_correct, reason}` + `MemoryCheckBlank {answer, accepted_variations}`; enriched `MemoryCheckItem` (options for MCQ/CCE, blanks for fill_blank, + why_prompt/expected_reasoning_keywords/correct_feedback/wrong_feedback/explanation); `correct_index` removed. Kind-keyed `model_validator`: MCQ/CCE → exactly 4 options + exactly 1 correct + no blanks; fill_blank → ≥1 blank + no options. pass_threshold stays locked 0.60 (our gate metadata).

**Commits (Nggaev-v2):**
1. `0a638b6` — schema (option/blank models + validator) + synth rewrite (clean lettered options for student, 🔑 TEACHER NOTE for correct + distractor reasons + blank answers; why_prompt student-facing) + updated `_item` fixture + teacher-note fixture + 4 new validator tests. Schema+synth landed together (teacher-note test couples them).
2. `bc3a5c1` — rewrote 7 memory-check prompts to option-objects + blanks + WHY apparatus (required for science subjects). Subagent proactively removed the contradictory old `correct_index`/string-options leftovers (the flashcards-round lesson carried over) — verification confirmed zero leftovers.

**Tests:** 228 green.

**Live smoke (real claude memory-check on geometry, throwaway removed):** SUCCESS attempt=1. 10 items (4 MCQ / 3 fill_blank / 3 CCE), **all satisfy their kind-shape** (4-option/one-correct or blanks-only — validator accepted), every distractor has a real-misconception `reason`, pass_threshold 0.60. Light: 12,287 output tokens / ~3 min. why_prompt=0 (correct — geometry is non-science, where it's optional).

**Remaining Flow v2 content workstreams (in order):** 4) Practice-game interaction payloads (pairs/grid/pieces/chips for the 4 CBP-mode games — the structured data the games need beyond the shared CBP skeleton); 5) Uzbek language contract. See [[0016]] [[0015]].

## [0018] Practice-Game Payloads (Flow v2 reshape, WS4) — code complete + GREEN, but live gen exposed a heaviness blocker — 2026-05-30 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Workstream 4. Added a required, typed `interaction_payload` to `CbpModeGame` so the 4 CBP-mode games are renderable. Brainstorm → spec → plan → subagent-driven (each task diff+suite verified). Spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-30-practice-game-payloads-design.md · Plan: docs/superpowers/plans/2026-05-30-practice-game-payloads.md.

**Code shipped + verified (228→233 green):**
1. `96de5f5` — schema: `GameChoice` + `MemoryMatchPair/Payload` (4-8 pairs), `JigsawPiece/Payload` (3-6 pieces + 1-3 assembly types), `SentenceFillPayload` (sentence + ≥3 chips, exactly 1 correct), `TicTacToePayload` (exactly 9 cells, ≥1 correct). `CbpModeGame.interaction_payload: Union[...]` + `_payload_matches_mode` validator (smart-union + isinstance guard). Synth `_CBP_MODE_PHASES` renders each payload (pairs/pieces/sentence+chips/3x3 grid; chip+cell correctness teacher-noted). `_cbp_mode` fixture builds a per-mode payload via `_PAYLOAD_FOR` (guarded setdefault so the unknown-mode rejection test still raises). +5 tests.
2. `abc88c4` — 11 CBP-mode game prompts instruct their payload (pairs/pieces+types/sentence+chips/9-cell grid). All verified present with correct keys.

**⚠ BLOCKER found in live smoke (the WS4 risk materialized):** a real claude `practice-jigsaw` generation (geometry, throwaway verify_game.py) ran **>21.5 min on a SINGLE call** (not a retry) and was KILLED — conclusively too heavy to be practical. Root cause is ARCHITECTURAL and predates the payload: a `CbpModeGame` inherits the ENTIRE Case-Based Preview shell (case_setup + 3 checkpoints + DPE + final_simulation + feedback + completion) PLUS the 2 required learning blocks (WS1) — that shell is the bulk of the weight; the payload itself is small. The spec calls these "CBP interaction modes" (shared skeleton), but generating the full shell PER GAME is impractical (~20+ min each; a HARD homework has 2-3 of them).

**FINDING / REQUIRED FOLLOW-UP (no longer optional):** design a LIGHTER schema for the CBP-mode games — they likely need only a compact prompt/scenario + the interaction_payload (+ maybe one checkpoint + DPE), NOT the full CBP shell. This is a new mini-workstream (its own spec/plan). The current payload schema + prompts are correct and stay; the lightening is a separate change to `CbpModeGame`'s inherited shape. Per-homework latency (~25-35 min for HARD, driven by CBP ~12min + the heavy games) is the motivation — see the latency analysis.

**Status:** WS4 schema/synth/prompts = DONE + green; live game generation NOT yet usable pending the lighter-skeleton follow-up. Remaining Flow v2: WS5 (Uzbek language contract) + the game-skeleton lightening follow-up. See [[0017]] [[0015]].

## [0019] General Prompts MVP (Path A) — subject-parameterized prompts for ALL subjects + single GENERAL_FLOW + classify removed — 2026-06-01 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Path A MVP. One set of general, subject-parameterized prompts now serves every subject (no more 7 per-subject prompt copies in the live path), the flow is a single linear sequence (no easy/hard, no classify), and the classify phase is gone from the generation path. Spec: docs/nets_general_prompts_mvp_design.md · Plan: docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-01-general-prompts-mvp.md.

**Shipped:**
- **General prompts** — `prompts/_general/` (7 files), one per live phase, written subject-neutral with a `{{SUBJECT}}` token.
- **Resolver** (`app/services/prompts.py`) — `get_prompt(subject, phase_name)` loads from `_general` by default and substitutes `{{SUBJECT}}` from `SUBJECT_LABELS` (e.g. `physics`→"Physics (Fizika)"). A `USE_SUBJECT_PROMPTS=False` switch (off for MVP) is the future override hook: when True it prefers `prompts/<subject>/<phase>.md` if that file exists, else falls back to `_general`. `get_prompt_hash` follows the same resolution for provenance.
- **Single flow** (`app/services/flows.py`) — one `GENERAL_FLOW` + `flow_for()`; no easy/hard split, no classify step.
- **Classify removed** — dropped from `STRUCTURED_PHASE_SCHEMAS` (agent.py) and from `pipeline.run()`; difficulty is now pinned `None` end-to-end (run() docstring de-staled to match).

**Commits (Nggaev-v2):** `daa9008` (general `_general` prompts — Path A), `24a2b67` (general CBP language directive + prompt polish), `fe1bc6d` (`_general` resolver + `{{SUBJECT}}` substitution), `8a4a8a1` (single `GENERAL_FLOW`, drop easy/hard), `5a3660b` (drop classify from schema map), `b1d758c` (classify-free `run()`, difficulty pinned None), `6738336` (de-stale run() docstring + pinned-difficulty note).

**Verification:** full suite **222 green** (`uv run python -m pytest tests/ -q`). Real claude-CLI smoke **PASS**: built `get_prompt("physics","boss-arena")` (asserted `{{SUBJECT}}` gone + "Physics" present), fed a hand-written Uzbek physics lesson_context (Nyutonning ikkinchi qonuni, F=ma) + a 3-concept source-map digest, ran the FAST boss-arena phase through the real `claude` CLI and `model_validate`'d the `BossArena` result → **5 questions, each non-empty why/how/what, concept_ids≥1** (out_tokens≈15.3k, ~4.5min). Throwaway `smoke_general.py` deleted (not committed).

**DEAD-BUT-UNTOUCHED:** the per-subject prompt dirs (`prompts/<subject>/`) and each subject's `flow.md` / `instruction.md` / `classify.md` are now unused by the live path but were INTENTIONALLY LEFT ON DISK — they become the override layer the moment `USE_SUBJECT_PROMPTS` is flipped True.

**Next workstream — Path B:** lighten `CbpModeGame` off the full `CaseBasedPreview` shell (the [[0018]] heaviness blocker — full CBP shell per game = ~20+min/call), then re-add a subject-matched game via a `SUBJECT_GAME` map + author the 4 CBP-mode general prompts. See [[0018]].

## [0020] English Target-Language Handling — `{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}` token: English content + Uzbek scaffolding, CEFR-leveled — 2026-06-01 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Fixed a live defect where `english` lessons generated Uzbek prose *about* English. Language is now resolved at prompt-build time per subject: `english` → English target content with Uzbek scaffolding; every other subject → formal Uzbek (unchanged). Spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-english-target-language-design.md · Plan: docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-01-english-target-language.md.

**Shipped:**
- **Substitution-time mechanism** (`app/services/prompts.py`) — `get_prompt` now substitutes a `{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}` token in addition to `{{SUBJECT}}`. A `LANGUAGE_RULES` dict maps `english`→an English-target block and `_default`→the formal-Uzbek block (`"Siz"`, never `"sen"`, verbatim formulas/numbers/units). No language *detection*; the subject key selects the block.
- **English-target / UZ-bridge model** — grounded in the (inert) `prompts/english/*`: the thing being LEARNED is English (target vocabulary, example sentences, passages, grammar items, anything the learner reads/produces); everything that HELPS them learn it is formal Uzbek (`"Siz"`) — task instructions, framing, hints, explanations, feedback, DPE/reasoning prompts.
- **NEW grade→CEFR ladder** — `G5→A1, G6→A1+, G7→A2, G8→A2+, G9→B1, G10→B1+, G11→B2`; if the source names no grade, infer from source complexity, default floor A2 when indeterminate. CEFR controls sentence length / tenses / vocabulary range and must never be exceeded. **This ladder is an assumption** (validated by the smoke below) — the prior classify-based CEFR detection is GONE (classify removed in [[0019]]).
- **7 `_general` prompts** now carry the `{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}` token (replacing the old single hard-coded Uzbek block); `flashcards.md` additionally has an English-conditional front/back hint (front = English target item, back = Uzbek bridge).

**Commits (Nggaev-v2):** `0226233` (`{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}` resolver block — English-target vs Uzbek), `f54fd8a` (test: lock English-block CEFR/principle text + CEFR default floor), `d893538` (7 general prompts use the token; English-target for `english`), `909a73d` (hoist test import; fix flashcards hint wording).

**Verification:** full suite **226 green** (`uv run python -m pytest tests/ -q`). Real claude-CLI smoke **PASS** on TWO structurally-different english phases (throwaway `smoke_english.py`, deleted, not committed): built `get_prompt("english", phase)` for `flashcards` + `memory-check` (asserted `English (L2)` present, no `{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}`/`{{SUBJECT}}` leftover), fed a "Grade 7 English, Unit 4 — Daily routines" A2 lesson_context, ran each through the real `claude` CLI and `model_validate`'d (`FlashcardsPack` / `MemoryCheckPack`) → both clean. **(a) Split correct:** flashcards front = English target (`get up`, `brush (one's) teeth`, `eat breakfast`, `go to school by bus`), back/explanation = Uzbek bridge; memory-check English items/options present with prompt + distractor reasons in Uzbek. **(b) CEFR correct:** reads ~A2/Grade 7 (simple present, common daily-routine vocab) — not B2.

**Caveat (deferred):** the inert `prompts/english/*` files still reference the deleted `classify.md` for CEFR detection (dead with `USE_SUBJECT_PROMPTS=False`). Fix that reference when/if the subject-override layer is revived. Doc `docs/nets_general_prompts_mvp_design.md` §3.3 updated: the "English terms acceptable as-is" bullet replaced with the English-target-now-exists note; "English-scaffolding branch" dropped from the deferred list.

## [0021] CBP-Mode Game Lightening (Path B) — compact `CbpModeGame` (payload + `why_prompt`) clears the [[0018]] 21-min blocker — 2026-06-01 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Resolved the [[0018]] blocker. The four CBP-mode games (memory-match / tictactoe / jigsaw / sentence) no longer inherit the full `CaseBasedPreview` shell — `CbpModeGame` is now a compact standalone schema, and one subject-matched game is back in the flow. Real-CLI generation dropped from **>21 min (killed)** to **71 s**. Spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-cbp-mode-game-lightening-design.md · Plan: docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-01-cbp-mode-game-lightening.md. Executed subagent-driven (fresh subagent per task; controller stress-tested every commit firsthand — diff read + tests re-run, not report-trust).

**Shipped:**
- **Compact schema** (`app/schemas/practice_games.py`) — `CbpModeGame(BaseModel)` replaces `CbpModeGame(CaseBasedPreview)`: `title`, `source_concept_ids` (≥1), `interaction_mode`, `instruction`, typed `interaction_payload` (Union unchanged), a single light `why_prompt` (min_length=1), `expected_reasoning_keywords`. `_payload_matches_mode` validator kept. The full-CBP fields (`case_setup`/3 `checkpoints`/2 learning blocks/`decision_process_explanation`/`final_simulation`/`feedback_summary`) are GONE — that shell was the heaviness. Dead `from app.schemas.flow_v2 import CaseBasedPreview` import removed.
- **Reasoning step = `why_prompt`, NOT full DPE** — deliberate decision this session: one open "explain the concept · the link · the trap" prompt, not the multi-field Decision Process Explanation. Avoids re-introducing CBP weight + a duplicate-DPE-per-packet. (The committed spec still says "DPE"; the plan + this entry supersede it.)
- **Subject-matched game re-added** (`app/services/flows.py`) — `SUBJECT_GAME` map (biology/history→memory-match, physics/kimyo/math-algebra→tictactoe, geometriya→jigsaw, english→sentence); `flow_for` now returns an **8-phase** flow `[*_BASE_PHASES, SUBJECT_GAME[subject], boss-arena, reflection]`. Public `GENERAL_FLOW` removed (all 4 refs across app/+tests/ repointed to `flow_for`). 4 game `PHASE_DEPS` restored.
- **4 compact `_general` prompts** — `prompts/_general/practice-{memory-match,tictactoe,jigsaw,sentence}.md`, each `{{SUBJECT}}` + `{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}` (so `english`→`practice-sentence` emits English-target automatically), one valid JSON `## Output format` example, NO full-CBP scaffolding. All 4 examples were validated against the live schema by the controller.
- **Compact synth render** (`pipeline.py` `_CBP_MODE_PHASES` branch) — renders title + instruction + per-mode payload + `why_prompt`. **§8 correction to the plan:** the plan showed correct cells/chips inline with `✓`; that would leak answer keys into the student-facing bundle, violating the documented teacher/student split every other game honors. Fixed → correct tictactoe cells, correct sentence chip, distractor reasons, and concept ids are all wrapped in `_teacher()` (`🔑 TEACHER NOTE:`). memory-match pairs + jigsaw pieces stay student-visible (no boolean answer key; matches prior behavior). Verified leak-check `False` for all 4 modes.

**Commits (Nggaev-v2):** `a6b9ece` (lighten `CbpModeGame` off full CBP), `9343559` (4 compact game prompts), `f3a9ad9` (`SUBJECT_GAME` + 8-phase `flow_for`, drop `GENERAL_FLOW`), `00f97a0` (compact synth render, answers teacher-marked).

**Verification:** full suite **227 green** (`uv run python -m pytest tests/ -q`). Real claude-CLI **acceptance-gate smoke PASS** (throwaway `smoke_game.py`, deleted, not committed): built `get_prompt("physics","practice-tictactoe")` + a 3-concept Uzbek source-map digest (Nyuton II / F=ma), ran through the real `claude` CLI via `run_phase` and `model_validate`'d `CbpModeGame` → **schema-valid in 71.2 s** (vs the [[0018]] 21-min kill — the heaviness is gone), `interaction_mode=tictactoe`, 9 cells / 3 correct, `source_concept_ids` = exactly the 3 supplied ids (none invented → source fidelity holds), Uzbek title (`{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}` works), `why_prompt` 262 chars. No payload-only fallback needed.

**Deliberate deviation (documented):** the Gamified-Practices spec files define these four as full Case-Based Previews ("Case-Based Preview Interaction Mode"). Lightening them to a board + one reasoning prompt is an intentional, measured departure — the full shell made them unusable ([[0018]]). The standalone `case-based-preview` phase still delivers the full learning case in every flow.

**Concurrency note:** during this work a separate session left UNCOMMITTED edits in the tree (`app/services/prompts.py` + `tests/services/test_prompts_resolver.py` — refining the [[0020]] grade→CEFR ladder to be Uzbek-curriculum-grounded; `docs/memory/ROADMAP.md` untracked). Left untouched; this worklog commit stages only the two `docs/memory/` files.

## [0022] Backlog R2–R8 — backend fix pass — 2026-06-02 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Fixed all seven worked-up backend issues from [[ROADMAP]] (R2–R8) as one ordered pass — brainstorm (3 design decisions) → spec → plan → subagent-driven execution, controller stress-testing every commit firsthand (diff read + tests re-run). Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-roadmap-r2-r8-design.md` · Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-01-roadmap-r2-r8.md`. Suite **227 → 240 green** (+13 tests), backend-only, parallel-safe with the live `web/` frontend session.

**Shipped (one commit each):**
- **R2** `40dab51` — size-gate the extract subset PDF. New `_should_subset_for_extract`: only carve a page subset when the PDF exceeds `_GEMINI_PDF_MAX_BYTES`; else attach the full book (prompt names the printed range). Kills the printed-vs-physical page-offset slice bug for all sub-20 MB books. **Approach B** (per-book offset detection deferred — YAGNI).

## [0023] Notion Integration Phase 1 (PUSH) — built + live-verified — 2026-06-02 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Connected the generator to the existing Notion lesson tree in the PUSH direction — on job-done, find-or-create the lesson + `Homework` page under the right subject page and write the packet in. Brainstormed → spec → plan → subagent-driven (11 tasks, implementer + spec-review + code-quality-review each). Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-02-notion-archive-design.md` · Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-02-notion-archive-phase1-push.md`. Suite **245 → 265 green**. Branch `Nggaev-v2`, commits `e5a4c93..7dd8823` (+ spec/plan `cfa9800`), **pushed**. Build is dark behind `NOTION_ENABLED=false` by default.

**Locked decisions (folded into spec):** Phase 1 (push) only — Phase 2 (pull) separate later plan; anchor = config map `{subject}|{grade}` → Notion subject-page ID; body = rendered blocks **AND** attached `homework.md`+`content.json`; grade = new real `books.grade` field; lesson title = app-owned `"{section_number} {section_title}"`; idempotency = `notion_archived_at` job guard + `toc_entries.notion_homework_page_id` + skip-write-if-`page_has_content` (no deletion path in Phase 1).

**Shipped (one commit each):** `e5a4c93` deps+config (`notion-client`/`httpx`; `notion_enabled`/`notion_api_key`/`notion_subject_pages`) · `6303588` migration 0016/`c9e3f1a07b62` + 3 model columns · `0bb9e66` repo setters · `cc9a727` upload accepts grade · `9567a6b` pure `blocks.py` (markdown→Notion blocks) · `4244cb2` sync `client.py` (rate-limit + 2-step `/v1/file_uploads` upload, `Notion-Version: 2022-06-28`) · `755d6f7` `page_creator.find_or_create` · `8e16852` `job_artifacts` (shared `structured_artifacts`+`build_content_json`; download endpoint refactored to reuse) · `3d97025` `notion_archive.archive_job` (best-effort, anchor-resolve→push→stamp) · `16a6564` review-fix: split DB session around the push (don't hold a connection during Notion I/O) · `941ec62` pipeline hook after job-done (double-guarded non-fatal) · `7dd8823` smoke runbook.

**Architecture note:** the reference `notion_client.Client` + `httpx` path is **synchronous**; `archive_job` runs `_push_to_notion` via `asyncio.to_thread` so it never blocks the event loop. Ported (trimmed) from `s1gmamale1/Notion---Video-Lesson` `tools/notion/`.

**LIVE SMOKE PASSED (real Notion, user's `ntn_` integration token):** instead of an 8-min regen, ran `archive_job` directly on an existing done job `442cc7c4` (Geometriya-8 §1.1 "Kesma va burchaklar", `notion_archived_at` NULL, content present), `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES={"geometriya-g7-11|8":"2c4998381c7680a099fcfa8277758da9"}`. Full HTTP trace confirmed: created lesson page → `Homework` sub-page → uploaded `homework.md`+`content.json` (2-step) → appended content in 3 PATCH batches (>200 blocks, chunked at 100) → stamped DB. Independently re-read the page: correct ancestor path (`Homework ▸ 1.1… ▸ Geometriya ▸ 8-sinf ▸ …`), full content + both attachments. Homework page `37399838-1c76-81e8-a547-db74b8c1dbbc`. **All previously-deferred unknowns empirically cleared:** integration write capability works; `properties.title` accepted WITHOUT `"type":"text"`; 2-step file upload + `2022-06-28` works; 100-block chunking works. (Token verified read-access first via a verdict-only PowerShell check that never surfaced the token.)

**Known accepted limit (logged R9):** inline SVG (CBP `visual_svg`) and markdown tables render as **escaped literal text** in Notion blocks — `markdown_to_notion_blocks` only handles headings/bullets/dividers/bold-italic. Lossless `homework.md`/`content.json` attachments carry the real data. Fix (SVG→image, tables→table blocks) deferred to the prompt-reshaping pass.

**TOC investigation (separate finding, logged R10):** user noticed Geometriya gave **1** TOC section vs English **22** / Algebra full. Verified cause: the Geometriya 2019 "elektron darslik" PDF has subsetted fonts with **no `ToUnicode` CMap** → `pypdf` extracts glyph-ID garbage (`/G55/G6D…`, 82k chars). The extractor's gate `has_local_toc_text = bool(...)` checks non-empty, not readable, so the garbage is injected as "the TOC text — use this" and the prompt biases toward `{}` when unreadable. NOT size (3.8MB < English 15MB), NOT scanned (has a text layer), NOT the >20MB limit. Clean-Unicode PDFs (English/Algebra) extract fully through the same path → isolated to that file's PDF quality. Deliverable (low-priority, gated on recurrence, UNPROVEN): detect garbage → fall back to gemini native attached-PDF read.

**Also this session:** re-verified the whole WISHLIST against current code (none stale) and reorganized `WISHLIST.md` Open into **Backend / Frontend** sections.

**Deferred follow-ons:** frontend **grade input** on the upload form (UI sends no grade today → set `books.grade` via SQL); Notion **Phase 2 PULL** (fetch textbook from Notion to source generation) — its own future plan.
