## [0025] Content-gen default provider → claude/sonnet-4-6 — 2026-06-02 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Made **claude / claude-sonnet-4-6** the default content-generation provider+model in the operator UI (was gemini). Triggered by a Kimyo job accidentally run on `gemini-3.1-pro-preview`. Small/clear/verified → controller-direct per the workflow exception. Commit `18ff032`.

**Root cause of the wrong default (investigated):** `section.tsx:33` initialized `provider="gemini"`; the model-init effect (`section.tsx:52`) picks `manifest.providers[provider][0]`, and the **frontend never consulted the server default** (`_PROVIDER_DEFAULT_MODEL`). Also a latent inconsistency: `agent_models.MODEL_MANIFEST["claude"][0]` was `claude-opus-4-7` (so `default_model("claude")`=opus) while `_PROVIDER_DEFAULT_MODEL["claude"]` is `claude-sonnet-4-6` — manifest "recommended" disagreed with the resolver default.

**Fix (3 one-line edits):**
- `app/services/agent_models.py` — reorder claude list so `claude-sonnet-4-6` is first (opus second, haiku third). Now `[0]`=sonnet → the frontend auto-default is sonnet AND `default_model("claude")` matches the resolver. Opus still fully available/selectable.
- `web/src/routes/section.tsx:33` — picker default provider `gemini → claude`.
- `web/src/lib/api.ts` — `generate()` options fallback `provider = "gemini" → "claude"` (rarely hit; section always passes provider explicitly, but kept consistent).

**Extractor explicitly NOT touched:** the extract phase is pinned via `config.py` `settings.extract_provider` (`gemini`) / `settings.extract_model` (`gemini-2.5-flash`), independent of the manifest/picker. The user flagged this; confirmed `config.py` was not modified. Extract stays gemini.

**Verification:** `pytest tests/services/test_agent.py tests/services/test_opencode_provider.py` → 26 passed (the `_resolve_model("gemini"/"kimi"/"codex", None) is None` leak-guard, `_resolve_model("claude", None)==sonnet`, and opus-still-resolvable all intact). `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` → 0. `npm run build` → OK (dist rebuilt). The new default is live once the server restarts (serves rebuilt dist).

## [0026] Fix: section-list status badges vanishing — 2026-06-02 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** The "Pick a section" list badges (Ready/Running/Failed) showed on first load but **intermittently disappeared** after a hard refresh / navigate-away-and-back. Diagnosed with systematic-debugging (code trace, no repro-by-guess). Real bug, but **display-only** (the job status is correct in the DB and on the section page; only the list badges were unreliable). Commit `4c9fe63`.

**Root cause:** `web/src/routes/book.tsx` sets its `entries` state from **two** backend sources that returned **different shapes**:
- REST `GET /books/{id}` → `_book_out_with_toc` enriches each TOC entry with `latest_job_id/status` via `jobs_repo.latest_by_section` (had status ✅).
- SSE `GET /{book_id}/toc/stream` `toc_ready` **replay** (`books.py:126-129`) built entries as plain `TOCEntryOut.model_validate(e)` with **no enrichment** (status = null ❌).

On mount `entries=null` so the SSE is enabled; for an already-`toc_ready` book it emits `toc_ready` immediately. The SSE query is lighter/faster than the enriched REST query, so it raced in and `setEntries(status-less)` overwrote the enriched getBook data → badges wiped. The non-determinism (lighter query usually wins on a cold refresh) explains "show now, gone after refresh." The deeper cause = **duplicated enrichment logic that drifted** between the two endpoints.

**Fix (root cause, DRY):** extracted one shared `async _enriched_toc_entries(session, book) -> list[TOCEntryOut]` in `books.py`; **both** `_book_out_with_toc` (REST) and the SSE `toc_ready` replay now call it. The two can no longer diverge, and the race is harmless (both paths carry status). No frontend change needed.

**TDD + verification:** `tests/api/test_toc_status_enrichment.py` — fakes a book with 2 sections (one with a job, one without), mocks `jobs_repo.latest_by_section`, asserts the helper attaches `latest_job_status` to the section with a job and leaves the other null. Red (AttributeError) → green. Full suite **265 passed**, plus **1 PRE-EXISTING unrelated failure** `test_config_notion::test_notion_defaults_disabled` (local `.env` has `NOTION_ENABLED=true` and the test doesn't isolate via `_env_file=None` — code default is correctly False; it's the backlog NEXT #1, not from this change).

**Note:** backend-only → the running server must be **restarted** to serve the new `books.py` before the badges stop flickering.

**Bonus this turn:** the **Kimyo §3 Notion push was live-verified** (job `f8b1214c` `done`, `notion_archived_at=22:27:46`, no error) — the auto-push now works for a **2nd subject** end-to-end (after mapping `kimyo-g7-11|8` in `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES`).

## [0027] Notion subject-page map — full grade-8 + history split routing — 2026-06-03 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Filled `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES` for all of grade 8 (biology, english, geometriya-g7-11, history, kimyo-g7-11, math-algebra, physics) and taught the resolver to route the **single app `history` subject** to the **two** Notion history pages (Jahon tarixi / O‘zbekiston tarixi) without splitting the subject or its prompt. Controller-direct (design locked with user, small + clear → CLAUDE.md small-fix exception), TDD.

**Design (chosen with user, Option A refined):** the map value is now `str | {keyword: page-id}`.
- string → used directly (every normal subject, **and** grades that have one combined history page).
- object → the book filename is folded and substring-matched against each keyword; first hit wins; no hit → None so `archive_job` logs the existing "no subject-page mapping … skipping" and never mis-files. This keeps split-vs-combined a pure per-grade **config** decision (no code branch on grade).

Rejected: (B) two map keys + per-book variant flag — needs upload/schema/UI changes; (C) conflate both into one page — throws away the separation Notion deliberately keeps.

**Code:**
- `app/services/notion_archive.py`: `_resolve_subject_page_id(mapping, subject, grade, hint="")` — string-passthrough / dict-keyword-match / None; new `_fold(s)` lowercases + strips `' ‘ ’ ʻ \`` so bare keyword `ozbekiston` matches `Ozbekiston` and `O‘zbekiston`. Call site passes `book.original_filename or ""`.
- `app/config.py`: `notion_subject_pages: dict[str, str | dict[str, str]]`.
- `.env` (gitignored): full grade-8 JSON; `history|8` = `{"jahon":…,"ozbekiston":…}`.

**Page IDs (grade 8):** biology `2c4998381c7680d494ddc5ce159d85ae` · english `2c4998381c7680188d03c61c47bb7109` (page is a WIP placeholder — confirmed right target) · geometriya-g7-11 `2c4998381c7680a099fcfa8277758da9` · history → jahon `2c4998381c76804bbfc7cde7d88f87ff` / ozbekiston `2c4998381c7680c6adc3ee31c9f3016f` · kimyo-g7-11 `2c4998381c7680a1bc94ee04f90d8492` · math-algebra `2c4998381c768030ade2de43088235c4` · physics `2c4998381c76806fbe6bc8e8fdcf1a4e`. Grade-8 parent "8 - sinf" = `2c8998381c768062b478ca09bf03c0b9` (under 8 Grade → Lessons → Class A Education).

**Verification:** 3 new resolver tests (dict-match incl. apostrophe variant, dict-no-match→None, string-ignores-hint) red→green; all 7 `test_notion_archive.py` pass. End-to-end against the real `.env` via `Settings`: union parses from env JSON (`history|8` loads as dict), both real history filenames route to the correct page, physics (string) resolves, unknown history book → None. The lone red `test_config_notion::test_notion_defaults_disabled` is the **pre-existing** backlog item (NEXT #1), not from this change.

**Caveats / next:** only grade 8 is mapped — other grades need their own `{N}-sinf` child IDs (and some lower grades likely have one **combined** history page → just a string value). Not committed (user runs their own test/commit). This is the hand-maintained-map approach; the auto-resolve-by-crawl idea remains the WISHLIST/Phase-2 item.

## [0028] Effort A — md-per-phase architecture flip — 2026-06-03 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Replaced the structured-JSON generation layer with "one markdown file per phase". Every content phase now produces free-text markdown stored on `phase_outputs.output_md` — no JSON, no Pydantic schema, no validation-by-schema, no combined `assembled_md` packet, no `content.json`. The per-phase `.md` files are the deliverable. Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-md-per-phase-generation-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-03-md-per-phase-architecture-flip.md` (11 tasks, subagent-driven, controller stress-tested every commit: read diff + re-run tests).

**Pieces:**
- **Validator** — new `app/services/phase_validator.py`: pure, deterministic, **warn-only** `validate(phase_name, md, *, subject)`. Starter common rules (non-empty body, top-level `#`, well-formed visuals: a markdown image must be http(s) or the `placeholder` sentinel). Per-phase rule registry `RULES` is empty (Effort B fills it). Warnings persist to `phase_outputs.validation_warnings` (new JSONB col, migration 0017) and log; never block.
- **Pipeline** (`_execute_phase`): removed the `STRUCTURED_PHASE_SCHEMAS` branch + `_synth_md_for_structured`; all non-extract phases take `run_phase_prompt` (free-text). Validator runs after each phase. Job-done no longer assembles. Source map dropped (`source_map_digest=""`).
- **Notion** (`notion_archive` + `notion/blocks`): `_push_to_notion` creates lesson → Homework → **one sub-page per phase** (`PHASE_TITLES`), each = rendered md blocks + attached `<phase>.md`, idempotent per sub-page. `blocks.markdown_to_notion_blocks` now renders a markdown image → external image (http) or **callout** (placeholder/non-http) — never a broken image block.
- **Download** (`jobs.py`): `_phase_zip` → zip of `<order:02d>-<phase>.md` for done non-extract phases. `?format=md` dropped.
- **Console** (`preview.tsx`): renders each `job.phases[].output_md` as markdown (reuses `MD_COMPONENTS`/`ReactMarkdown`/`rehypeRaw`) + per-phase warnings strip; `DonePanel` counts phases/warnings. Deleted `flow-v2-phases.tsx` + all `components/flow-v2/*`.
- **Prompts**: 10 `_general/*.md` converted JSON→markdown output + visual convention (inline `<svg>` for diagrams; `![placeholder: … — image gen required](placeholder)` for raster). `reflection.md` left untouched (already md) — see finding below.
- **Teardown + migration**: removed `STRUCTURED_PHASE_SCHEMAS`, `run_phase_prompt_structured`, `extract_source_map`, `format_source_map_digest`, `_assemble`/`_render_homework_md`/`_synth_md_for_structured`/`_JSON_COLUMN_SETTERS`/`_unknown_concept_ids`, all `jobs_repo.set_*_json`, `job_artifacts` (whole module), 9 `app/schemas` modules + their `__init__` re-exports, `JobOut.*_json`/`assembled_md`, TS `Job.*_json`. Migration 0018 (`e2a5b8c4f1d9`) drops `assembled_md` + 15 `*_json` columns from `homework_jobs`.

**Decomposition:** this is **Effort A (the plumbing flip)**. **Effort B** (separate, NOT started) = rewrite the 8 phase prompts faithfully to the `docs/Infra_prompts` specs + author each phase's full deterministic validator rules + Uzbek Foundation language contract.

**Verification:** every commit controller-stress-tested. Backend suite 280→**179 passed** (101 removed tests were all structured-layer/schema coverage for deleted code) + 1 pre-existing red (`test_config_notion::test_notion_defaults_disabled`, `.env NOTION_ENABLED=true` leak — NEXT #1). **PROTECTED & kept**: the `_resolve_model` provider-default leak-guard (gemini/kimi/codex→None, claude→sonnet-4-6) + `extract_toc` schema-retry tests. Frontend tsc 0 + `npm run build` OK. Migration 0017 + 0018 applied; 0018 up/down/up round-trip clean.

**LIVE ACCEPTANCE SMOKE — PASS** (CLAUDE.md gate): forced generation of Kimyo §1 (book `d87e4f5c…`) on **claude/sonnet-4-6**, 9/9 phases in ~17 min. All content phases emit markdown (verified `output_md` opens with `#`, real formal-Uzbek chemistry: H₂SO₄ Mr = 1·2+32+16·4 = 98 → N = m/M = 49/98 = 0.5 mol, with wrong-path M=32 error). Validator **correctly flagged** `reflection` `["missing top-level heading (\`# \`)"]`; all other phases clean. Notion: **8 sub-pages** under `Kimyo → "1-§ …" → Homework` (`37499838-1c76-81be-98ab-f64cccc82110`), each with rendered md + attached `.md` (verified CBP sub-page via MCP). Download zip = `01-case-based-preview.md … 08-reflection.md` (extract excluded). `JobOut` serves `phases[].output_md` + `validation_warnings`, no `*_json`. Notion archive runs AFTER status flips `done` (~40s push) — don't read `notion_archived_at` immediately or you'll race it.

**Finding (→ WISHLIST / Effort B):** `reflection.md` outputs `##`-level headings (no top-level `#`), so the validator flags **every** reflection with "missing top-level heading". Trivial fix: give reflection a `#` title, or exempt reflection from that rule.

**Safety:** local backup branch `backup/md-per-phase-pre-teardown` at `dc4a99a` (pre-teardown state). Commits: 15e9e19·7333bf0·6aadefe·31cdbcf·abf6b7d·934a9fd·8731ccf·52dfa58·dc4a99a·1ac49de·7ba2143 (+ spec/plan docs).

## [0029] Notion homework page regroup + attachments-at-top — 2026-06-03 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Restructured the Notion `Homework` sub-tree from 8 flat per-phase pages into a grouped layout (user request). Driven by a declarative `_HOMEWORK_LAYOUT` in `app/services/notion_archive.py`:
- **Case-Based Preview** — own page (unchanged).
- **Flashcards** — one page rendering `flashcards` + `memory-check` **inline** (both `.md` attached at top, then both content sections divider-separated).
- **Gamified Practices** — a **container** page (no body) holding one child sub-page per present game: Real-Life Challenge, Error Detection, and the subject game (memory-match / tictactoe / jigsaw / sentence).
- **Boss Arena** — own page, **excluded** from the games grouping (unchanged).
- **Reflection** — own page (unchanged).

**Attachments moved to the very TOP** of every content page (above the rendered markdown), mirroring how the textbook PDF sits atop a lesson page. One `.md` per phase, kept.

**Code:** `_push_to_notion` signature changed `phases: list[(title,phase,md)]` → `phase_md: dict[phase_name,str]`; new `_leaf_blocks(client, present)` builds attachments-first + content sections; `_HOMEWORK_LAYOUT` (leaf vs container entries) drives the tree; `archive_job` builds the `phase_md` dict. Idempotent per page (skip if `page_has_content`); a job only ever has one practice game so absent game phases are skipped.

**Verification:** TDD — `test_push_builds_grouped_structure` (asserts exact find_or_create title sequence: lesson, Homework, CBP, Flashcards, Gamified Practices, RLC, Error Detection, TicTacToe, Boss Arena, Reflection; 8 uploads, 7 content pages), `test_flashcards_page_attachments_at_top_then_content` (first two blocks are `file`), `test_push_skips_pages_already_populated`. 8 `test_notion_archive.py` pass; full suite **180 passed + 1 pre-existing** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`). **LIVE-verified** against real Notion: pushed the existing Kimyo §1 job's phase-md into a throwaway lesson under the Kimyo subject page, read the tree back via `NotionClientWrapper` (5 top-level pages; Flashcards first blocks `['file','file','divider','heading_1',…]`; Gamified Practices container → 3 game child pages each `['file','divider','heading_1',…]`), then archived/deleted the throwaway.

**Scope note:** only **new** archives use this layout; previously-archived lessons keep the old flat 8-page structure — no Notion migration of existing pages was in scope.

## [0030] Effort B — faithful Infra-spec prompt rewrite (family-aware, markdown) — 2026-06-04 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Rewrote `prompts/_general/*.md` to be faithful to the `docs/Infra_prompts/` specs — markdown-native, family-aware — and purged the dead JSON-field vocabulary Effort A left behind. SHIPPED + live-verified on real gemini output.

**Mechanism (Option A, dict-backed `{{FAMILY_RULES}}`):** `app/services/prompts.py` gains `_SUBJECT_FAMILY` (7 subjects → 4 families: sciences=biology/kimyo/physics · math=math-algebra/geometriya · languages=english · humanities=history) and `FAMILY_RULES[phase][family]` named-block constants. `get_prompt` resolves `{{FAMILY_RULES}}` exactly like `{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}` — `phase_blocks.get(family) or phase_blocks.get("_default","")` — placeholder-gated, with a **no-cross-family-leak** guard (mirrors the `_resolve_model` invariant) and token-no-entry collapse to `""`. Only CBP + Flashcards carry family blocks; the other 9 phases are untouched by the mechanism.

**Tiered rewrite (drift-audited):**
- **Tier 1 (family-aware):** `case-based-preview.md` + `flashcards.md` rewritten as base + `{{FAMILY_RULES}}`; 4 family blocks each in `prompts.py`. **Humanities CBP authored** (no source spec — extrapolated from the 3 CBP specs + the humanities flashcard visual policy). Per-family visual defaults (sciences/languages IMAGE, math/humanities SVG) + atomisation examples ported verbatim from specs (photosynthesis→6, quadratic→3, Present-Perfect→4, Amir Temur→6).
- **Tier 2a (light):** memory-check (reconciled "all 3 kinds"→**≥2 of 3**, distractor-misconception rule), rlc + error-detection (**Strip Test** rule), boss-arena (**weak-skill adaptation** + Why/How/What all-three-mandatory).
- **Tier 2b (compact divergence):** the 4 CBP-mode games (memory-match/tictactoe/jigsaw/sentence) — purged JSON vocab, ported content rules, **kept compact** (deliberately NOT expanded into the specs' full CBP-case framing; a `"checkpoint 3" not in` test guards it).
- **Tier 2c (polish):** reflection emits a top-level `#` title (fixes the validator `_has_top_heading` flag), explicit markdown-only, all-sections gate.

**Reviewer notes folded in:** flashcards enum is **in-prompt only** (`FlashcardType` schema deleted in Effort A); IMAGE-family blocks state the validator-safe `![…](placeholder)` sentinel (never a URL); `{{FAMILY_RULES}}` token-no-entry resolution defined, not just guarded.

**Bonus fix:** pre-existing test-fixture cache-pollution bug — `tmp_prompts`/`tmp_lang`/`tmp_family` monkeypatched `PROMPTS_DIR` + cleared `_cache` with no teardown → order-dependent `KeyError` when resolver ran before coverage. Converted to generators with cache-clear teardown.

**Verification:** spec→plan→subagent-driven (10 tasks), controller stress-tested every commit (read diff + re-run). New `test_prompts_resolver` family tests (leak-guard, `_default` fallback, token-no-entry), `test_prompt_coverage` per-phase anchors + global no-dead-vocab + no-unreplaced-token guards. CBP family-block authoring got an independent spec-faithfulness review (clean — forbids verified as exact ports, not hallucinated). Suite **195 passed + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`). **LIVE SMOKE PASS** (real gemini, history/humanities — the authored variant): job `defe06b6`, 8 phases all markdown, **validator-clean (0 warnings) · 0 dead JSON vocab · 0 JSON fences** across every file; CBP rendered the full 10-section structure with a structural SVG (Xalq majlisi↔Konung) per the humanities visual policy; flashcards atomised correctly with a causal-discipline `misconception` card ("Rome fell only due to barbarians" corrected). Commits e118f03·f0b80a8·5be1c6f·ae049db·99cb49b·5383d94·b0bf697·9b49c67·9b6dd9f (spec `0dbb34a`, plan `61dc6c6`). NEXT = per-phase `phase_validator.RULES` (derive from the finalized prompts) + WS5 Uzbek contract; **Notion lesson-page matching** spec (`299f5b1`) is build-ready.

## [0031] Job Resilience — phase resume + provider failover + lease-TTL reclaim — 2026-06-04 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** a job now **survives interruption** without redoing finished work or dying on one provider's failure. Four composing mechanisms over one "rebuild phase context from persisted rows" foundation. Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-job-resilience-resume-failover-design.md`, plan `…/plans/2026-06-03-job-resilience-resume-failover-plan.md` (8 tasks, subagent-driven, controller stress-tested every commit: read diff + re-run).

**Pieces:**
- **T1 config** (`6beb25d`): `heartbeat_seconds=30`, `reclaim_stale_seconds=120` (lease TTL), `per_attempt_timeout_seconds=600`, `failover_provider_order=[codex,gemini,kimi,opencode]` (**claude deliberately excluded** — reserved for the user's Max allocation).
- **T2 attribution** (`44af511`): additive `phase_outputs.provider` (migration 0019 `a7c1e9d2b4f8`, down/up round-trip verified) — records who *actually* produced each phase.
- **T3 heartbeat** (`f124377`): `jobs_repo.touch_claim` (refresh `claimed_at` only while `status='running'`) + `Worker._heartbeat` task wrapping `_execute_job` (awaited cancel in `finally`). **Prerequisite for T4.**
- **T4 lease reclaim** (`17ca630` + test-strengthen `b805ee4`): `_sweep_stuck_jobs` uses `reclaim_stale_seconds` (was `job_timeout×2`); `main.lifespan` resets orphaned `running→pending` (stale=0) **inside the commit block** (a reviewer caught the original "after commit → silent no-op" placement). Single-host assumption documented (not multi-pod safe).
- **T5 classifier** (`515d056`): pure `failure_classifier.classify(error) → transient|wall|hard`, transient-checked-before-wall so "not your usage limit" ≠ wall.
- **T6 failover** (`4925edf`): pure injectable `_run_with_failover(run_fn)` + `_failover_chain` in `pipeline.py`; `_execute_phase` non-extract branch runs through it, records `produced_by`. `asyncio.TimeoutError` intercepted BEFORE the classifier → immediate failover (no same-provider retry on a hang). Same-retry budget transient=2/hard=1/wall=0.
- **T7 resume** (`213574f`): pure `_done_phase_md`/`_pending_phases`; `pipeline.run` pre-loads `done` rows (skip+reuse extract, pre-inject content into `prior_outputs`); scheduler `pending` excludes pre-injected. **Always-on, no flag** — `force`-generate makes a NEW job so nothing is skipped; `/retry` resumes (ratified behavior change).

**Two-review pass before build:** two independent fresh-session reviews, every claim re-verified against live code. Fixes folded: `_reclaim_loop`→`_sweep_stuck_jobs` (no such method), the lifespan-reset commit placement (🔴 silent no-op), `per_attempt_timeout` 300→**600s** (CBP alone is ~274s — 300 would kill legit work → `asyncio.TimeoutError`→misclassified failover), force-citation file, T2 line anchors. Two judgment calls ratified (resume-on-`/retry`; failover-budget can exceed `job_timeout`, resume is the backstop).

**Verification:** suite **218 passed + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`); DB-free tests (signature/getsource/pure-fn) per the harness; migration round-trip verified. **LIVE ACCEPTANCE SMOKE — PASS** (CLAUDE.md gate; opencode-requested so **claude budget untouched**): retried interrupted history job `6a760767` (opencode/mimo-v2.5-free). Proven on real data: **resume** (attempt 1 reused 1 done extract; attempt 2 skipped 5 done phases, `completed_at` unchanged) · **heartbeat** (`claimed_at` advanced ~30s, age 8.8s ≪ 120s lease) · **multi-hop failover** opencode→codex→gemini on every content phase (`agent_usages` shows codex hard-fail ×2 then gemini success) · **per-attempt timeout** (opencode hung the full 600s each wave → immediate failover) · **attribution** (all 8 content phases stamped `provider=gemini`, the actual producer; job badge stays requested `opencode`) · **job_timeout→retry→resume** (3 opencode 600s waves ate the 1800s budget → attempt 1 TIMED OUT → attempt 2 resumed and finished — the *ratified* timeout-budget interaction, self-healing as predicted) · **2-attempt convergence** → job `done`, 9/9 phases. **Controller mis-diagnosis owned:** I alarmed about a heartbeat-lapse/duplicate-execution bug mid-smoke; the server log disproved it — clean `job_timeout`, no live-reclaim. Fact over theory.

**Findings logged:** **R11** (ROADMAP `dcdd5e7`) — failover not legibly recorded (`phase_outputs.model_name` stale after failover, timed-out attempt leaves no `agent_usages` row, no explicit switch flag); LOW priority, ties to the autonomous-gen observability pillar. WISHLIST — opencode too flaky to be a *primary* provider (600s hangs blow the job budget + burn attempts); fine as last-resort fallback only.

**NEXT (resilience follow-ups):** R11 observability; consider a shorter per-attempt timeout for known-flaky providers; multi-pod-safe startup reset (host-scoped or lease-window) when horizontal scale lands.

## [0032] Notion lesson-page matching — word-match adoption + Generated Lessons fallback — 2026-06-04 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** the Notion archive now places each lesson's `Homework` **fully automatically** (no operator — the system is going autonomous). When the app's TOC-extracted lesson title **uniquely** matches a human-built Notion lesson page by *content words*, it writes `Homework` **inside** that page (co-located with Adrian's Text/Video/Images). Otherwise it creates an app-owned **`Generated Lessons`** container under the subject and puts the lesson there. Fixes the prior *accidental* behavior (exact-normalized-title match → co-located only when a human happened to type the app's exact format: kimyo yes, history/algebra no). The `_HOMEWORK_LAYOUT` subtree (worklog [0029]) is untouched — this only changes *where the lesson page hangs*.

**Why words-not-marks:** verified against the live workspace + DB — human page titles diverge by subject (`1-§ …` kimyo · `1-mavzu. …………………6` history with U+2026 leaders · `1. … ....57` algebra ASCII leaders), and algebra's human page is a *genuinely different lesson* (different words). So: tokenize to content words, adopt on a **unique subset match**, else fall back. History adopts (identical words once marks stripped), kimyo adopts, algebra correctly falls back (0 candidates).

**Code:**
- New pure `app/services/notion/lesson_match.py` — `tokenize(title)→frozenset` (drops numbers, `§`, punctuation, **page-leaders: ASCII dot-runs AND U+2026 ellipsis glyphs**, marker words `mavzu/bob/bolim/paragraf`; folds apostrophe variants — **NOT diacritics**) + `match_lesson(app_title, human_pages)→str|None` (app words ⊆ human words, **unique** candidate, ≥2 content-word guard, `Generated Lessons` excluded via `_normalize` both sides). `CONTAINER_TITLE="Generated Lessons"`.
- `notion_archive._push_to_notion:133` — `get_child_pages` → `match_lesson`; hit → adopt, miss → `find_or_create(subject,"Generated Lessons")`→`find_or_create(container,lesson_title)`. Line 134+ unchanged.

**Decisions (brainstormed):** app owns its pages by default (at autonomy scale most lessons have NO human template); adopt the human page only on a unique word-match; **subset+unique** strictness (a false-positive adoption — homework in the wrong lesson, silently, no operator to catch — is the worst outcome, so bias to fallback); no stopword list / no stemming in v1 (near-miss safely falls back).

**Process:** spec `299f5b1` → plan `3222ac2` (each passed an independent fresh-session review — 5 reviewer notes folded incl. the U+2026 ellipsis-leader test on the verbatim history title). Built in an **isolated git worktree** (parallel with the live job-resilience subagent-dev — verified file-disjoint, no rival migration; the worktree gave a clean test gate + own index). 2 tasks subagent-driven, controller stress-tested each commit (read diff + re-ran). Worktree commits `4c80017` (matcher, 11 tests) · `17fc0b5` (wiring + 2 path tests + 3 existing-test updates). Merged `--no-ff` → `6a2a361`; **combined full suite 231 passed + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`); worktree removed, branch deleted.

**Handoff:** wrote `docs/handoff-notion-archive-validation.md` for the (separate) content-validator session — the expected Notion tree, phase→page map (memory-check inline in Flashcards; games under Gamified Practices), DB success markers (`notion_archived_at`, `toc_entries.notion_homework_page_id`), idempotency caveats, and a verified `defe06b6` example.

**NEXT:** Notion **anchor auto-resolve** (WISHLIST — resolve subject page by crawling grade→`{N}-sinf`→subject instead of the hand-maintained map) is the related-but-separate follow-on; the content-validator (separate session, spec `78b0c73`) will assert archived structure using the handoff doc.

## [0033] Fetch From Notion -- alternate textbook source (SHIPPED + live-verified) -- 2026-06-05 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** the New Session screen gets a source chooser -- **Fetch From Notion** | **Upload a Book**. Fetch walks the Notion `Lessons` tree (grade -> Uzbek `N - sinf` -> subject), downloads the textbook PDF attached to the subject page, and joins the existing upload->TOC->generate pipeline at the same point an upload does. Upload is unchanged. **sinf-only v1** (Russian `N - klass` is hidden -- a separate WS5-sibling effort); **keep the 7 supported subjects** (others listed disabled: "unsupported" / "no textbook"); **reject >20 MB** (Gemini TOC ceiling) with a clear message and no dead book row.

**Architecture:** "Fetch" is an alternate *source of PDF bytes* + a navigation layer; nothing in book/TOC/generate changed. The inline `upload_book` body was extracted into a shared `ingest_pdf(session, *, body, subject, grade, filename) -> BookOut` (replicating **both** original return shapes -- dedup hit -> `_book_out_with_toc`, new book -> plain `BookOut`); `upload_book` is now a thin wrapper. New `app/services/notion_fetch.py` (pure helpers `_map_subject`/`_first_pdf_block`/`_url_from_block`/`_fold` + crawl `list_grades`/`list_subjects` + `download_textbook`). New read-only `/notion` router (grades, subjects; `asyncio.to_thread` around the sync wrapper) mounted with `Depends(get_current_user)`; `POST /books/from-notion` on `books.router`. New `settings.notion_lessons_root`; `NotionClientWrapper.get_page_title` (rate-limited). Frontend: `NotionGrade`/`NotionSubject` types + 3 api methods + a `source`-switched wizard in `upload.tsx`.

**Subject map:** folded longest-keyword-first substring (`ozbekiston tarixi`/`jahon tarixi`/`tarix`->history, `geometriya`, `biolog`->biology, `algebra`->math-algebra, `ingliz`->english, `fizika`->physics, `kimyo`); **`matematika` deliberately absent** (lower-grade math != the app's algebra).

**Process:** spec `d61ad0b` -> plan `35bd683` (+ review fixes `edb16c3`, three fresh-session reviews folded: router-split+auth, dual-return-shape, BookOut response-model mock, `.pdf` file-block guard, rate-limited `get_page_title`). 10 tasks subagent-driven, **controller stress-tested every commit** (read diff + re-ran tests independently). In-place on `Nggaev-v2` (user confirmed no parallel session). Commits: `b83f790` (config) `872b7bf` (ingest_pdf refactor) `5c2c66c` (pure helpers) `a92e145` (crawl) `146c846` (download) `c8c7e60` (/notion router) `088d807` (/books/from-notion) `8ec5750` (FE api+types) `cce089d` (FE wizard) + `3e8687e` (the HEAD->stream fix below).

**ACCEPTANCE GATE caught a real bug (fact over theory).** Live read-only smoke proved the crawl is perfect (11 grades; 21 subjects under 9-sinf; all 7 supported subjects mapped, rest `app=None`; `has_textbook` flags correct) **but `download_textbook` 403'd**. Root cause (verified by a HEAD-vs-GET probe against the live S3 URL): Notion attachment URLs are **presigned for GET only** -- the plan's `HEAD` Content-Length pre-check 403s on a signature mismatch. Fix (`3e8687e`, controller-direct TDD: red test first): a **streaming GET**, read `Content-Length` off the response headers, reject oversize **before** reading the body (post-read length fallback kept). Re-smoke PASS: grade-9 Kimyo downloads 3.84 MB with valid `%PDF-` magic; grade-2 Ingliz tili (54.4 MB) rejected as `54.4 MB > 20 MB`.

**Product finding (important):** a size scan of all grades' supported subjects shows **20 of ~47 textbooks (~43%) exceed 20 MB** -- far above the spec's "~1 in 4" estimate (e.g. grade-9 Jahon tarixi is **497 MB**; grade-7 Biologiya 93.6 MB). So **~43% of fetchable books are un-generatable until subset-TOC/auto-shrink lands** -- that follow-on (logged) is now the top product gap for this feature, not a nicety.

**Verification:** backend suite **268 passed + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`, unrelated `.env` leak); `tsc --noEmit` 0; `npm run build` OK. Throwaway smoke/probe/scan scripts removed.

**NEXT:** subset-TOC / auto-shrink for >20 MB books (top gap, ~43% affected); workbook-vs-textbook preference when a subject page has multiple PDFs (v1 takes first-in-order); Russian `klass` generation (needs WS5 Uzbek-sibling language contract); optional grade-greying in the picker.

## [0034] TOC bugs — section-reshuffle fix (SHIPPED) + garbled-heading root-cause — 2026-06-05 (Nggaev-v2)
**Two TOC defects reported on book `54cd9ff3` (Kimyo 8-sinf). Root-caused both; shipped the clean one, tracked the other.**

**Bug A — sections reshuffle after refresh/navigation (SHIPPED, `86bb0bd`).** Root cause: `GET /books/{id}` (and the SSE replay) serialize the TOC via `_book_out_with_toc` → `_enriched_toc_entries` which iterates the **`book.toc_entries` ORM relationship** (`books.py:292`), and that relationship (`app/models/book.py:32`) had **no `order_by`** → SQLAlchemy emits no `ORDER BY` → Postgres returns rows in arbitrary heap order, which **drifts the moment any row is updated** (`notion_homework_page_id` at archive, TOC edits) → reshuffle on refresh. The ordered `toc_entries.list_for_book` (`order_by(order_index)`) existed but the read path bypassed it. Data was fine (`order_index` unique 0..N); only the read ordering was unpinned. Intermittent because a freshly-extracted book's heap is coincidentally sorted until a row is touched. **Fix:** one line — `order_by="TOCEntry.order_index"` on the relationship (covers REST + SSE + any `book.toc_entries` access). Controller-direct TDD (introspection regression test red→green; live-confirmed `order_by=False`→`['order_index']`). Suite **279 passed + 1 pre-existing red**. Per CLAUDE.md small-clear-fix exception (verified root cause + low risk → no spec/plan).

**Bug B — garbled chapter/theme headings (root-caused, NOT fixed → tracked in [[R10]]).** Root cause: the book's back-of-book Mundarija (PDF p.205) sets the `bob`/theme **headings** in a decorative font with a broken cmap; `_extract_toc_source_text` (`agent.py:899`, pypdf) extracts them **letter-correct but case-scrambled + space-injected** (`KURSINING ENG MUHIM…` → `KUr SININg e Ng MUh IM MaVZUl ar…`; even `MUNDARIJA`→`MUNDar IJa`); the extractor prompt (`agent.py:1016`) hands that excerpt to gemini as authoritative TOC text → it lands verbatim in `chapter_title`. Clean `§` body-font sections come through fine; frontend renders faithfully (not a UI bug; the gray uppercase line is just the same string `text-transform`'d). **Second manifestation of R10** (glyph-ID case) — but it **breaks R10's proposed printable-ratio heuristic** (the garble is all real letters). NOT a clean one-liner: the real fix is prompt-level (prefer gemini's native visual read for TITLE fields; excerpt only for page numbers) and **touches the generation path → needs a real gemini re-extract smoke** before shipping. Deferred per user ("only clean-verified fixes ship without a spec"). Evidence + sharpened deliverable folded into ROADMAP [[R10]].

## [0035] Extract robustness — local-text injection + deterministic gates + failover (SHIPPED + live-verified) — 2026-06-05 (Nggaev-v2)
**The per-lesson `extract` phase no longer silently produces garbage.** Root incident (R12, history book `41aec815`): gemini-cli's `read_file` now honors gitignore (`var/` is in `.gitignore:22`) → it could no longer read `source.pdf` → `gemini-2.5-flash` returned `rc=0` with a **275-char "couldn't read the PDF" refusal** that *looked* like success; that empty `lesson_context` threaded into every downstream phase → refusals ("Dars konteksti mavjud emas") / hallucinations; the job "completed" 9/9 with garbage, caught only by the LLM judge. Three structural facts made it worse: the block hits **all** gemini models; **R2** (printed≠physical pages) means page numbers can't be trusted; extract was a **single point of failure** (no failover, unlike content phases since §0031).

**Fix (new extract flow):** read the WHOLE book's text **locally** via pypdf (no CLI file-read → dodges the gitignore block AND the >20MB gemini ceiling for the injected text) → the model **locates the lesson by TITLE** within that text (R2-immune; page numbers passed only as a hint) → **two deterministic gates** → **`_run_with_failover`** across providers → **loud-fail** (never silent garbage).
- **Gate A** (raw local text, pre-summary, **terminal**): min-length + printable-letter-ratio → catches scanned / broken-font / empty PDFs no provider can fix.
- **Gate B** (produced summary, per-attempt, **failover**): length floor (400, above the 275 refusal) + refusal-marker anchored to the first 240 chars (so a legitimate Uzbek `"…mavjud emas"` deep in a real summary never false-fires) → raises typed `ExtractRefusal` → classifier `"wall"` → **0 same-provider retries = immediate failover**.
- **Size gate**: whole-book text > `extract_max_text_chars` (600K) → terminal `"book too large — needs subset-TOC/shrink"` (the >20MB / scanned ~43%-of-corpus slice is the deferred subset effort; `read_whole_book_text` reads a 64K MARGIN past the budget so overflow is actually detectable — the plan-review blocker).

**Code:** `app/config.py` (4 settings: `extract_max_text_chars=600_000`, `extract_min_text_chars=500`, `extract_min_printable_ratio=0.55`, `extract_min_summary_chars=400`); `failure_classifier.py` (`ExtractRefusal` → `classify` short-circuit `"wall"`); `agent.py` (`validate_extract_text`=Gate A + `validate_extract_summary`=Gate B pure fns; `read_whole_book_text` local pypdf whole-book read [module-level `from pypdf import PdfReader` added] + pure `extract_text_is_oversize`; `summarize_lesson` single-provider text-injected summarizer, `attachments=[]`, no Gate/failover inside); `pipeline.py` `_execute_phase` extract branch rewired (local text → size gate → Gate A terminal → `_run_with_failover` over `summarize_lesson` with Gate B in the run_fn) + cache key `builtin:extract:v1`→`v2`. The old `extract_lesson_context` is left in place (now unused by pipeline; harmless — cleanup later).

**Failure handling is message-only (verified, no new mechanism):** extract is a head phase; on any raise `_execute_one_phase` marks the job `failed` + raises → `pipeline.run`'s head loop catches and `return`s cleanly (`pipeline.py:161-164`) → `mark_failed_with_retry` is never called → extract failures already neither consume `max_attempts` nor auto-retry; the job sits `failed` until a manual `/retry`. The fix's value is the distinct, loud failure REASON + never proceeding downstream with bad output.

**Process:** spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-05-extract-robustness-design.md` (two adversarial reviews folded: cache cite `:881`→`:532`; terminal-vs-recoverable reframed to message-only after tracing the head-loop unwind) → plan `…/plans/2026-06-05-extract-robustness-plan.md` (review caught the **size-gate blocker** — `_read_pdf_pages` truncates to budget so `len > budget` could never fire → fixed with the read-margin + pure `extract_text_is_oversize`). 6 TDD tasks **subagent-driven** (sonnet), **controller stress-tested every commit** (read diff + re-ran tests independently). Suite **280 passed + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`, unrelated `.env` leak).

**ACCEPTANCE GATE — real gemini CLI smoke (fact over theory).** In-process smoke on the exact book/lesson that produced the garbage (`41aec815`, "Franklar davlati", §2-3): local read = **227,237 chars** from the 15.6MB PDF with `attachments=0` (no PDF attached → gitignore-immune); not oversize; Gate A PASS; `summarize_lesson` via real gemini-2.5-flash (`prompt_chars=228,902`, `attachments=0`, input=89,961 / output=528 tok, 33s) → a **1,544-char real factual summary** (Xlodvig, Merovinglar, Suasson jangi, 496-yil xristianlik, Karl Martell / Puate / benefisiy) — **NOT** the 275-char refusal; Gate B PASS. Verdict: REAL SUMMARY. Throwaway smoke removed.

**Resolves / updates:** **R12(b)** extract no-failover → FIXED (extract now runs through `_run_with_failover`, claude still excluded); **R12(c)** gitignore-blocked PDF read → FIXED (local text bypass, obsoletes the `respectGitIgnore:false` workaround); **R12(a)** blank `EXTRACT_PROVIDER` KeyError → now *mitigated* by failover (a blank-provider first attempt fails over) but the config-validator footgun-removal remains the clean fix (kept in R12). **R10** broken-font glyph garbage is now caught by **Gate A at the extract phase** — but R10's primary manifestation is at **TOC extraction** (a separate gemini file-read path, deliberately out of scope here), so R10 stays OPEN for TOC. **New follow-up flagged (WISHLIST `extract-1`):** TOC extraction has the SAME gitignore + glyph risk on its own file-read path — the local-text + title-locate building blocks from this effort can be reused there.

**NEXT:** subset-TOC / auto-shrink for >20MB & scanned books (the deferred slice — terminal-fails here today; ~43% of corpus; WISHLIST `fetch-1`); apply local-text read to TOC extraction (`extract-1`); R12(a) blank→default config validator.

## [0036] TOC garbled-heading fix — de-garble in extract_toc prompt (SHIPPED + live-verified) — 2026-06-05 (Nggaev-v2)
**Closes the case-scramble half of [[R10]]'s TOC manifestation (Bug B from [[0034]]).** Broken-cmap decorative heading fonts make pypdf extract chapter/theme titles **letter-correct but case-scrambled + space-injected** (`KUr SININg e Ng MUh IM` for `KURSINING ENG MUHIM`; `MUNDARIJA`→`MUNDar IJa`); `extract_toc` fed that garble to gemini verbatim → stored in `chapter_title`. **Fix:** a single **general, conditional** clause in the `extract_toc` instruction (`agent.py:~1078`) — *"ONLY when a title shows this scrambling, reconstruct it into clean normally-spaced Uzbek; leave every title that already reads normally exactly as printed."* Applies to **every book/subject** (not keyed to any book); the `KUr SININg` bit is just an illustrative pattern example.

**Why prompt-not-vision:** brainstormed against the just-shipped [[0035]] which standardized on **local-text, no model-reads-PDF** (gemini-cli `read_file` honors gitignore → `var/` PDFs unreadable; the "attach PDF for native read" backstop has been silently dead). Two routes proven live: (A) stage a non-gitignored PDF copy → gemini **vision** reads clean headings; (B) gemini **de-garbles the readable-but-scrambled text with no PDF at all**. Chose **B** — consistent with 0035, zero infra, no gitignore workaround. Decisive probes: (1) gemini vision off a non-ignored copy → perfect headings; (2) gemini given ONLY the 4 garbled strings → reconstructed all 4 perfectly.

**Process:** brainstorm (surfaced the gitignore root cause + the 0035 reframing + option B) → controller-direct TDD per the small-verified-fix rule (user: "clean verified fixes skip spec/plan"). Structural guard test (clause present) red→green. **ACCEPTANCE: real gemini `extract_toc` smoke** on the garbled book `54cd9ff3` — **v1** over-normalized (Title-Cased the clean `§` sections); tightened the clause to "leave clean titles as printed" → **v2**: 4 chapter headings repaired to clean ALL-CAPS, **section titles byte-for-byte unchanged** (sentence case preserved). Suite **280 + 1 pre-existing red**. Commit `1fde640`. **Forward-only** (user's call): re-extracted/new books come clean; the one stored garbled book stays until re-extracted.

**Scope limit (fundamental, NOT book-specific):** handles the *scrambled-but-readable* corruption (letters present) on any book. Does **not** recover the *glyph-ID* (`/Gxx`) corruption (R10's original Geometriya) — there the letters are physically gone from the text layer, unrecoverable from text; that tail needs vision/staging or Gate-A-at-TOC. **R10:** case-scramble TOC sub-case CLOSED forward; glyph-ID TOC sub-case + `extract-1` (local-text-for-TOC) remain OPEN.

## [0037] LLM Phase Validator — self-verifying judge replaces the deterministic rule engine (SHIPPED + live-verified) — 2026-06-05 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** Every generated content phase is now graded by an **LLM judge** against its own prompt contract; the deterministic warn-only `phase_validator` (worklog [[0028]]) is **retired/deleted**. The judge is **prompt-derived** (reads `get_prompt(subject, phase)` — the exact composed family/language contract the generator saw), **self-verifying** (one CLI call lists violations each citing the exact offending text, then refutes its own list — anti-hallucination), and **retry-then-warn** (one regen with the cited failures fed back; still failing → accept with warnings; never blocks the job; `extract` exempt; any judge error degrades to `judge-unavailable`). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-04-llm-phase-validator-design.md`, plan `…/plans/2026-06-04-llm-phase-validator-plan.md`.

**Pieces (plan T1–T4):** **T1 `model_tiers.py`** (`83f9ffa`) judge-model selection. **T2 `agent.run_phase` `operation` param** (`d4c72a7`) → `judge:<phase>` usage attribution (6 sites were hardcoded `phase.run`). **T3 `phase_judge.py`** (`0478fb8`→`b93fea1`) `Verdict`/`Failure` via `run_phase(schema=…)` reparse, cite-then-refute meta-prompt, graceful degradation; a code-review caught a real **never-raise bug** (`get_prompt` outside the `try`) → widened + behavioral tests. **T4 wire into `_execute_phase`** (`651961d`) keyed off the **actual producer** `produced_by`; regen reuses `_run_with_failover` (guarded — an exhausted regen keeps the original, never fails the job); retired `phase_validator.py` + test.

**Post-plan tuning (controller-direct, each proven by a real CLI smoke before commit):** log the cited reasons at rejection (`98e75eb`); **judge model → configurable, default `claude-opus-4-7`** (`50174dd`, user request) — was a NON-claude tier-up to spare the Max pool; switched to opus (strongest, so ≥ every generator; quota tradeoff flagged + reversible via `JUDGE_PROVIDER`/`JUDGE_MODEL`); `_SELF_FALLBACK` (opus-gen → gemini-3.1-pro peer) — also **fixed the failover-to-gemini "unjudged" bug** (old no-self alternate was phantom codex); flashcards prompt dropped the rigid `front 3–14` / `back 5–22` word-count **minimums** (`def1d66`); **severity gate** (`67d7882`, a verified enhancement NOT in the plan) — judge tags each `Failure` `major|minor`, **regen ONLY on `major`**, minor nits warn-only → kills the pedantic-regen cost (the ~18-min CBP won't regen over a word-count nit). Verified the assumption first: real opus smoke classified planted major→`major`, minor→`minor`, and caught real majors.

**Codex:** confirmed actually installed (`codex.exe`, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\OpenAI\Codex\bin`) and **working end-to-end through the agent** (real call returned clean output). The judge's `codex not found` was only the **server's stale PATH** — restart fixes it; no code change (`shutil.which("codex")` finds it).

**Process:** spec→plan **fresh-session reviewed** — 2 plan bugs caught & fixed pre-execution (dropped `failure_classifier` import → would NameError shipped failover; unguarded regen → would fail the job). T1–T4 **subagent-driven**, controller stress-tested every commit (read diff + re-ran); T3/T4 code-quality subagent review. Post-plan tuning controller-direct (each a *verified* move).

**LIVE ACCEPTANCE (real opus/gemini CLI smokes + real jobs):** catches a planted CBP violation **with citation**; **zero hallucinations** (every flag evidence-backed); **placeholder rule both ways** (correct `![…](placeholder)` compliant, invented `http(s)` URL flagged); opus judges cleanly with structured Verdict (more thorough than gemini); **severity classification reliable**; **e2e regen on real jobs** — `ec3c52d5` (flashcards + CBP rejected → regenerated → passed) and `927b664d` (flashcards rejected on **4 substantive** issues — answer-leaking hint + omitted `quldorlik`/`erkin dehqonlar` + atomization → regen → done; residual word-count nit → warn, no loop). Suite **281 + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`).

**Commits:** `83f9ffa`·`d4c72a7`·`0478fb8`·`b93fea1`·`651961d`·`98e75eb`·`50174dd`·`def1d66`·`67d7882` (+ spec/plan + WISHLIST `d069f4a`/`d6b93b9`).

**Deferred / superseded:** the **Notion archive validator** (a SEPARATE deterministic job-level check) is spec'd (`78b0c73`) + planned (`9aad1ae`) but **NOT executed** — parked in WISHLIST by decision. The old "hand-author `phase_validator.RULES`" item is **superseded** (engine retired). **⚠ RESTART** the server to pick up judge=opus + codex + softened flashcards + severity gate. **NEXT:** watch the claude-Max draw from opus judging (flip to gemini via env if it bites); distinguish `judge-unavailable` infra warnings from content warnings in the console (WISHLIST).

## [0038] Cancel Generation — kill a running job + its CLI process tree (SHIPPED + live-verified) — 2026-06-05 (Nggaev-v2)
**What:** an operator can cancel a queued or running homework job; a running cancel **kills every provider-CLI subprocess (and its children/grandchildren)**, unwinds the pipeline, and marks the job `cancelled`. User decision: **hard stop** — discard the in-flight phase, but **preserve completed phases** so `/retry` can resume. Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-05-cancel-generation-design.md`, plan `…/plans/2026-06-05-cancel-generation.md`.

**Why it was mostly in the scheduler, not the endpoint (the key insight, found by review):** `_spawn` already kills its CLI on its own `CancelledError` (`agent.py:346`) — but (a) it was **top-process-only** (`proc.kill()`), leaking node/python grandchildren, and (b) cancelling a job did **not** propagate to the running phase tasks: `_run_content_phases_parallel` waits at `await asyncio.wait(...)` which does NOT cancel its awaitables, and the existing peer-cancel is an `except Exception` (can't catch `CancelledError`, a `BaseException`). So a naive cancel would mark the job cancelled while CLIs kept burning tokens. The feature's core is making cancellation **cascade**.

**Pieces (plan T1–T11):** **C1 scheduler cancel-cascade** (`pipeline.py`, `81bd644`) — wrap the loop in `except asyncio.CancelledError` → cancel all `in_flight` + `gather` (so each `_spawn` runs its kill) → re-raise; head phase (extract) already propagates. **C2 `proc_tree.kill_tree`** (`a6d6e49`, new `psutil` dep) — suspend parent → `children(recursive=True)` → kill → `wait_procs`; wired into `_spawn` replacing `proc.kill()` (`406e357`). **Repo helpers** (`64989e6`) — `cancel_if_pending` (atomic pending→cancelled), `request_cancel` (running→cancelling), `get_status`, `mark_cancelled` (job=cancelled, non-done phases→failed, **done preserved**), `reclaim_stale_cancelling`. **Worker layer** — `RUNNING_JOBS` registry (`a29aa91`); `_heartbeat` reads status + self-cancels on `cancelling` for the cross-pod path (`935e337`); **shielded discriminating finalize** (`0b55e84`) — `except CancelledError` reads status: `cancelling`→`uncancel()`+`shield(mark_cancelled)`, else shutdown re-raise; **stale-`cancelling` sweep** (`a166e6a`, crash-mid-cancel backstop). **Endpoint** `POST /jobs/{id}/cancel` (`bb8ba16`) — atomic queue-cancel → else running→cancelling + instant `RUNNING_JOBS[id].cancel()` → else 404/409. **`/retry` allows cancelled** as resume (`c1fa229`). **FE** Cancel button + `cancelling`/`cancelled` status (`cb27933`; also fixed the exhaustive `Record<JobStatus>` in `book.tsx` — required type-widen consequence). **No migration** (`status` is free `String(32)`).

**Process:** spec **reviewed 3×** (fresh-session), each fold verified against live code before the next: review-1 → the scheduler-cascade reframe + portable tree-kill; review-2 → heartbeat status-read is net-new, stale-`cancelling` sweep, shielded finalize; review-3 → the `JobOut` test stubs omitted required `toc_entry_id` (→500 not 200, same class as fetch-0033 Task 7) + `uncancel()` double-cancel hardening. 11 tasks **subagent-driven**, controller stress-tested every commit (read real diff + re-ran tests). Worker-trap mechanisms each got a dedicated task + source-level guard test (the half-work spots: cascade, heartbeat self-cancel, shielded finalize, stale sweep).

**LIVE ACCEPTANCE (real gemini CLI, in-process, CLAUDE.md gate):** spawned a real `gemini-2.5-flash` call → a **3-process tree** (`gemini.CMD → node → child`, PIDs e.g. 3784/9772/9840); cancelled the task mid-flight → **`alive AFTER cancel: []`** — entire tree reaped, **0 orphans** (proves C2 + cancel→`_spawn`→`kill_tree` on a real CLI incl. grandchildren). `kill_tree` also unit-proven on a real parent+child (T1). Suite **299 passed + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`); `tsc` 0 + `npm run build` OK.

**Commits:** `a6d6e49`·`406e357`·`81bd644`·`64989e6`·`a29aa91`·`935e337`·`0b55e84`·`a166e6a`·`bb8ba16`·`c1fa229`·`cb27933` (+ spec/plan `b5eac84`·`dad30ad`·`22a0b84`·`c800a77`).

**NEXT / notes:** the full HTTP→worker→cascade→multi-parallel-CLI chain is proven link-by-link (endpoint tests + cascade structural + real-CLI tree-kill smoke) — a single end-to-end live run (cancel a real multi-phase generation, assert all CLIs die) is worth doing once the server's up, plus the queue-cancel + resume-on-retry paths. A sub-`heartbeat_seconds` cross-pod cancel watcher is a logged follow-on (separate-pod cancel latency ≈ `heartbeat_seconds`; embedded is instant). **RESTART the server** to expose the endpoint + Cancel button.

## [0039] SSE session leak — release DB session before yielding in job/book streams (SHIPPED) — 2026-06-05 (Nggaev-v2)
**Fixed a `SQLAlchemy "non-checked-in connection" GC warning** seen during a live generation run. Hunt (controller-direct, verified against code): all ~25 `SessionLocal()` uses ARE `async with`-wrapped (no missing-context-manager bug); the unretained-TOC-`create_task` candidate was **already fixed** (`books.py:89-91` retains via `_TOC_TASKS` + `add_done_callback` — the WISHLIST `:80` ref was stale). Real cause: **both SSE generators (`jobs.stream_job`, `books.stream_toc`) `yield` their initial-state events while still inside the `async with SessionLocal()` block** → the pooled connection is held open across the suspension; on an abrupt client disconnect the async generator can be GC'd without a clean `aclose()` → connection orphaned → GC reaps it with the warning. (The pypdf line in the log was a red herring — just where GC happened to fire, in the 0035 whole-book read thread.) **Caveat:** the in-session-yield is the strongest candidate by inspection, not reproduced/proven to be the sole source — but the fix is correct hygiene regardless.

**Fix:** snapshot the needed data into plain dicts INSIDE the session block, release the session, THEN yield (event order preserved exactly — traced every branch). Both streams. `_enriched_toc_entries(session, …)` stays inside the block. **AST regression test** (`tests/api/test_sse_session_no_yield.py`) parses each endpoint's source and asserts no `yield`/`yield from` sits inside an `async with SessionLocal()` — robust structural guard (endpoints need a live DB to run). Controller-direct TDD (red→green), per CLAUDE.md small-clear-fix exception. Suite **301 + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`); import clean. Commit `2f56136`.

**Related WISHLIST item now moot:** the "SSE teardown noise / asyncpg CancelledError" entry is the same area — this removes the connection-leak half (the `CancelledError` log line on `EventSource` close may remain; cosmetic).

## [0040] Gate A false-positive — readability ratio over visible chars, not total (SHIPPED) — 2026-06-06 (Nggaev-v2)
**Fixed a 0035 regression that terminal-failed readable math/science books.** Live job `98d41117` (7-sinf Algebra, `62865c70`, 27.6 MB) failed at `extract` with `unreadable PDF (no text layer): printable-letter ratio 0.44`. Root cause (verified in code): `agent.validate_extract_text` computed `letters / len(stripped)` where `stripped = text.strip()` only trims OUTER whitespace — so the denominator still included all internal layout whitespace, digits, and symbols. A math textbook extracts with ~35% layout whitespace + digits + symbols, sinking a perfectly readable book below the 0.55 floor. Hit algebra/physics/chemistry/geometry broadly, not just this book.

**Fix:** divide by VISIBLE (non-whitespace) chars — "of the actual glyphs, how many are letters" — the real readability signal, immune to layout whitespace. Threshold unchanged (0.55). **Verified before shipping:** scanned all 12 books on disk — only the algebra was wrongly rejected (`old 0.444 → new 0.680 pass`); every other real book already passed and passes wider (≥0.815); English book from the same log (`d463c690`) passes both. Synthesized both garbage modes the gate exists for — glyph-soup `/Gxx` → 0.25, CID-soup → 0.375 — both still REJECT. Clean separation band: real ≥0.68, garbage ≤0.375, floor 0.55 in the gap. Regression test (`test_extract_gates.py`) uses a math-shaped fixture that asserts the OLD metric would false-reject (`<0.55`) and the new one passes. Suite **302 + 1 pre-existing red**. Controller-direct (small-clear-fix; review-framed but user OK'd the verified fix). The log's other error (SSE `CancelledError` via `sse_starlette cancel_on_finish`) is the cosmetic teardown noise from [[0039]] — needs server restart to deploy 0039's fix; not chased further.

## [0041] Cancel UI dead-end + dead-bus stream hang — close the cancellation gaps in [[0038]] (SHIPPED) — 2026-06-06 (Nggaev-v2)
**Fixed two FE/SSE gaps a second reviewer found in the shipped Cancel feature** (0038 was backend-sound; these are the client/stream halves). (1) Cancelling a *running* job dead-ended: the page kept the `cancelling` badge + frozen "Running" phase rows until manual reload, never reaching the `cancelled`/Resume panel. (2) Opening a fresh stream for an already-`cancelled`/`cancelling` job hung on a dead bus (leaked subscriber, self-heals only on disconnect).

**Root cause (traced + independently re-verified against live code):** cancellation publishes **no terminal SSE event** — `pipeline.run`'s `finally: events_bus.close()` (`pipeline.py:252-253`) tears the bus down DURING unwind, i.e. *before* `_execute_job`'s `except asyncio.CancelledError` runs `uncancel()` → `await asyncio.shield(_finalize())` → `mark_cancelled` commit (`worker.py:215-241`). So bus-close strictly precedes the `cancelled` DB commit. The naive "refetch getJob on stream close" fix has a **close-before-finalize race** (reviewer-caught): a single refetch fires before the commit and re-reads stale `cancelling`. The symmetric trap — publishing a `job_cancelled` event inside `mark_cancelled` — is dead-on-arrival because the bus is already closed.

**Fix (controller-direct TDD, small-verified-fix rule):** (1) FE `job.tsx` — **poll `getJob` to terminal** while `status === "cancelling"` (1.5s interval, stops on any non-`cancelling` status; race-free, also covers a mid-cancel reload landing). (2) API `jobs.py` `stream_job` — initial-state replay returns a **terminal `job_cancelled` event** for `cancelling`/`cancelled` instead of subscribing to a dead bus. RED→GREEN behavioral test (`tests/api/test_stream_cancelled.py`) drives the inner `event_gen` async generator (DB mocked) and asserts it yields `job_cancelled` and returns WITHOUT calling `events_bus.subscribe`. The 0039 AST no-yield-in-session guard still passes (new branch is a dict build, no yield). `tsc --noEmit` clean. Suite **304 + 1 pre-existing red**. Commit `9b87b51`. **⚠ restart server** to deploy. NOTE: backend cancel mechanics (0038) unchanged — this only closes the client-visible/stream gaps.

## [0042] Book grade derivation + silent-skip visibility — close the unarchived-job root cause (SHIPPED + live-verified) — 2026-06-06 (Nggaev-v2)
**Why a `done` job (`3b026cfb`, math-algebra) never reached Notion:** its book (`62865c70`, `7-sinf_Algebra…`) had **`grade = NULL`**. The archive keys the target page on `{subject}|{grade}` (`notion_archive.py:60`) and `_resolve_subject_page_id` short-circuits on a falsy grade (`:58-59`) → logs `no subject-page mapping … grade=None — skipping` and returns; job stays `done`, `notion_archived_at` NULL — the silent-skip mode ([[notion-subject-page-map]]), this time from a NULL grade not a missing map entry. The leak is the **upload** path (`books.py:100` `grade=Form(default=None)`); Fetch-from-Notion passes a grade. **Key verified fact:** `book.grade` is READ in exactly one place — the archive (`notion_archive.py:184`); `pipeline.py` references grade nowhere, and English CEFR is leveled by the model **from the PDF source** (`prompts.py:34-39`, static string), never from the column. So the NULL grade created **no wrong content** — it only blocked archiving. (Corrected an earlier reviewer CEFR-regen caveat: moot.)

**Fix (full brainstorm→spec→plan→inline-TDD pipeline; spec `e9cdeb2`, plan `9b0094f`; both reviewer-verified, 2 spec flags folded):** Commit 1 — `app/services/grade.py` `derive_grade_from_filename` (regex `(\d{1,2})\s*[-_ ]?\s*(?:sinf|klass|класс)`, clamp 1–11) + wired into the shared `ingest_pdf` chokepoint as `grade = grade or derive(...)` (explicit wins; dedup returns earlier) + **Alembic data-migration `0020`** backfilling `books.grade WHERE NULL/''` (regex inlined — frozen-snapshot best practice). Commit 2 — `homework_jobs.notion_skip_reason` column (`0021`, real `drop_column` downgrade) + `jobs_repo.set_notion_skip_reason` (+ `set_notion_archived` clears it) + `archive_job` stamps the reason on **resolvable** skips only (no-mapping/no-phases/missing-row), **committing in-block** (the first archive_job session is otherwise read-only → stamp would roll back — the load-bearing reviewer flag) and leaving disabled/api-key/idempotent returns untouched + `JobOut.notion_skip_reason` (optional → no stub regression) + job-page indicator. Plus `scripts/rearchive_unarchived.py` one-off sweep.

8 commits (T1 helper → T8 script), each TDD red→green, controller-run inline (manual-confirm gate on the 3 outward-facing steps). **LIVE-VERIFIED:** `alembic upgrade head` applied 0020+0021 (DB was exactly at `a7c1e9d2b4f8`, so only these two ran); backfill set algebra→7, english→8, **0 gradeless books remain**; sweep re-archived **all 6 done+unarchived jobs** (the 3 gradeless-after-backfill + the 3 graded backlog history|7/kimyo|8/kimyo|9) — **all 19 done jobs now `archived=t`, 0 skip-reasons** (history|7 resolved via the jahon-keyword dict match, so the hedged case pushed cleanly too). Suite **321 + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`). **⚠ restart server** to load new archive/JobOut code. Closes the NULL-grade arm of the silent-skip concern; Notion anchor auto-resolve still the bigger WISHLIST fix.

## [0043] Notion-fetch size cap 20→50MB, tied to the upload cap (SHIPPED) — 2026-06-06 (Nggaev-v2)
**Raised the "Fetch From Notion" textbook-size reject from 20MB → 50MB and tied it to the upload cap so the two can't drift.** The fetch path (`notion_fetch.download_textbook`) had a separate hardcoded `_TOC_MAX_BYTES = 20MB` ("Gemini TOC ceiling") while uploads accept 50MB (`settings.max_file_mb`) — an artificial mismatch: TOC extraction already handles >20MB via local pypdf text (drops the gemini attachment), so there was no reason to cap fetch below upload. Most >20MB books are big from IMAGES, not text, so this unlocks a large slice of the ~43% over-20MB corpus (per worklog [0033]) for fetch.

**Change:** removed the `_TOC_MAX_BYTES` constant; `download_textbook` now computes `max_bytes = settings.max_file_mb * 1024 * 1024` and rejects against it (dynamic — single source of truth with upload; raising `max_file_mb` moves both). Error messages now interpolate the actual cap. `TextbookTooLarge` docstring updated. Controller-direct TDD (CLAUDE.md small-clear exception): added `test_download_accepts_above_old_20mb_cap` (30MB now passes — red under old 20MB cap, green after) + retargeted the oversize test to `settings.max_file_mb + 5`; de-staled `test_from_notion_oversize_422` (20→50). Suite **322 + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`). Files: `app/services/notion_fetch.py`, `tests/services/test_notion_fetch.py`, `tests/api/test_from_notion.py`.

**Scope note (the cap is NOT "all books"):** raising the number only lets bigger files *in*. Two walls remain that a bigger cap can't move — **scanned/no-text-layer books** (need OCR/vision; the 497MB one is this) and **text-massive books** at generation (the per-lesson `extract_max_text_chars=600_000` gate → needs subset-TOC, `fetch-1`). Upload also reads the whole file into RAM (`await file.read()`), so >50MB giants need streaming-to-disk before the cap goes much higher. Logged separately. Also logged **WISHLIST `deadcode-1`**: the `source_map_*` plumbing (dropped in Effort-A [0028], `pipeline.py:177` hardcodes empty) is still threaded through ~10 functions — dead, rip out.

## [0044] Phantom gemini-3.5-flash removed + model-not-found → immediate failover (SHIPPED) — 2026-06-06 (Nggaev-v2)
**Killed a phantom model that was silently degrading every gemini job.** `gemini-3.5-flash` was in `MODEL_MANIFEST` (`agent_models.py`) so the UI offered it and `is_valid()` accepted it — but the gemini CLI rejects it: `ModelNotFoundError: Requested entity was not found`. **Verified live** two ways: a real job (`3b026cfb`, math-algebra) where every content phase on `gemini-3.5-flash` failed → leaned on failover to codex/claude (job still finished, but ~15–30s wasted per phase + log spam); and a direct CLI sweep of all 7 manifest gemini models (`gemini -m <m> -p "say ok"`) — **only `gemini-3.5-flash` returned MISSING**; the other 6 (`2.5-flash/-lite/-pro`, `3.1-pro-preview`, `3-flash-preview`, `3.1-flash-lite-preview`) all OK. So it's a phantom name, NOT a CLI-version issue (installed 0.45.0; latest 0.45.2 is a patch — model availability is server-side anyway).

**Two changes:** (1) removed `gemini-3.5-flash` from the manifest (one line; other 6 stay). (2) `failure_classifier` now classes model-not-found (`modelnotfounderror` / `requested entity was not found` / `model not found`) as **`"wall"`** → 0 same-provider retries → immediate failover, since a non-existent model can NEVER succeed on retry (the old `"hard"` path wasted one retry per phase). **Caught a cross-test trap** (90% bar): two existing tests used `"ModelNotFoundError"` as a generic *"hard"* placeholder (`test_failure_classifier.test_unknown_defaults_to_hard`, `test_failover_driver.test_failover_switches_provider_on_hard_failure`) — repointed both to `"malformed response envelope"` and added regression guards (`test_model_not_found_is_immediate_failover` in both unit + driver). Confirmed `classify`'s result only feeds `_SAME_RETRY_BUDGET` (no other side effect of "wall"). Controller-direct TDD (red→green). Suite **326 + 1 pre-existing red** (`test_notion_defaults_disabled`). Commit `55c05cc`. **NOT pushed** (per user). Files: `agent_models.py`, `failure_classifier.py` + 3 test files.

**Follow-up (not done):** the manifest has no guard against future phantom entries — it's a hand-maintained list never validated against the CLIs. A periodic "probe each manifest model against its CLI" check would prevent recurrence (WISHLIST-worthy if it bites again).

Reuses `--color-*` tokens.
