## [0047] Autonomous fleet Phase 0 — one DB + Dockerized worker (BUILT + container-accepted) — 2026-06-08 (feat/autonomous-fleet-engine)
**First phase of the autonomous generation fleet** (spec + plan under `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-06-*fleet*`). Built in an **ISOLATED git worktree `feat/autonomous-fleet-engine`** (off Nggaev-v2 @ `fcaffce`); per user the WHOLE engine is built+tested there and the worktree becomes the accepted copy — **NOT merged back**, so this worklog lives on the engine branch (no conflict with the parallel session's MASTER_MEMORY churn on Nggaev-v2). Goal: prove N standalone worker containers pull from ONE shared Postgres head, contention-safe.

**Reused, NOT rebuilt (verified against live code):** Dockerfile, `docker-compose.yml` worker service (scaled profile), `docker-entrypoint.sh` RUN_MIGRATIONS switch, `main.py:62` WORKER_CONCURRENCY=0 gate, `run_standalone`/`__main__`, `claim_next_job` FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED, and the continuous worker stuck-job sweep ([[0031]]). **Whole-job work-stealing, NO host-affinity** — our simplification over NETS (our phase outputs are markdown-in-DB, not media-on-disk, so any PC runs any job).

**Built (subagent-driven, controller stress-tested every commit = read diff + re-ran the proof):** T1 `tests/integration/test_claim_contention.py` — guarded (`RUN_DB_INTEGRATION=1`) real-DB proof that two concurrent claims never grab the same row (`6c3008b`); T2 `docker-compose.worker.yml` (remote-PC worker → head `DATABASE_URL`) + `docs/fleet/phase0-bringup.md` (`cde3375`); T3 `scripts/fleet_contention_smoke.py` — asserts **`attempts>0`** (durable), NOT `claimed_by` (the failure path clears it) (`5f225c7`).

**ACCEPTANCE — full container smoke PASSED:** built `class-homework-builder:fleet`, isolated docker network + throwaway pg, migrated, ran **2 worker containers** (distinct `hostname:pid` ids), seeded 4 jobs → **4/4 pulled (attempts>0)** → PASS. Deterministic contention-safety = T1 (`1 passed` on an isolated pg, re-run by the controller).

**3 REAL bugs the acceptance caught + fixed (the whole point of running it):** (1) **CRLF entrypoint** — Windows checkout (`core.autocrlf=true`) baked `docker-entrypoint.sh` with `\r` → `env: 'sh\r': not found` → containers never start. Fix: `.gitattributes` (`*.sh eol=lf`) + Dockerfile `sed -i 's/\r$//'` (`63cd23e`). (2) **smoke sys.path** — `python scripts/x.py` only puts `scripts/` on path → `ModuleNotFoundError: app`; self-bootstrap repo root onto sys.path (`63cd23e`). (3) **psutil missing from `uv.lock`** (present in `pyproject.toml` deps, absent from the lock) → `uv sync --frozen` never installs it → worker AND api crash `ModuleNotFoundError: psutil` (`proc_tree.kill_tree`, the [[0038]] cancel feature). Regenerated `uv.lock` via the uv docker image (`dca008a`). **⚠ THIS ALSO BREAKS THE PRODUCTION IMAGE ON Nggaev-v2** — the dev venv had psutil installed directly, masking the lock drift; fix on main too before any container deploy.

**Op lessons:** integration tests need an ISOLATED throwaway DB (the shared `edu-postgres` has a live worker that races seeded jobs → false failures); the worktree has no `.venv` (run tests via the MAIN repo venv python with cwd = worktree — conftest puts repo root on path). **Next: Phase 1 — `workers` registry table + head-side liveness view** (the reclaim engine already shipped in [[0031]], so Phase 1 is small).

## [0048] Autonomous fleet Phase 1 — workers registry + liveness (BUILT + container-accepted) — 2026-06-08 (feat/autonomous-fleet-engine)
**Gave the head a live fleet registry — which worker PCs are up** (continues [[0047]] on the same isolated worktree). Built subagent-driven, controller stress-tested EVERY commit (read diff + re-ran): **T1** `workers` table + `WorkerNode` model + migration `0022` (rev `d5e9f1a2b3c4`) (`0a47f80`); **T2** `app/repositories/workers.py` — `is_online` pure helper (4 DB-free unit tests) + `upsert_heartbeat` (Postgres `on_conflict_do_update`) + `list_with_liveness` (`aee14b5`); **T3** worker registers + heartbeats on a **DEDICATED `asyncio` task** (`81ff3e7`); **T4** `GET /api/v1/workers` liveness endpoint (`83a764e`). New setting `worker_registry_stale_seconds=90` (online = beat within window); workers beat every `heartbeat_seconds` (30s). **Reclaim/self-healing NOT rebuilt — already shipped [[0031]]; no host-affinity (whole-job model).**

**🔴 Design bug caught IN REVIEW + fixed (the load-bearing lesson):** my first plan put the registry beat at the main-loop top — but the loop BLOCKS at `_wait_for_slot_or_stop` (`worker.py:107`) whenever all slots are busy, and fleet lessons run MINUTES, so a fully-busy worker would beat-starve → get falsely marked **offline exactly when hardest at work** (inverse of correct liveness, and the steady state for a long-job fleet). I VERIFIED the claim against `worker.py:96-130` before accepting. Fix: beat on its OWN `asyncio.create_task(_registry_heartbeat_loop())`, mirroring the per-job `_heartbeat` (`:203`); cancelled+awaited in `run()`'s finally (matches `_execute_job:257-259`). Regression test `test_busy_worker_still_heartbeats` holds ALL slots + asserts still-online.

**T4 subagent EXCEEDED scope (controller-verified + accepted, not trusted):** it also (a) fixed a latent `w.id`→`pc` test-cleanup bug (a T3 transcription error, dormant since the endpoint test wasn't run in T3), (b) added a defensive `Authorization: Bearer 123` header (works auth-on-or-off), (c) set `pyproject` `asyncio_default_{fixture,test}_loop_scope="session"` so the Worker-task test can't corrupt the module-global async engine pool across tests. All three re-verified non-regressing.

**ACCEPTANCE — container smoke PASSED:** API container (`WORKER_CONCURRENCY=0`, `WORKER_REGISTRY_STALE_SECONDS=10`, auth off) + **2 worker containers** (`HEARTBEAT_SECONDS=3`) on an isolated network → `GET /api/v1/workers` = `total=2 online=2`; killed one, waited 12s → `total=2 online=1` (killed one `online:false`, survivor `online:true`). Real end-to-end liveness incl. the kill→stale→offline transition.

**Worktree DB-free baseline: `5 failed / 329 passed / 4 skipped`** — the 5 failures are PRE-EXISTING Notion tests (the fresh worktree has no `.env` → Notion 503s), unrelated to fleet code; 4 skipped = the guarded integration tests (Phase 0 contention + 3 Phase 1) which need `RUN_DB_INTEGRATION=1`. Commits `0a47f80`·`aee14b5`·`81ff3e7`·`83a764e` (+ plan `a2e4371`). **Next: Phase 2 — `batches` table + the fallible Notion→TOC lesson resolver + batch launcher.**

## [0049] Autonomous fleet Phase 0.5 — clock-skew hardening (BUILT + container-accepted) — 2026-06-08 (feat/autonomous-fleet-engine)
**Moved every worker-timing comparison off each host's `datetime.now()` onto the single head-DB clock (`func.now()`)** — a multi-PC fleet compares its host clock against the head DB's timestamps, so clock skew was uneven/delayed claiming + false liveness. Surfaced by the [[0047]]/[[0048]] review, VERIFIED against code (7 spots), planned (`8242829`+`701b211`+`f804923`), executed subagent-driven, controller stress-tested EVERY commit (read diff + re-ran the real-DB proof). **Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs/2026-06-06-autonomous-fleet-design.md §6,plans/2026-06-08-fleet-phase0.5-clock-skew.md}`.**

**The 7 spots, all → DB clock (`app/repositories/`):** **jobs.py (T1, `0f39f82`)** — (1) `claim_next_job` filter `scheduled_at <= func.now()` + stamps (`claimed_at`/`last_attempt_at`/`started_at`) `func.now()`; (2) `touch_claim`; (3) `reclaim_stuck_jobs` cutoff; (4) `mark_failed_with_retry` retry-branch backoff `func.now() + make_interval`; (5) `queue_depth` filter; (6) `reclaim_stale_cancelling` cutoff. **workers.py (T2, `47b9a22`)** — (7) `upsert_heartbeat` stamps `func.now()` + `is_online` gains an injected `now=` + `list_with_liveness` fetches `db_now = SELECT now()` and injects it (keeps the pure helper + its 4 unit tests, routes both sides of the freshness compare through the DB clock). **Record-only `completed_at` writes (`jobs.py` terminal-fail/cancel/mark_cancelled/reclaim_stale_cancelling) LEFT as `datetime.now()`** — no comparison depends on them; keeps the diff focused. Dropped now-unused `timedelta` (jobs) + dead `_utcnow`/`timezone` (workers).

**🔴 Decision-1 bug caught by the reviewer + VERIFIED empirically before changing:** the plan first used `func.make_interval(secs=N)` — but that raises `TypeError: Function.__init__() got an unexpected keyword argument 'secs'` AT CONSTRUCTION (SQLAlchemy's `func.X(key=value)` does NOT emit PG's `name => value` syntax). Reproduced it + the fix in-venv: **positional `func.make_interval(0,0,0,0,0,0,N)`** (N=7th arg=seconds) compiles to `make_interval(0,0,0,0,0,0,:p)`, binds N as a param, runs on PG. Used in spots 3/4/6. (Supersedes the spec §6 `text("interval '…'")` caveat — our intervals are DYNAMIC ints, not constants.)

**T3 (`8927084`) removed the [[0047]] T1 past-pinning crutch:** the source fix makes the un-pinned `test_claim_contention.py` deterministic, so the un-pinned form IS now the skew regression guard. **Gate — real-DB proof (the unit suite is DB-free, can't catch bad interval SQL):** new `tests/integration/test_clock_skew.py` (4 tests) — un-pinned just-scheduled job immediately claimable (the canonical skew symptom, **10/10 deterministic**), every converted interval query runs on PG, retry backoff schedules server-side-future, heartbeat DB-stamped + online. **ACCEPTANCE — container smoke PASSED:** rebuilt `class-homework-builder:fleet` with the new code, isolated network + throwaway PG, **2 worker containers** → contention smoke `4/4 jobs pulled (attempts>0)` via the new `func.now()` claim path, AND `list_with_liveness` reported **BOTH** containers (`4f5f9764bcc8:1`+`d6bb527eff73:1`) registered + `online=True` (spot-7 end-to-end, a cleaner 2-worker proof than the smoke's transient `claimed_by` sample). **Worktree DB-free baseline `5 failed (pre-existing Notion)/329 passed/8 skipped`** (+1 skip vs 0048 = the new integration file). **Severity: robustness, not blocking** — the dev-box ±0.7s was Docker-Desktop VM drift; an NTP fleet is <100ms. **Next: Phase 2 — `batches` table + the fallible Notion→TOC lesson resolver + batch launcher.**

## [0050] Autonomous fleet Phase 2 — batch automation (BUILT + container-accepted) — 2026-06-08 (feat/autonomous-fleet-engine)
**One `POST /jobs/batch` fans out one generation job per textbook lesson into the shared queue, tracked by a `batches` row with a drift-free, computed-on-read rollup.** Brainstormed→spec (5-round reviewed)→plan→subagent-driven build, controller stress-tested EVERY commit (read diff + re-ran). **Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs/2026-06-08-fleet-phase2-batch-automation-design.md,plans/2026-06-08-fleet-phase2-batch-automation.md}`.** Commits `e02e700`(T0)·`0866767`(T1)·`a21395f`(T2)·`74e5dda`(T3)·`d50b18c`(T4).

**Big design decision: FAN-OUT ONLY** (vs full-auto / hybrid). The batch is a **label + a tally** over jobs; it only ever sees an already-`toc_ready` book. The fragile download→ingest→TOC-extraction lifecycle (+ its first-class failure handling) STAYS owned by the existing `POST /books/from-notion`→`ingest_pdf`→`toc_extractor` flow — re-wrapping it was rejected as duplicating a working lifecycle. **Narrows master spec §5** (explicit, intentional deviation, edited in place). UI = Phase 3; `generation_mode` cli/api + Gemini-API provider = Phase 4 (so a batch just carries a `provider`/`model`, default **`claude`** — fleet cli-first, diverges from `/generate`'s gemini default).

**Schema (T1, migration `0023`/rev `a1b2c3d4e5f6`):** `batches` table with **`UNIQUE(book_id)`** (→ one logical batch per textbook, makes find-or-create race-safe + adoption unambiguous) — immutable facts only (book/subject/grade/provider/model/notion_source), **NO status counters**; nullable `homework_jobs.batch_id` FK + index. **Repo (T3, `app/repositories/batches.py`):** `get_or_create_for_book` (Postgres `ON CONFLICT(book_id) DO UPDATE … RETURNING id`); `rollup_for_batch` = **per-lesson-latest** (`DISTINCT ON (toc_entry_id) … WHERE batch_id ORDER BY created_at DESC` then GROUP BY status — mirrors `jobs.latest_by_section:181/193` scoped to batch_id) so a retried lesson (old failed + new pending, both batch_id=X) counts ONCE → tally always reconciles to the lesson count. **Denominator = sum(tally), derived on read — fully counter-free** (dropped an interim `lessons_targeted` field in review: it would drift on top-up/adoption).

**Endpoint (T4, `app/api/v1/batch.py`):** `POST /jobs/batch {book_id, toc_entry_ids?(=all), provider?, model?, force?}` + `GET /jobs/batches[/{id}]`. **Readiness guard** (the load-bearing precondition): `toc_ready`→fan out; `uploading`/`toc_extracting`→409 retry; `failed`→409 + surface `error_message`; empty `list_for_book`→422 (uses the REAL statuses — **T0 (`e02e700`) killed the fake `status="ready"` test-seed value** in 2 integration seeds, a copy-paste trap for this guard). **Reconcile in ONE atomic transaction:** reuse advisory lock + `find_active_for_section` (dedups pending/running/done); per lesson → active+`batch_id IS NULL`→**adopt** (no poaching; `UNIQUE(book_id)` makes it unambiguous), active+this-batch→skip, none/`force`→**create** tagged batch_id. `provider`/`model` are per-job authoritative; the batch row's are a launch-default label.

**🟡 Gotcha caught while writing the plan (real, handled):** the `UUIDPK`/`Timestamps` mixins use **Python-side** defaults (`default=uuid4`/`_utcnow`) that fire only in the ORM unit-of-work — a Core `pg_insert` (the `ON CONFLICT` path) does NOT trigger them, so `get_or_create_for_book` supplies `id=uuid4()`+`created_at`/`updated_at`=`func.now()` explicitly (would silently crash the repo otherwise). Also verified `expire_on_commit=False` (so building the response after `commit()` is greenlet-safe). Both verified against `base.py`/`db.py` before writing.

**Gate — guarded real-DB tests (the DB-free suite can't catch the DISTINCT-ON rollup / ON-CONFLICT / guard SQL):** `tests/integration/{test_batches_schema,test_batches_repo,test_batches}.py` — schema (UNIQUE + FK), repo (idempotent-per-book + rollup reconciles to 3 not 4), **endpoint cases 1–7** (happy fan-out, guard matrix, subset+foreign-toc 422, **re-launch reconciles to 5 not 7**, **adopt orphan**, **concurrent race → exactly 1 batch + 5 jobs** [3/3 deterministic], force) + a DB-free validation unit test. **ACCEPTANCE — container smoke PASSED:** rebuilt the image with Phase 2 code, networked throwaway PG + API container (`WORKER_CONCURRENCY=0`) + worker container → `POST /api/v1/jobs/batch` returned `jobs_created=4` provider=claude, the worker pulled **4/4** (attempts>0), `GET /api/v1/jobs/batches/{id}` rollup reconciled to `lessons_covered=4` `complete=true` (jobs `failed` for lack of a CLI in the image — expected; proves batch→queue→drained→rollup end-to-end + the image builds/runs with the new code). **Worktree DB-free baseline `5 failed (pre-existing Notion)/330 passed/18 skipped`** (+1 pass = validation test; skips = the guarded integration files). **Next: Phase 3 — fleet dashboard (batch funnel + rollups, PC liveness cards, launch/pause/cancel/retry/drain, Notion→TOC resolver UI).**

## [0051] Autonomous fleet Phase 3 — operations dashboard (BUILT + acceptance-passed) — 2026-06-08 (feat/autonomous-fleet-engine)
**A new `/fleet` operator screen: prepare a Notion subject → launch it as a batch → watch per-status rollups + PC liveness → drill into a batch's lessons with per-lesson Cancel/Retry/Open.** Brainstormed (visual companion, themed mockup)→spec→plan→subagent-driven build, controller stress-tested EVERY commit (read diff + re-ran the gate). **Scope = BARE MINIMUM (user-locked).** Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs/2026-06-08-fleet-phase3-dashboard-design.md,plans/2026-06-08-fleet-phase3-dashboard.md}`. Commits `b95689d`(T1)·`4f7a6c9`(T2)·`4c6cdf8`(T3)·`57f01d0`(T4)·`a734ca5`(T5 subset)·`5a7e28d`(route-order fix).

**Pure frontend + exactly ONE new backend read endpoint.** **T1 (`b95689d`):** `GET /jobs/batches/{batch_id}/jobs` — per-lesson-latest drill-in (`batches_repo.list_jobs`: same `DISTINCT ON (toc_entry_id) … ORDER BY created_at DESC` as `rollup_for_batch`, joined to `TOCEntry` for `section_title`/`order_index`, ordered by order_index), so **row count == the rollup denominator** (funnel + drill-in can never disagree); 404 on unknown batch; guarded real-DB test asserts one-row-per-lesson + shows the NEWEST job (a pending retry over the old failed one). **T2 (`4f7a6c9`):** FE data layer — types (`Worker`/`BatchSummary`/`BatchRollup`/`BatchLessonRow`), 5 `api` methods (`listBatches`/`getBatch`/`batchJobs`/`launchBatch`/`listWorkers`), `/fleet` route + `Rocket` nav pill (+`/fleet` in `layout` wide set), react-query scaffold polling batches/workers/books @ 3500ms. **T3 (`4c6cdf8`):** `WorkerCards` (online-dot + `ago()` relative heartbeat, `online N/total`) + `FleetLauncher` — Prepare form mirrors `upload.tsx` (grade `Select` → subject `Select`, **digits-only grade `title.replace(/\D/g,"")`**, `fetchBookFromNotion`, non-blocking: toast + invalidate `["books"]`, extraction ~1–3 min) and a **server-derived tray** (`toc_extracting`/`uploading`→preparing · `toc_ready` AND book_id ∉ any batch→ready · `failed`→error); each ready row is its own `ReadyRow` fetching `getBook` for the lesson count + a provider `Select` (from `getAgentModels`, default claude) + Launch. **T4 (`57f01d0`):** `RollupBar` iterates `STATUS_ORDER` filtered to non-zero (so **cancelled/cancelling always surface** — no hardcoded 4-set; shared `status.ts` color map green/blue/amber/faint/muted/red), `BatchFunnel` (one `CARD` per `BatchSummary`, `subjectLabel`·grade, complete chip, expand toggle), `BatchLessonList` (drill-in **gated by `enabled`** so the query only fires when a card is open; Cancel shown for pending/running, Retry for failed, Open→`/job/{id}`; reuses existing `cancelJob`/`retryJob`). **T5 (`a734ca5`):** optional lesson-subset launch — `ReadyRow` "Choose lessons" toggle renders checkboxes from the already-fetched `detail.data.toc` (NOT `BatchLessonList`/`batchJobs` — a not-yet-launched book has no batch; controller corrected the plan's reuse suggestion); `subset = choosing && selected.size>0` sends `toc_entry_ids`, else launch-all stays the default/primary path.

**🔴 REAL bug acceptance caught + fixed (`5a7e28d`):** `GET /jobs/batches` (plural list, the funnel's data source) was **shadowed by jobs' dynamic `GET /jobs/{job_id}`** — FastAPI matches in include order and `jobs.router` was registered before `batch.router`, so `/jobs/batches` parsed "batches" as a `job_id` → **422**. A latent Phase-2 bug (no test ever hit the plural list); **Phase 3's funnel is its first real consumer.** Fix: register the static-prefixed `batch.router` BEFORE `jobs.router` in `app/api/v1/__init__.py` (specific-before-dynamic) + regression test `test_batches_list_not_shadowed_by_job_route`. (Drill-in `/jobs/batches/{id}/jobs` and single `/jobs/batches/{id}` are 2-segment so they never collided — only the 1-segment plural did. Reverse-shadow checked: moving batch first can't swallow any `/jobs/{job_id}[/stream|/download|...]` — segment counts/literals differ, a real `/jobs/<uuid>/…` still falls through to jobs.router.) **Never reached prod — no backport:** the batch routes exist ONLY in this unmerged fleet worktree; `Nggaev-v2`/main has no `/jobs/batches*` at all, so the bug was latent-but-unreachable there and is fixed at the exact point it became reachable.

**ACCEPTANCE — in-process real-DB endpoint click-through PASSED** (the dashboard touches NO generation path, so a CLI smoke buys nothing; the right proof is the exact HTTP path the FE renders). Threw a throwaway script driving `main.app` via httpx ASGITransport on the migrated PG:5436: `GET /workers` (shape) → `POST /jobs/batch` (`jobs_created=5`, rollup pending=5) → `GET /jobs/batches` (funnel summary `lessons_covered=5`, complete=false) → `GET /jobs/batches/{id}/jobs` (5 rows, ordered, carry section_title/attempts/job_id) → `POST /jobs/{id}/cancel` → drill-in reflects `cancelled` and rollup is **per-lesson-latest `{cancelled:1, pending:4}`** (sum still 5) → **subset** `POST /jobs/batch {toc_entry_ids:[0,2]}` → exactly 2 jobs, drill-in shows lessons [0,2]. **17/17 checks PASS** (script deleted after). **Gate:** full integration suite **20 passed** real-DB (incl. the +1 route regression); DB-free baseline `5 failed (pre-existing Notion)/330 passed/20 skipped` (no new failures from the reorder — notion router has a distinct prefix); FE `tsc --noEmit` clean + `npm run build` ✓ on every FE commit. **Deferred (in WISHLIST `fleet-ctrl/ui/api/infra-*`):** batch-level cancel/retry, pause/resume (budget-saver), drain, live SSE, historical view, Phase-4 cost. **Worktree NOT merged** (becomes the accepted copy; user owns merge/cleanup). ⚠ still-open: `uv.lock`/psutil fix on `Nggaev-v2` before any prod container deploy [[0047]]. **Next: Phase 4 — Gemini-API provider + credential-pool + cost-ledger (own effort), or merge the fleet worktree.**

## [0052] R14 — CBP prompt self-conflict fix (universal first-pass MAJOR regen eliminated) — 2026-06-10 (feat/autonomous-fleet-engine)
**Tracked + fixed the case-based-preview regen tax: EVERY job's first CBP attempt failed the judge with a MAJOR (observed 5/5 models — claude Sonnet baseline + all 4 gemini tiers — on the same England lesson), forcing +1 content call +1 Opus judge call per lesson.** Controller-direct small fix (root cause verified before code, per CLAUDE.md exception). Commits: `e9f1f75` (R14 roadmap entry), `f5c4400` (prompt fix).

**Root cause (verified from judge first-pass verdicts in `agent_usages.raw_envelope`):** the live CBP prompt (`prompts/_general/case-based-preview.md`; subject files DEAD — `USE_SUBJECT_PROMPTS=False`, `prompts.py:8`) conflicted with itself. Self-check #6 ("concept NOT named in setup/checkpoints") vs the visual rule demanding a **"self-sufficient"** setup visual with **"every label/value/axis"** — for cause-identification cases the complete visual IS the tested causal chain. All 5 failures cited #6; **3 of 5 leaked via the visual caption** (3.1-pro's diagram mapped poll-tax→revolt = CP1's answer; 3-flash's caption named CP2's answer; claude wrote a "core concept" meta-line). Secondary: the DPE "not auto-passed" sentence (buried mid-paragraph) was dropped by 3/4 gemini models (minor warnings).

**Fix — 3 contract edits + 1 follow-up:** (1) **setup-visual no-spoiler rule** — the caption is PART of the setup, depict scene only (place/actors/time), never the tested cause→effect; explicitly overrides label-completeness for the setup visual; (2) **narrative symptoms-not-diagnosis** rule in the canonical-structure block (added after first smoke showed 2.5-pro leaking via prose instead of the now-clean visual); (3) **DPE required closing line** (must END with the not-auto-passed note) + self-check #6 extended to captions + new self-check #9. Judge reads the same contract → enforcement is now explicit on both sides.

**PROOF (in-process real-CLI smoke, throwaway script, deleted after):** real England-lesson extract from edu_copy → generate CBP → judge once (opus-4.7), per model. Iteration 1 (visual rule only): 3.1-pro **FIRST-PASS PASS 0 warnings**, 2.5-pro still MAJOR via narrative prose → added edit (2). Iteration 2: **both gemini-3.1-pro-preview AND gemini-2.5-pro FIRST-PASS PASS with 0 warnings** (before: 5/5 MAJOR + recurring DPE minors). Output sanity-skimmed: setup states symptoms ("angered by recent policy"), CP1 genuinely asks the cause. Gate: `test_prompt_coverage + test_prompts_resolver + test_phase_judge` = **89 passed**. Payoff at mass-gen: ~1 content + ~1 Opus call saved per lesson (~100% of lessons paid the tax). Residual: 2-model/1-lesson sample — weaker tiers may still occasionally leak via prose; the judge+regen gate remains the backstop.

## [0053] Autonomous fleet Phase 4 — CLI | API transport toggle (SHIPPED + in-band acceptance; billed smokes operator-run) — 2026-06-11 (feat/autonomous-fleet-engine)
**A per-job (and per-batch) `transport` toggle — `cli` (today's subscription auth, $0 marginal, default) or `api` (pay-per-token, claude+gemini only) — so the operator can empirically benchmark CLI-vs-API for mass generation.** Brainstormed→spec (user-locked 2026-06-10, amended 2026-06-11)→plan (TDD-per-task)→subagent-driven build, controller stress-tested EVERY commit (read diff + re-ran the gate). Ships the **real-time toggle only**; batch-discount transport stays deferred (`fleet-api-1`). **Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs/2026-06-10-fleet-phase4-transport-toggle-design.md,plans/2026-06-11-fleet-phase4-transport-toggle.md}`.** Commits `49e2670`(T1)·`dfa3973`(T2)·`0468d62`(T3)·`7317280`(T4)·`aeb5fd8`(T5)·`21e799e`(T6)·`a200c20`(T7), all merged+pushed.

**What shipped (7 tasks):** **T1** — `transport` column on `homework_jobs`/`batches` + `auth_mode` on `agent_usages` (migration 0024, `server_default='cli'` covers existing rows), **batch unique key `(book_id)` → `(book_id, transport)`** (`uq_batches_book_id_transport`) so a same-book cli-vs-api re-launch forks a fresh batch instead of blending the rollup. **T2** — `agent._auth_env(provider, transport, base_env)`: cli = scrub both provider keys + gemini `GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_GCA=true`; api = inject exactly the active provider's key with a **loud `RuntimeError` on missing** (an empty env var is falsy → claude would silently OAuth-fall-back while the row says `auth_mode=api`); threaded through `_spawn` + all 4 call sites + the wrapper fns. **T3** — `validate_transport` + `api_supported` (claude/gemini only) at `POST /generate` + `POST /jobs/batch` (api requires an explicit model — no `model=None` divergence between OAuth/key); `get_or_create_for_book` conflict target `(book_id, transport)`; **§9a transport-scoped `find_active_for_section` dedup** (an api batch over a cli-generated book creates N fresh api jobs, doesn't skip — without this the flagship benchmark silently no-ops with zero jobs). **T4** — `transport` threaded through pipeline (content/extract-pin/regen/judge); **api failover restricted to the requested provider only** (verified gap: a transport-blind chain wastes non-api legs on the loud RuntimeError AND lets api-claude→api-gemini `model=None` under-report `$0`); **judge auth/401 on an api job fails the job loudly** (both initial + post-regen judge) instead of degrading to `judge-unavailable, passed=True`. **T5** — worker **fail-fast claim gate**: `claim_next_job` gates `transport=api` on a startup-computed `has_api_keys` (`WHERE transport='cli' OR :has_api_keys`; both `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`+`GEMINI_API_KEY` required — an api job touches content+gemini-extract+claude-judge); startup warnings incl. the gemini `selectedType` re-persist guard. **T6** — `auth_mode` recorded on every usage row (the cross-job `<cache>` reuse row correctly stays `cli`, on purpose); **`app/services/pricing.py`** static `(provider,model)→$/Mtok` map (**Claude VERIFIED $5/$25 opus** not the deprecated $15/$75; **gemini best-effort, tagged `VERIFY`**); `cost_usd(provider, None)` resolves to the provider default-model price (never silent `$0`); `/agent/stats` per-transport `$` rollup. **T7** — FE `CLI|API` segmented toggle (claude/gemini only, forces a concrete model on api, defaults back to cli on unsupported provider), transport-aware launcher "ready" gate (a book with a cli batch stays launchable on api), `api` badge, per-transport `$` on `/usage`.

**Key design decisions:** (1) **cli is the unconditional baseline for EVERY spawn — job or not** (default `"cli"` at every layer), because once `selectedType` is removed from `~/.gemini/settings.json` headless gemini has no persisted auth → book-upload TOC extraction (no job) must still get GCA via the env var; `transport=api` is the only deviation. (2) **batch key `(book_id, transport)` + §9a transport-scoped dedup** — the benchmark is launching the SAME book on cli then api; a transport-blind key/dedup would collapse both into one row / skip every lesson. (3) **api = requested-provider-only failover** — no cross-provider `model=None` leg → explicit-model rule preserved, cost stays attributable, `$0`-pricing path eliminated at the source; same-provider retry budget still gives resilience. (4) **loud judge auth-fail on api** — a silent judge 401 would ship unjudged content with no error. (5) **`has_api_keys` claim gate** — a half-configured worker never claims api jobs (covers the extract-failover path too, at claim time). (6) **verified-claude / flagged-gemini pricing** — stale prices silently corrupt the readout this feature rides on, so gemini stays `VERIFY` until the operator confirms.

**IN-BAND ACCEPTANCE (all free, recorded):** Full DB-free suite **`5 failed (pre-existing Notion-only) / 362 passed / 35 skipped`** (no new reds). Phase-4 units `tests/services/{test_auth_env,test_failover_api,test_judge_auth,test_execute_phase_api_auth,test_pricing}.py` = **25 passed**. Real-DB `tests/integration/{test_transport_schema,test_claim_contention}.py` + `tests/api/test_transport_validation.py` (against migrated PG `edu_copy:5436`) = **19 passed**. **Free invalid-key 401 proof (spec §7.1 partial, ZERO cost):** an in-process `agent.run_phase(provider="claude", model="claude-opus-4-8", transport="api", ...)` with a deliberately invalid `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="sk-ant-invalid-deadbeef"` (DB write stubbed) → the claude CLI returned `{"is_error":true,"api_error_status":401,...,"result":"Invalid API key · Fix external API key","total_cost_usd":0,"usage":{...,"output_tokens":0}}`, surfaced as `RuntimeError: 'phase.run __smoke__: claude CLI exited rc=1 :: Invalid API key · Fix external API key'`. Proves the api adapter injects the key directly → the call HITS the API, returns 401, **NO subscription fallback, ZERO spend, fails closed**. (Network + working claude CLI were available; the proof ran clean — not deferred.)

**DEFERRED to operator (billed, real-PC):** `docs/runbooks/phase4-transport-operator-acceptance.md` documents §7.1 live valid-key smokes (claude+gemini bill the right account, envelope carries token stats in api mode, gemini api auth actually selected at runtime), the **§3 deployment ordering** (ship GCA-injecting code BEFORE removing `selectedType` on a PC — removal-first instantly breaks gemini), §7.2 post-removal upload smoke (cli baseline), §7.5 end-to-end api attribution (every non-`<cache>` row `auth_mode=api`, nonzero `$`; `<cache>` reuse rows correctly stay `cli` per §9b; use a fresh-extract book for a clean cost benchmark), worker env setup, and the **⚠️ gemini-price `VERIFY` flag**. Further deferrals in WISHLIST: `fleet-api-1` batch transport · `-2` credential rotation pool · `-3` cost ledger + kill-switch · `-4` never-pay-twice idempotency · `-5` codex API mode. **Next: operator runs the billed gates + verifies gemini prices, or the fleet effort moves to one of the `fleet-api-*` / `fleet-ctrl-*` deferrals.**

## [0054] Autonomous fleet Phase 4.1 — per-role transports (extract/judge) (SHIPPED + in-band acceptance incl. LIVE mixed-billing smoke; billed §9.4 leg operator-run) — 2026-06-11 (feat/autonomous-fleet-engine)
**Per-job (and per-batch) `extract_transport` / `judge_transport` overrides (`cli|api|inherit`, default `inherit`) so e.g. gemini-api content can pair with a subscription (cli) claude judge — the `fleet-api-7` user request.** Phase 4's all-spawns-one-transport rule is replaced by `resolve_role_transport(role_value, job_transport)` (`agent_models.py`): inherit follows the job, explicit wins; content always follows the job-level `transport`. Per-row `auth_mode` attribution (Phase 4 T6) is what keeps a mixed job's `$` readout honest. **Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs/2026-06-11-fleet-phase4.1-per-role-transport-design.md,plans/2026-06-11-fleet-phase4.1-per-role-transport.md}`.** Commits `ca770b5`(T1)·`87a7cf6`(T2)·`9755886`(T3)·`e750ac0`(T4)·`4a5b475`(T5)·`5e97ddf`(T6)·T7=this docs commit.

**What shipped (7 tasks):** **T1** — `extract_transport`/`judge_transport` columns on `homework_jobs` + `batches` (migration 0025, `server_default='inherit'` covers existing rows). **T2 (§5a)** — **typed `AuthEnvError`** (subclass raised by `agent._auth_env` on missing/half credentials) + isinstance-based auth-classification in the judge/pipeline guards — credential mispredictions are loud **by construction**, no string-matching on error text. **T3** — validation + persistence of both role fields at `POST /generate` + `POST /jobs/batch` (same `api_supported` rules per role) + `resolve_role_transport`. **T4 (§4/§4a)** — **claim gate v2**: `has_api_keys` → `worker._compute_capabilities`/`CAPABILITIES` (per-side `can_claude_api`/`can_gemini_api` + role-level `judge_api_ok`/`judge_fallback_api_ok`/`extract_api_ok`); `claim_next_job` ANDs each job's RESOLVED role transports against them, so a gemini-only worker claims api-content-gemini + cli-judge jobs (no Anthropic key needed) and a worker missing a needed side never claims (fail-fast at claim time, covers extract failover); per-side startup warnings replace the all-or-nothing one. **§4a**: jobs generating ON the configured judge pair are judged by `model_tiers._SELF_FALLBACK` → the gate checks THAT provider's capability for exactly those jobs. **T5** — pipeline resolves both role transports once per job and threads them to every spawn (content/extract-pin/judge/regen); the regen/judge guard fails the phase loudly when `content==api OR judge==api` and the error is auth-classified. **T6** — FE Extract/Judge billing selects (Auto/CLI/API) beside the Phase-4 transport toggle, threaded through generate + batch payloads.

**Review story (the §4a/§5a amendments + an empirical SQL find):** the spec was amended twice during review — **§5a** replaced "match the RuntimeError message" with the typed `AuthEnvError` (isinstance, not string fragility), and **§4a** added the judge **self-fallback branch** the first gate draft missed (a job generating on the judge pair isn't judged by the judge pair — gating those jobs on `judge_api_ok` was wrong in both directions). The deepest find was **empirical, not theoretical**: the gate's judge-pair equality `model == :judge_model` with a **`model=NULL` job** evaluates to SQL `NULL`, and `NOT(NULL AND …)` stays `NULL` — so NULL-model jobs whose provider equals the judge provider were **silently unclaimable**. Proven against real Postgres (`test_null_model_job_claims_via_not_pair_branch`), fixed with `coalesce(model, '')`; safe today because `judge_model_for` resolves `None` via `default_model(provider)` (sonnet ≠ the opus judge pair), and the worker now **warns at startup if `JUDGE_MODEL == default_model(JUDGE_PROVIDER)`** (the config that would re-open the edge). Also fixed in T7: stale `HAS_API_KEYS` comment in `config.py` → `CAPABILITIES`.

**IN-BAND ACCEPTANCE (recorded verbatim, 2026-06-11):** Full DB-free suite **`394 passed, 46 skipped in 20.71s`** (0 failed — the 5 Notion reds of 0053 are gone). Real-DB `tests/integration/{test_transport_schema,test_claim_contention}.py` + `tests/api/test_transport_validation.py` (migrated PG `edu_copy:5436`) = **`34 passed in 4.86s`**. **LIVE mixed-billing smoke (§9.3 — the user's exact case, job api + roles cli, NO `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in env):** in-process real spawns — (a) content `run_phase_prompt(provider="gemini", model="gemini-2.5-flash", transport="api")` → Vertex-billed sentence about osmosis, `prompt=19963 output=1031`; (b) judge-shaped `run_phase(provider="claude", model="claude-sonnet-4-6", transport="cli", operation="judge:__smoke41__")` → `ok` on the subscription. `agent_usages` rows: **claude `auth_mode=cli, success=True`** + **gemini `auth_mode=api, success=True`** — mixed per-row attribution proven end-to-end. **Environmental find during the smoke:** this Mac's `~/.gemini/settings.json` had re-persisted `security.auth.selectedType: oauth-personal`, which on gemini-cli 0.46.0 **overrides** the `GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI=true` env signal → the first api spawn failed LOUD (`Cloud Code Private API has not been used…`, recorded `auth_mode=api, success=False` — no silent wrong-account billing); re-run with an isolated `HOME` succeeded. Confirms the §3 selectedType rule + the startup guard matter on real hosts; documented in the runbook.

**DEFERRED to operator (billed):** the **§9.4 end-to-end leg** — one opus-content + `judge_transport=api` job on a both-sides-credentialed worker (needs an `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`): judge rows `auth_mode=api` with nonzero `$`, and a gemini-only worker never claims it. Gate-refusal is already covered by the T4 real-DB claim matrix; loud-`AuthEnvError` by the T2/T5 unit tests. Runbook updated (`docs/runbooks/phase4-transport-operator-acceptance.md`, "Phase 4.1" section): inherit/cli/api semantics per role, capability routing, the §9.4 leg, the selectedType-vs-Vertex live finding, and the **⚠️ reviewer note: role transports are NOT part of the dedup natural key (spec §8) — re-running the same lessons with different role billing requires `force=true`**, or the new billing choice is silently ignored. `fleet-api-7` struck in WISHLIST. **Next: operator runs §9.4 (+ the still-open Phase-4 billed gates), or the fleet effort moves to the remaining `fleet-api-*` / `fleet-ctrl-*` deferrals.**

## [0058] Fix — homework download 401 on live completion — 2026-06-12 (PR #5 → Nggaev-v2)

Downloading generated homework right after a live generation failed (browser saved `download.json`, "File wasn't available"). Root cause: `job.tsx`'s `job_completed` SSE handler used the server's `download_url` field, which `app/api/v1/jobs.py:324` emits **token-less** (`/api/v1/jobs/{id}/download`). The download is a plain `<a href download>` (can't send an Authorization header), so it depends on `?token=` — absent → 401. The load/poll paths already used the token-aware `api.jobDownloadUrl(id)`; the live-completion path was the lone exception. Fix (`7a1e21d`, FE 1-liner): `job_completed` now also builds via `api.jobDownloadUrl(id)`, ignoring the server's token-less field. Verified live: `GET …/download?token=123` → 200 + 16KB zip; no token → 401. Built into the combined Nggaev-v2 bundle + served (tsc clean). Backend's token-less `download_url` field now unused by the FE (harmless; a follow-up could drop it). Found by the operator during the chunk-3 clickthrough; shipped as its own PR (scope discipline — separate file from chunk-3).

## [0060] Fleet launcher — launch-remaining + per-lesson retry on partially-done batches — 2026-06-15 (PR #7 → Nggaev-v2)

Two launcher defects the operator caught on a 7/26-done batch: (A) the launch button was disabled on `alreadyBatched` (a batch exists for this transport) instead of on **completion**, so a partially-done batch showed a dead **"CLI launched"** and the remaining lessons couldn't be continued; (B) done lessons rendered live checkboxes in "Choose lessons", so finished work was freely re-selectable/re-launchable as if fresh. **KEY: backend already supported both flows — FE-only fix.** `jobs_repo.find_active_for_section` includes `done` in its status filter (`app/repositories/jobs.py:81`), so a plain re-launch (no `toc_entry_ids`) skips done + in-flight sections and creates jobs ONLY for the rest = idempotent-continue (`app/api/v1/batch.py:108-130`); `force=true` (batch.py:108) bypasses the lookup to regenerate. Controller-direct fix (root cause verified, FE-only, low-risk — CLAUDE.md small-fix exception). 2 commits on `feat/launcher-continue-retry`: `8ddaf91` core + `85ffa28` toc-loading guard. Changes (`web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx` + 1-line `web/src/lib/api.ts` exposing the existing backend `force`): primary button now `Launch` (fresh) / `Launch remaining N` (partial; N = lessons − done − in-flight) / disabled `Complete` (all done) / disabled `In progress` (rest in-flight), and stays `Launch` while toc detail loads (`lessons != null` guard); a **`Re-run all`** ghost button (force, confirm, notes api billing) to deliberately regenerate the whole batch — shown when `alreadyBatched && !choosing`; the picker makes done lessons non-selectable (green check + "done") each with a per-lesson **`Retry`** (force, single `toc_entry_id`, confirm). Locked with operator: A = "Launch remaining N" + re-run-all affordance; B = per-lesson Retry. FE gates: tsc clean + build ok (controller-run). Operator behavioral verification on the live dev server. NOTE: branched off `Nggaev-v2` in parallel with PR #6 (Sigma reshape, worklog 0059) — both append to this file, so a trivial both-append merge conflict is expected when the second PR merges.

## [0061] api-3 — remove the dead classify/difficulty cluster (Group 1) — 2026-06-16 (Nggaev-v2)

Picked up `api-3` from WISHLIST in parallel with R13 (PDF bytes-distribution) being done in another session — chosen specifically to avoid R13's files. Investigation found **two separable `difficulty` clusters**, and the split is what keeps this R13-safe: **Group 1** = the classify-era event/schema/column cluster (the api-3 target, **zero `pipeline.py` footprint**); **Group 2** = a `difficulty=None` parameter threaded through ~20 sites in `agent.py`/`pipeline.py` (self-contained None-threading, sibling to `deadcode-1`, **heavily overlaps R13's `pipeline.py`**). Removed **Group 1 only**; left Group 2 for a later deadcode pass when R13 isn't in flight. Verified dead before removal: `set_difficulty` had **zero callers**, `ClassifyDecision`/`Difficulty` types were unused outside their own file (the `agent.py` "Difficulty:" hits are literal prompt strings), and no test constructs the model with `difficulty`. **Backend removed:** the `difficulty_classified` SSE event emit (`api/v1/jobs.py`), `DifficultyClassifiedEvent` (`schemas/events.py`), `set_difficulty` (`repositories/jobs.py`), `JobOut.difficulty` (`schemas/job.py`), `app/schemas/classify.py` (deleted) + its `__init__` export, and the `homework_jobs.difficulty` ORM column. **Column dropped via Alembic migration** `0026_drop_difficulty.py` (rev `a8c7e6d5f4b3` ← `b9d8e7f6a5c4`; single clean head verified; safe drop of all-NULL dead data; needs `alembic upgrade head` to apply on the shared head DB). **Frontend removed:** the now-unused `Difficulty` type + `Job.difficulty` field + `difficulty_classified` event (`lib/types.ts`), the dead difficulty state/handler/chip in `routes/job.tsx`, the difficulty spans in `routes/preview.tsx`; **reworded** the inaccurate "classify difficulty" copy in `job.tsx`/`section.tsx` and folded in the `upload.tsx` "classifies the lesson" freebie. Left untouched: the unrelated `FlashcardDifficulty` type and the `[Difficulty:]` meta-tag stripping in `rich-text.tsx`/`utils.ts`. **Gates:** backend suite **408 passed / 48 skipped** (no regressions); FE `tsc --noEmit` clean + `npm run build` ok; migration imports + chains as single head. Controller-direct dead-code removal (root cause verified, low-risk). R13-independent by file footprint (jobs/events/job/homework_job/classify/migration + FE — no storage/pipeline/worker/books overlap).

## [0063] SDK-based transport=api (gemini google-genai + claude anthropic) — 2026-06-16 (feat/sdk-api-transport → Nggaev-v2, PR #11)

`transport=api` now calls the provider **SDKs** directly instead of running the CLI with an injected key; `transport=cli` is unchanged. **Why:** measured (`scripts/api_vs_cli_compare.py`, gemini-3.1-pro-preview, identical prompt) the CLI-with-key path bills **~2.6–10× the tokens and is ~1.5–3× slower** for equal-quality output — the gemini CLI prepends a ~9.2k-token agent system prompt every call (and runs multi-turn); a spec-graded review found direct-API content on par with CLI. Full gated pipeline: brainstorm→spec (3 review rounds)→reviewer-written plan (2 rounds)→subagent-driven exec (controller stress-tested every commit). **Shipped (5 commits, merged `f760f26`):** `9503c60` deps (`google-genai`+`anthropic`) + `settings.api_max_output_tokens=16384`; `d415a63` **`app/services/api_transport.py`** — `generate(provider, model, prompt, attachments) -> (rc, text, usage, stderr)` (same 4-tuple as `agent._spawn`); mirrors `_auth_env` creds (gemini key→Vertex-SA fallback; claude key; loud-on-missing), **loud truncation** (claude `stop_reason==max_tokens` / gemini `MAX_TOKENS`+partial → rc=1, never silent), blocked/empty → `(0,"")` for the existing empty-body retry, defensive usage mapping (per-provider cached semantics; claude total=input+output+cache_read+cache_creation); `7302424` one dispatch branch in `_spawn` — early (no CLI binary needed for a pure-API worker) but **inside `_semaphore()`** so api fan-out is bounded; `3ffd00f` test that SDK auth strings trip `_AUTH_SIGNALS` (api fails loud, not judge-unavailable); `0cc05fc` CLAUDE.md no-SDK rule scoped to `transport=cli`. **Scope:** gemini+claude; text-only v1 (attachments → loud `NotImplementedError`; TOC-extract-over-api stays cli-pinned until file-over-API lands). **Verified:** **422 passed / 48 skipped / 0 failed** (14 new tests); **real gemini SDK smoke** via the production path (Vertex SA): rc=0, valid output, usage tracked. **Deferred:** claude real smoke pending an `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (path fully unit-tested). **Also de-staled the live-system reference docs** (README.md, HOW_IT_WORKS.md, CODE_MAP.md) to scope their "no SDK" claims to `transport=cli`. Follow-up to Phase 4 [[0053]]. Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-16-sdk-api-transport-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-16-sdk-api-transport.md`. NOTE: numbered 0063 — `0062` is held by R13's (still-unmerged) branch, so this entry took 0063 to avoid the collision.
## [0062] R13 — pull-on-demand source PDFs for multi-PC workers (bytes-distribution half) — 2026-06-16 (PR → Nggaev-v2)

**Closed the bytes-distribution half of R13** (the `var_dir` dead-setting half shipped 0056). A fleet worker generates by reading `<var_dir>/books/<id>/source.pdf` from local disk; on a multi-PC fleet a worker claiming a lesson for a book it doesn't physically have failed instantly at `extract` with `Book PDF missing on disk` (observed live 2026-06-09). Now a worker fetches the PDF once from the head over HTTP and caches it. **Chosen approach: pull-on-demand** (over shared-volume: new runtime failure domain + brittle cross-OS mounts; over object-store: overkill at this scale) — the head is already a hard dependency (shared DB), so fetching bytes from it adds no new failure domain. Full gated pipeline: brainstorm → spec (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-16-r13-pull-on-demand-pdf-design.md`) → plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-16-r13-pull-on-demand-pdf.md`) → subagent-driven exec, controller stress-tested every commit (read diff + re-ran tests). **FE-none, backend-only, NO schema change** (lightweight integrity by decision). Commits: `2c59ce5` T1 `fleet_head_url: str = ""` config (env `FLEET_HEAD_URL`; **empty = today's behavior** — raise on missing, so single-box/head unchanged); `f4db6d0` T2 `GET /api/v1/books/{id}/source.pdf` (`FileResponse`, file-presence only/no DB lookup, auth auto-applied via the router include) + 3 endpoint tests; `c7cb8ee` T3 `app/services/book_fetch.py::ensure_book_pdf_sync` (**synchronous** — called via `asyncio.to_thread`, the house idiom matching `pipeline.py:673`; exists→fast-path no-HTTP; missing+no-url→raise; missing+url→fetch via `_fetch_to_temp` seam → atomic pid-temp+`os.replace`; first-token auth, empty→no header) + 5 unit tests; `8d4c538` T4 pipeline read-site swap (`pdf_path = await asyncio.to_thread(book_fetch.ensure_book_pdf_sync, book_id)`, old raise removed). **Four robustness gaps handled** (reviewer-caught, all verified against source): off-loop via `to_thread` (sync httpx would stall the head's API loop), `mkdir(parents=True)` for a first-time remote worker, `auth_token.split(",")[0]` (it's a comma-string, not a list), and **Windows-safe temp cleanup** — the `with open(tmp,"wb")` closes before the except `unlink`s (can't unlink an open file on Windows). **Gate:** full suite **416 passed / 48 skipped / 0 failed** (baseline 408 + 8 new). **Acceptance (real HTTP, in-band):** localhost two-process smoke — isolated head on :8077 (`VAR_DIR=/tmp/r13head` + dummy PDF), client with separate empty `VAR_DIR` + `FLEET_HEAD_URL` → fetched 29 bytes, content match, fast-path on re-call; negative (book head lacks) → `fetch from head failed: head returned HTTP 404`, no temp leak. The real cross-machine 2-PC run remains the operator-run `fleet-test-1` proof. **Operational precondition:** remote workers need `FLEET_HEAD_URL` + a matching `AUTH_TOKEN`. **Incidental fix** (`6e1b7d0`): T2's `test_401_without_token` was order-dependent — four pre-existing api test modules (`test_cancel_endpoint`/`test_retry_cancelled`/`test_notion_router`/`test_from_notion`) install a **module-level `app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user]` and never remove it**, bypassing auth for the rest of the suite; our 401 test now drops that override via `monkeypatch` (auto-restored). No production bug (route IS gated at the include); the 4 leaking files are pre-existing test-hygiene debt (→ WISHLIST `test-hygiene-1`).

**Addendum — `fleet-test-1` PROVEN live (2026-06-16, real 2-PC run).** Ran the cross-machine field test: Mac head (native Postgres exposed on LAN `192.168.1.74:5432`, head API on `0.0.0.0:8000` with `WORKER_CONCURRENCY=0` so only the remote generates) + a Windows worker (`python -m app.services.worker`, `FLEET_HEAD_URL`+matching `AUTH_TOKEN`, fresh clone with no PDFs). Launched one math-algebra g8 lesson → the Windows worker fetched the PDF from the head (`GET /api/v1/books/.../source.pdf 200` from `192.168.1.57`, bearer-header auth), cached it to local disk, and **generated all 9 phases to `done`**. R13 + the multi-PC fleet are proven end-to-end on real hardware. **Three Windows-surfaced bugs fixed on the branch** (each verified, suite green): (1) `4c175c7` — `run_standalone` used `loop.add_signal_handler` (Unix-only → `NotImplementedError` on Windows, worker wouldn't boot); now falls back to `signal.signal`. (2) `93b65c2` — the claude provider's argv lacked `-p` (print mode); without it the CLI takes a slow interactive-vs-print detour reading piped stdin **on Windows** (a per-call stall that hung `case-based-preview` past `per_attempt_timeout_seconds=600` → failover → `opencode CLI not found` → job failed); added `-p`, verified the JSON envelope is byte-identical with/without it on macOS (parse_envelope unaffected, Mac neutral 4s vs 5s) — a fleet-wide robustness fix the slow box surfaced. (3) `<this commit>` — `book_fetch.ensure_book_pdf_sync` named its temp `source.pdf.{pid}.tmp`; concurrent same-book lessons in one worker share the PID → raced on one temp (Windows sharing violation); now `…{pid}.{uuid4().hex}.tmp` (unique per call) + a barrier-based concurrency test. **Field-test findings → WISHLIST:** `fleet-failover-1` (failover reaches uninstalled CLIs → confusing hard-fail; should skip providers not on PATH) and `r13-fetch-1` (concurrent same-book fetches still each download redundantly — optional per-book lock). **Note:** the ~60 tok/s claude throughput on the Windows box (heavy phases ~5 min) is genuine generation speed, not a harness issue — a full lesson is ~10-15 min there; raise `JOB_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` if a whole job nears the 1800s ceiling on slow workers.

## [0064] Gemini 4-model api run + single-PC max-concurrency research — 2026-06-16 (worktree-gemini-4model-concurrency → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Autonomous task — generated **4 homeworks** (identical lesson, biology g9 §6 «Zamburug'lar dunyosi», a fresh not-previously-done lesson; one per gemini model: `2.5-flash`, `2.5-pro`, `3.1-pro-preview`, `3.1-flash-lite-preview`) all on `transport=api` (gemini SDK / **Vertex service-account**), saved to `~/Documents/gemini-4model-biology-g9-fungi/`; then stress-tested **max concurrency on this one Mac** for the api/SDK path. **Why:** validate the SDK api transport ([[0063]]) at scale on real content + measure the single-PC ceiling ahead of the Oct/Mar campaign. **Files:** new `scripts/{smoke_4models,save_homeworks,stress_concurrency,fanout_analysis,diag_claim}.py` + `docs/research/2026-06-16-gemini-api-concurrency.md` (no app-code change — harness + research only). **Verified (real, not predicted):** all 4 jobs reached `done` (wall 423–682 s, 8 content phases each, 0 errors); `agent_usages` confirms **37 api/gemini `phase.run` rows + 42 cli/claude rows** (4 extract + 38 judge) — content really went through the SDK/Vertex api. **Money guardrail honored** (hard user rule): only the 4 homeworks generated (**$1.27** real api spend, content only); the concurrency ceiling was found with **cheap minimal-token SDK probes** (796 calls, **$0.0009**), never by spamming homeworks.

**Operational gotcha discovered (reusable):** a `transport=api` gemini job sat **pending/unclaimable** on this Mac because `claim_next_job` ANDs **per-role** capability and the default-inherited **judge** (`judge_provider=claude`) + **extract** (`.env` `EXTRACT_PROVIDER=claude`) roles needed a claude-api key this box lacks (`can_claude_api=False`) — and `api_transport` is **text-only** so extract (PDF attachment) can't use api on *any* provider anyway. **Fix:** launch api jobs with `extract_transport=cli` + `judge_transport=cli` (content stays gemini-SDK-api; extract+judge use the local claude CLI, $0). `scripts/diag_claim.py` prints runtime `CAPABILITIES` + dry-runs the gate per pending job.

**Max-concurrency finding (api/SDK, one Mac, Vertex SA):** ramped concurrent minimal-token `gemini-2.5-flash-lite` calls N=1..64 ×2 runs — **100% success through N=48**, knee at **N=64** (run 1: 29/128 rate-limited `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`; run 2: clean), throughput plateau **~10–11 req/s** from N≥24. **The PC is NOT the bottleneck** (~25% CPU, ≤270 MB RSS at N=64); the **Vertex per-model quota** is (soft ceiling ~50–64 concurrent flash-tier calls). Real per-homework peak fan-out = **3** concurrent SDK calls (4 concurrent homeworks peaked at 7). First-pass model: `≈(50–64)/3 ≈ 16–21` flash-tier homeworks. **Multi-model follow-up (`scripts/stress_multimodel.py`, all 4 models ×2 runs, thinking-off probes) overturned the clean-ceiling picture:** the limit is a **per-model *rolling RPM quota*, bursty + partly shared, NOT a fixed concurrency wall** — `3.1-flash-lite` ran clean to N=64 in one run then 429'd at N=2 the next (budget drained by prior ramps). Robust per-model safe concurrent **jobs** (÷3): `2.5-flash` **~2–3** (tightest), `2.5-pro` **~16–20** (0 errors to N=64), `3.1-pro-preview` **~10–16** (slow, p95 to 160s), `3.1-flash-lite` **~2–15** (unstable). **No single stable max** — authoritative number is the Vertex console quota (`aiplatform.googleapis.com` RPM+concurrent, per region). Practical: `~4` jobs safe for any model (=`worker_concurrency` default); pin `2.5-pro` for ~16–20; quota-increase to go higher; PC never the bottleneck. **Notes:** pro-tier models burn far more output (thinking) tokens → 4–20× flash cost (probes capped output/disabled thinking to stay cheap). **Pricing verified against the real Vertex billing CSV** (`~/Documents/My Billing Account_Reports…csv`): every per-token rate in `pricing.py` matched to the cent — no fix needed; the month total $1.81 ≠ the 4-job $1.27 only because the CSV is the whole June window (incl. prior api jobs + cli-vs-rest benchmarks). Full report + raw CSV/JSON in `~/Documents/.../STRESS_TEST/`; repo copy `docs/research/2026-06-16-gemini-api-concurrency.md`.

## [0065] All-subjects support — single subject registry (P1) — 2026-06-16 (feat/all-subjects-prompts → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Made the supported Uzbek-curriculum subjects (grades 1–11, **26 codes**) first-class, driven by one source of truth. (Surveyed 30 curriculum codes → first trimmed to 21 "academically-testable" by user request → then a Notion textbook audit showed 6 of the 9 dropped subjects actually ship textbook PDFs, so the user kept the 5 textbook-bearing non-exam subjects — music, fine arts, technology, upbringing, CHQBT — and dropped only **PE** (by choice) plus the 3 truly textbook-less soft subjects: odobnoma/ethics, ma'naviyat/spirituality, kelajak-soati/future-hour. Net: 26 kept, 4 excluded. `subjects.EXCLUDED_KEYWORDS` blocks "Jismoniy tarbiya"/"Axloqiy tarbiya" from mis-mapping to Upbringing via the bare "tarbiya" keyword.) New module **`app/services/subjects.py`** (`SubjectDef`: code, label, family, game, language, Notion keywords + `REGISTRY`/`SUBJECT_CODES`/`notion_keyword_pairs()`). `flows.SUBJECTS`/`SUBJECT_GAME`, `prompts.SUBJECT_LABELS`/`_SUBJECT_FAMILY`, and `notion_fetch._SUBJECT_KEYWORDS` now **derive** from it; FE `web/src/lib/types.ts`/`subjects.ts` mirror it (manual). Added a Russian-as-L2 language rule (`prompts._LANG_RUSSIAN`).

**Why:** P1 prereq of the Oct/Mar content campaign ([[oct-mar-content-campaign]]) — the system only supported 7 subjects; new ones were hard-rejected at `flow_for` (KeyError), `books.py` validation, the FE union, and (the real campaign gate) `notion_fetch._map_subject` → the fleet launcher disabled any subject whose Notion title didn't map. Per the user's directive: implement "according to `docs/Infra_prompts`", work autonomously (self-approved spec+plan gates), fresh worktree.

**Key finding (verified):** the **prompt layer was already fully general** — `prompts.py` already had `_SUBJECT_FAMILY`, `FAMILY_RULES` (sciences/math/languages/humanities + `_default`), `LANGUAGE_RULES`, and `get_prompt` already fell back to `_default`/raw-subject for any unknown subject, with the Infra_prompts family blocks already ported in. So this was a **registry** job, not a prompt rewrite (spec-agent over-scoped; discarded). Subjects classified into the existing family/game/language buckets (e.g. geografiya→humanities — the humanities flashcard block already references geography; informatika/arts/PE→`default` block; two history subjects merge into `history`; science variants→`tabiiy-fanlar`).

**Files:** + `app/services/subjects.py`, `tests/services/test_subject_registry.py`; ~ `app/services/flows.py`, `app/services/prompts.py`, `app/services/notion_fetch.py`, `tests/services/test_notion_fetch.py` (2 stale "unsupported" assertions flipped to supported), `web/src/lib/types.ts`, `web/src/lib/subjects.ts`; docs de-staled (README via HOW_IT_WORKS, `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md`, `docs/CODE_MAP.md`, `CLAUDE.md`). `books.py` needed **no change** (validates against derived `SUPPORTED_SUBJECTS`). No DB migration (subject is unconstrained `String(64)`).

**Verified:** full backend suite **617 passed / 48 skipped** (with `NOTION_API_KEY` set — 5 notion-API tests 503 in a bare worktree because they assume a configured key; not a regression, proven by re-run). FE `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` clean. **Acceptance smoke (real `claude` CLI, in-process):** generated `case-based-preview` for two NEW subjects — `geografiya` (humanities: geologist field-case in formal Uzbek) and `russian` (languages: the learned content in Russian, all scaffolding in formal Uzbek — the new L2 rule working exactly as designed). Both: `flow_for` accepted (8 phases, correct game), prompts rendered with zero leftover `{{}}`. (Gemini CLI failed on interactive OAuth in the headless shell — environment, not code; campaign uses SDK transport anyway.)

**Notes:** existing 7 codes preserved verbatim (DB rows depend on them) with a regression test. Keyword shadowing handled by longest-first ordering (`jismoniy/axloqiy tarbiya` before bare `tarbiya`) + tests. `matematika` now its own code (was intentionally absent so it wouldn't collide with algebra). Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/specs/shipped/2026-06-16-all-subjects-registry-design.md` + `plans/shipped/2026-06-16-all-subjects-registry-plan.md`. Russian-medium ("klass") track still out of scope (notion_fetch reads the Uzbek "sinf" page only). With P1 done, the campaign's remaining blockers are the user's two inputs (definitive subject list confirm + Oct/Mar lesson ranges).

## [0066] Three fleet-robustness fixes — 0-entry TOC, R13 cache integrity, failover-skip — 2026-06-17 (worktree-fleet-robustness-fixes → Nggaev-v2, PR #18)

**What:** Three small, root-cause-verified fixes surfaced during the live 2-PC fleet field test, closing three open WISHLIST items. **Controller-direct** (small-fix exception — each root cause verified against real code/live evidence; TDD red→green per fix). Merge commit `188c83c`; backend-only, no FE, no migration. **(1) `toc-empty-ready`:** `toc_extractor.run` now raises on `not extracted.entries` instead of persisting `count=0` + flipping the book to `toc_ready` — a scanned/image-only or unparseable PDF (0 lessons) now takes the existing `except` path (`status="failed"` + `error_message` + `error` SSE event the FE already renders) instead of silently showing an empty book. Live: math-algebra g9 `a0173601` (240 scanned pages, watermark-only text). **(2) `r13-integrity-1`:** `book_fetch.ensure_book_pdf_sync(book_id, expected_size=None)` — a wrong-size cached PDF is dropped + re-fetched **only when `fleet_head_url` is set** (never deletes the head's canonical copy), and a truncated download is rejected (size mismatch) before `os.replace` promotion; `pipeline.py` threads `book.file_size_bytes`. Stops a corrupt/partial cache from failing every job at extract with `pypdf: Cannot find Root object`. Live: Windows worker `Oliver`, book `50b71915` (head copy intact sha `64ec4137…`, worker copy corrupt). **(3) `fleet-failover-1`:** new `agent.provider_cli_installed(name)` (`shutil.which` over the provider's `binary_names`); `_run_with_failover` skips a **fallback** provider whose CLI isn't installed (the **requested** provider is never skipped — a missing requested CLI is a real surfaced error; api transport never reaches fallbacks). Stops the confusing `opencode CLI not found` hard-fail on a single-CLI worker. Live: R13 Windows field test.

**Why:** all three were operator-visible failures during the cross-machine fleet test (the precursor to the Oct/Mar campaign) — each previously failed *silently* or *confusingly*, which is the worst mode for a many-PC fleet. **Files:** `app/services/{toc_extractor,book_fetch,agent,pipeline}.py` + `app/api/v1/{jobs,batch}.py` (the latter two only carried the reverted 4th fix — see below); tests `tests/services/{test_toc_extractor(new),test_book_fetch,test_failover_driver,test_agent_models}.py`; `docs/memory/WISHLIST.md` (3 items → Done/promoted). **Verified:** full suite **625 passed / 48 skipped / 0 failed** (3 new test files, 21 new cases); imports + runtime assumptions checked (`Book.file_size_bytes` column, `shutil` imported, provider `binary_names`, `get_provider`). No real-CLI smoke needed — none of the three alters generated content (loud-fail, cache integrity, provider routing).

**Notes — a 4th fix was committed then reverted (`b3e0772`).** An `extract→cli` coercion (`effective_extract_transport`, wired into both launch endpoints) was built on a **false premise**: that extract attaches the PDF and the api/SDK transport is text-only. Verified against `agent.py:1579/1601/1606` — lesson-extract injects the whole-book TEXT *inline* (`attachment_preamble=""`, `attachments=[]`) and passes `transport` through, so **extract runs fine via api**. The user's unclaimable job was the **claude provider/key mismatch** (extract+judge pinned to claude on a no-`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` worker → claim gate blocks), NOT a transport limit; coercion would also have forced a `gemini`-extract api job onto the gemini CLI's ~10× token tax and overridden a valid choice. Lesson captured in [[api-job-claim-gate-and-concurrency]]. **Deferred (next PR, design locked):** the two extract *features* — subset extraction for oversize text books (adabiyot 665K > 600K) + scanned-PDF vision/OCR fallback (algebra-g9) — brainstormed + user-approved approach, spec not yet written ([[extract-robustness-feature-design]]). **Worklog filed as a follow-up** (this entry) — PR #18 itself shipped without it (process gap caught in post-merge verification).

## [0067] Generate all 7 Gamified Practices every job (8→11 phases) — 2026-06-17 (feat/all-games → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** `flow_for(subject)` now emits **all four interactive mini-games** (`practice-memory-match`, `practice-tictactoe`, `practice-jigsaw`, `practice-sentence`) every job instead of only the single `SUBJECT_GAME[subject]` pick — so the full **Gamified Practices** set (those 4 + `practice-rlc` + `practice-error-detection` + `boss-arena` = all 7) is generated, none skipped. Flow grew **8 → 11 content phases** (full sequence with the pinned `extract` head: 9 → 12). Deterministic game order (memory→tictactoe→jigsaw→sentence) fixes phase_order/display.

**Why:** the job was generating content for only one mini-game per subject; the user needs all of them generated so every game type has content — *which* game fits a subject is curated **downstream/manually**, not by skipping generation.

**Scope — one functional file.** `app/services/flows.py`: added `_GAMES`, `flow_for` returns `[*_BASE_PHASES, *_GAMES, "boss-arena", "reflection"]`. `SUBJECT_GAME` **kept** (derived from `subjects.REGISTRY[*].game`) but **no longer gates the flow** — repurposed as the per-subject *recommended-game* hint for downstream curation (comment says "not consumed by the flow — don't delete as unused"). Verified-and-unchanged (grep + read): FE (`preview.tsx` renders phases dynamically by `phase_order`; `PHASE_LABELS` already had all 6 practice games), pipeline (`sequence=["extract",*flow_for]`, counts derived — no hardcoded 8), `PHASE_DEPS` (all 4 games already declared → run concurrently in one wave), prompts (all 7 exist in `_general/`), DB (no schema). `SUBJECT_GAME` consumers were only `flows.py` + `test_general_flow.py`.

**Files:** `app/services/flows.py`, `tests/services/test_general_flow.py` (shape test rewritten: 11 phases, base[:5], 4 games at [5:9], boss+reflection tail, all 7 present, no dups); docs de-staled (`README.md`, `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md`, `docs/CODE_MAP.md` — 8→11, all-games rationale); spec/plan in `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/shipped/2026-06-17-all-gamified-games-*`.

**Verified:** full suite **703 passed / 48 skipped** (up from 625: `test_prompt_coverage` params now exercise all 4 games × every subject). **Acceptance — real `claude` CLI smoke:** generated `practice-jigsaw` for `biology` (recommended game was `memory-match`, so jigsaw was NOT generated before) → coherent on-spec Jigsaw Matching markdown (board P1–P3, term↔definition / cause↔effect, photosynthesis in formal Uzbek), prompt rendered with zero leftover `{{}}`, `flow_for("biology")` has jigsaw at index 7/11. Gated pipeline followed (brainstorm→spec→plan→subagent exec, controller stress-tested the commit: read diff + re-ran tests independently).

**Cost note (for the campaign model):** ≈**+37% phase count, but materially *less* in $** — the costly whole-PDF `extract` is unchanged and the 3 added phases are small-output CBP games (output bills ~5× input). Wall-clock add is smaller still: the 4 mini-games parallelize in one scheduler wave. **Fleet watch-item (no code change):** per-job peak wave width rises (4 games + boss in one wave) → more concurrent spawns per job → at fleet scale pushes harder on the Vertex flash quota (see [[api-job-claim-gate-and-concurrency]]). Campaign cost model updated in [[oct-mar-content-campaign]] (the C-stages live there, NOT in ROADMAP.md — reviewer's pointer was off).

## [0068] Per-role provider/model selection + API-default generator — 2026-06-17 (feat/per-role-exec → Nggaev-v2, PR #20)

**What:** A job can now pin a **separate provider/model per role** — generator (content), `extract`, and `judge` — instead of one provider for everything. The launch forms expose generator + per-role overrides; an unset role inherits a sensible default. Extract resolves per-job (provider/model with `settings.extract_*` fallback), and the LLM judge takes an explicit override with a **hard self-grade guard**.

**Why:** benchmarking + the Oct/Mar campaign need e.g. gemini-API content + a cli `extract` on a Gemini sub + a cli `judge` on the Claude sub, in one job — and the judge must never grade the model that produced the output (benchmark integrity).

**Scope (backend + FE + DB):** `0027_per_role_provider_model` migration adds per-role provider/model columns on `jobs`+`batches`; repo persists them; `/generate` + `/jobs/batch` validate per-role (api requires an explicit manifest model); pipeline resolves extract per-job and threads provider/model through `_execute_one_phase`; **cross-job extract reuse re-keyed on provider+model** (else a different-model extract would wrongly adopt another's output); `/agent/models` exposes per-model **tier**; FE adds per-role provider/model controls + API-default generator + a weak-judge warning. **Self-grade guard** (`model_tiers.resolve_judge`): explicit judge override wins EXCEPT when it resolves to the generator's model → hard-swapped to the auto-tier judge.

**Blocker fixed pre-merge (reviewer-caught, `455d238`):** the guard compared the *raw* `judge_model` — a same-provider judge with `model=None` (the FE's default for a cli judge) slipped past and silently self-graded. Now **both sides' models are resolved to the provider default BEFORE comparison**, and the fallback is `judge_model_for(gen)` (guaranteed non-self), NOT the `_SELF_FALLBACK` constant — which is itself a fixed gemini model that would self-match a `gemini-3.1-pro` generator. So the reviewer's "optional `_SELF_FALLBACK` hardening" edge is **already closed by the fix's design** (no extra change needed). Regression test for the NULL-model path in `tests/services/test_judge_resolution.py`.

**Files:** `alembic/versions/0027_per_role_provider_model.py`; `app/services/{pipeline,model_tiers,phase_judge}.py`; repos; `app/api/v1/{generate,jobs}.py` (per-role fields+validation) + `/agent/models` tier; FE `web/src/...` (role controls, tiers type, weak-judge warning). Tests: `tests/api/test_agent_models_tiers.py`, `tests/api/test_role_validation.py`, `tests/repositories/test_role_provider_persist.py`, `tests/services/{test_judge_auth,test_judge_resolution,test_phase_judge,test_pipeline_role_threading}.py`, `tests/test_migrations_per_role.py`.

**Verified:** branch updated from `Nggaev-v2` (folds in #18/0066 + all-games/0067) — merge clean; **full suite 719 passed / 51 skipped** on the merged result (controller re-ran, not the pre-merge 638); FE `tsc` clean (per reviewer); in-process per-role routing smoke passes (`42d3604`). Gated pipeline: spec→plan→subagent exec; reviewer verify caught the NULL-model blocker → fixed + re-verified. Spec/plan in `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/shipped/2026-06-17-per-role-provider-model*`.

**Notes:** worklog + git-mv-to-shipped folded into the PR before merge (the all-games discipline — never land merged code without its record). The orphan docs-only `feat/per-role-provider-model` branch (early spec/plan, superseded by the shipped copies here) is retired.

**Files:** `web/src/components/category-browser.tsx` (new); `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx` (collapsible + reconciled per-role controls); `web/src/routes/{library,upload}.tsx`; `web/src/lib/subjects.ts` (grade helpers + accents). Spec in `docs/superpowers/specs/shipped/2026-06-17-subject-category-drilldown-design.md`.

Confirmed still rebased on the current `Nggaev-v2` tip (`merge-base`). FE-only, no automated route tests exist — proof is typecheck + build + Playwright (repo's UI acceptance bar).
