## [0070] Extract robustness — scanned-PDF vision + oversize text subset — 2026-06-17 (feat/extract-robustness → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Two fallbacks in the lesson-extract branch (`pipeline._execute_phase`) so books that previously hard-failed now extract. The **normal whole-book-text path is 100% unchanged**; only a problematic book takes a fallback:
- **Oversize text books** (`extract_text_is_oversize`, whole text > `extract_max_text_chars=600_000`, e.g. adabiyot-g9 `e585a5f3`, 665K chars): scope to the lesson's pages as **text inline** via new `agent.read_page_range_text` (built on `_read_pdf_pages`), then run the existing `summarize_lesson` on the subset. Cheap, **keeps `transport=api`**.
- **Scanned / sparse-text books** (no/low text layer): **vision-attach** a page-window PDF and let the model read the images. New `agent.summarize_lesson_vision` — builds a windowed subset via the (extended) `_subset_pdf(…, margin, max_pages)`, attaches it, and **forces `transport=cli`** (api transport raises `NotImplementedError` on attachments). A real vision provider reads the pages.

**The detector — caught by the acceptance smoke (the real win):** the plan first assumed Gate A (`validate_extract_text`) detects scanned PDFs. The smoke disproved it: the scanned algebra-g9 book `a0173601` is 240 pages × **~71 chars/page** of running-header text = 17K chars total, which **clears Gate A's absolute 500-char floor**. So I restored the originally-designed **per-page density gate**: new `agent.extract_text_is_too_sparse(text, n_pages)` + `agent.pdf_page_count`, config `extract_min_chars_per_page=300`. Validated against the whole 16-book corpus (measured `chars/page` = `read_whole_book_text` ÷ `pdf_page_count`, sorted) — **clean separation, zero false positives** at 300:

| book | pages | chars/pg | class |
|------|------:|---------:|-------|
| `a0173601` math-algebra g9 | 240 | **71.5** | SCANNED → vision |
| `56d020a2` / `9e7833bc` (1-pg cover-only) | 1 | 838.0 | normal |
| `50b71915` math-algebra g8 | 240 | 1420.9 | normal |
| `41aec815` history g7 | 152 | 1495.0 | normal |
| `d87e4f5c` kimyo g8 | 208 | 1514.0 | normal |
| `54cd9ff3` kimyo g9 | 208 | 1627.8 | normal |
| `62865c70` math-algebra g7 | 192 | 1788.8 | normal |
| `e585a5f3` adabiyot g9 | 368 | 1808.5 | OVERSIZE (caught before the sparse check) |
| `9c0e5362` biology g9 | 192 | 1838.6 | normal |
| `42f44dce`/`a4fa550f`/`d408fc65` | 161 | 1952.7 | normal |
| `5e295cbc` history g8 | 160 | 2008.1 | normal |
| `d463c690` english g8 | 104 | 2088.7 | normal |
| `120888c6` english g9 | 156 | 2450.8 | normal |

The threshold **300** sits in the wide empty gap between the lone scan (71.5) and the lowest real book (838) — ≈4× above the scan, ≈2.8× below the nearest normal. **Re-tune `extract_min_chars_per_page`** if a figure-heavy real book ever measures < 300/pg; the failure is **graceful** (it routes to vision, which still reads it correctly — cli cost + extract-only, never wrong content). Dispatch routes to vision when `gate_a is not None OR (not was_oversize AND too_sparse)`; the `not was_oversize` guard prevents an oversize book's small subset from being mis-read as "sparse".

**Page-offset robustness:** printed TOC page numbers can be off-by-N from physical PDF order (front-matter). For the vision window we attach `[ps-W .. pe+W]` (`extract_window_pages=5`, capped `extract_window_max_pages=25`) and tell the model to find the lesson **by TITLE** within the window — the same title-first trick the whole-text path uses, so a small offset is absorbed. Missing `page_start`/`page_end` (nullable) → **fail loud** (no worse than today). Observability: when a job requested `extract_transport=api` but the book is scanned, one log line notes the forced cli (vision can't use api).

**Scope / honest limitation:** this fixes the **lesson-extract** half. The **TOC-extract** scanned case is unchanged — a fully-scanned book with no text-layer TOC (like `a0173601`, 0 TOC entries) still fails at upload (`toc-empty-ready`, worklog 0066), so the vision lesson-extract only kicks in for a book that HAS a real TOC (page ranges) but scanned/sparse lesson bodies. The scanned-TOC OCR item (WISHLIST line 79, `toc_extractor.py`) stays open.

**Files:** `app/services/agent.py` (`_subset_pdf` margin/cap; new `summarize_lesson_vision`, `read_page_range_text`, `extract_text_is_too_sparse`, `pdf_page_count`); `app/services/pipeline.py` (extract-branch dispatch); `app/config.py` (3 new int settings). 5 new/extended test files (`test_extract_subset`, `test_summarize_lesson_vision`, `test_pipeline_extract_dispatch`, `test_config_extract_robustness`).

**Verified:** gated pipeline (one approved plan + per-task subagents, controller stress-tested every commit — diff read + tests re-run). **741 passed / 51 skipped** post-rebase (the 5 notion-router "fails" are the worktree-has-no-`.env` → empty `notion_api_key` 503 guard, unrelated; pass with the key supplied). **Real-CLI acceptance smoke** (in-process, `_record_usage` stubbed → zero DB writes): scanned algebra-g9 → vision read the image pages and produced an accurate §5 parabola-graphing summary (GATE-B PASS); oversize adabiyot-g9 → 29.5K subset (from the 665K book) → real "Alisher Navoiy" summary (GATE-B PASS). **Smoke used claude cli** because gemini-cli OAuth wasn't available on the Mac at smoke time (gemini runs via api here, which can't attach for vision) — the path is provider-agnostic; production pins gemini for extract, which reads PDFs natively via cli when OAuth'd. Closes WISHLIST `fetch-1`(b) + the scanned-lesson half of `fetch-1`(c)/line-79.

## [0071] Fleet launcher UX fixes — dead Model dropdown, vanishing launched books, honest status — 2026-06-17 (fix/fleet-launcher-ux → Nggaev-v2, PR #24)

**What:** Three frontend fixes to the redesigned Fleet launcher, all surfaced reviewing the merged redesign ([[0069]]). FE-only; shared components, so the dropdown fix also covers the single-section page.

**1. Dead per-role Model dropdown** (`RoleAgentControls.tsx`): the Model `<Select>` was always rendered but `disabled` while the role provider was on "Auto" — two un-openable "Auto" dropdowns (Extract + Judge) in the default state. Now rendered **only once a concrete provider is chosen** (reappears + auto-fills for api roles). Safe: the existing model-clearing effect already nulls the model on Auto, so no stale model reaches the payload. (Cosmetic in the end — the disabled state was intentional — but the cleaner hide was kept.)

**2. Launched book vanished from the Fleet** (`launcher.tsx`): the tray's ready list excluded any `fullyBatched` book (a batch on **every** transport via `["api","cli"].every`). With the generator now API-default ([[0068]]), launching an already-cli-batched book added the api batch → both present → dropped from the tray while still in Library. **Pre-existing bug**, made trivially reachable by the API-default. Fix: removed the `!fullyBatched` exclusion — every prepared book stays.

**3. Status always read "ready"** (`launcher.tsx`): a launched, in-progress book still showed "ready". Now lifecycle-aware, split by the data each level has (the books-list endpoint returns `toc: null` — per-lesson progress only exists in the per-book card's `getBook` fetch): **caption/sections** (batches only) = "ready" (never launched) vs "launched"; **per-book card chip** (own TOC) = "ready to launch" / "generating" / "complete". `complete` requires **every** lesson done (no false complete on partial); `generating` requires **`activeCount > 0`** (running/pending/cancelling) so a fully-failed/idle book does NOT falsely pulse "generating" — the honest-status discipline this PR exists for.

**Files:** `web/src/components/fleet/RoleAgentControls.tsx`, `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx`. No backend/API/DB change; `fullyBatched` removed (`batchedTransports` kept for per-card re-run).

**Verified:** tsc clean; vite build OK. **Playwright** on a live build (auth → drill Grade 7 → History → expand): **zero console/runtime errors**; Model dropdowns hidden on Auto (5 comboboxes) and reappear on provider pick (6); History present (not vanished); chip "generating" consistent with its launch button; captions show ready/launched. **Known fast-follow:** with #2 the tray grows monotonically (launched/complete books never leave) — fine for a few books, may want a "Complete" split for the all-subject campaign; doing it honestly needs per-book progress at the list level (a backend change), so deferred.

## [0072] Scanned-TOC vision fallback — read a scanned book's contents page via vision — 2026-06-17 (feat/scanned-toc-vision → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** The upstream sibling of 0070. #0070 fixed the *lesson* extract for scanned books; this fixes the *TOC* extract so a fully-scanned book gets past **upload** at all. In `agent.extract_toc`, when the local TOC text excerpt is unusable (empty OR `extract_text_is_too_sparse(text, pages_read)` — a watermark-only scan), **drop the junk text** (which was steering the model to `{"entries": []}`) and **vision-attach a bounded front+back page-window PDF** (new `_toc_source_pdf`) so the extract provider OCRs the printed contents page (a "Mundarija" prints front OR back). The normal dense-text TOC path is **byte-identical** (moved verbatim into the `else`, incl. the gemini `keep_pdf` ≤20MB augment).

**Why it was broken (verified, not assumed):** `extract_toc` already had a *conditional* vision attach — but **gemini-only** (`keep_pdf = provider == "gemini" and ≤20MB`), so claude/others never saw the PDF; and the watermark text was non-empty → `has_local_toc_text=True` → fed as "the TOC text" → the prompt's "return [] if no readable TOC" steered a `[]`. A live **claude-cli probe** proved the scanned book's TOC is fully vision-readable — it just lives at the **back** (front 12 pages → "NO TOC HERE"; last 15 → the complete contents with page numbers).

**Decisions:** bounded **front+back window** (not whole-PDF — works for >20MB scans the gemini attach rejects; textbooks print contents only front/back, never mid-book → YAGNI on auto-widen). Window is **configurable + asymmetric** via new knobs `extract_toc_front_pages=12` / `extract_toc_back_pages=20` (back larger: the back-TOC is the no-front-matter-offset case, real Mundarija is 2–4 pages) — so the remedy for a too-narrow case is "bump an env var + re-extract", config not code. `_TOC_TAIL_PAGES` (=15) is the *separate* pre-existing text-scan constant, deliberately not reused (can't be configurable/asymmetric). Forced cli is automatic (TOC runs at upload, no job; api can't attach anyway). **Actionable loud-fail:** the 0-entry message now names the cause AND remedy ("widen extract_toc_front_pages/extract_toc_back_pages and re-extract") — the cheap insurance that replaces auto-widen.

**Files:** `app/services/agent.py` (`_toc_source_pdf` helper; the `too_sparse`→vision dispatch in `extract_toc` + temp-cleanup `try/finally` around the attempt loop; `source="vision_toc"`), `app/config.py` (2 knobs), `app/services/toc_extractor.py` (actionable message). 3 new/extended test files.

**Verified:** gated pipeline (approved plan → per-task subagents → controller stress-tested every commit: whitespace-ignored diff confirmed the dense path is byte-identical + loop body untouched). **749 passed / 51 skipped** post-rebase. **Real-CLI acceptance** (claude cli, `_record_usage` stubbed → zero DB writes): on the real scanned book `a0173601` (which previously got **0 entries**), the live `extract_toc` detected sparse text (3909 chars / 55 pages → too_sparse) → vision-attached front 12 + back 20 → returned **39 real TOC entries** with correct titles AND page ranges (§1 Kvadrat funksiyaning ta'rifi p5-6, §2 y=x² p7-9, …). Used claude because gemini-cli OAuth is down on this Mac (gemini runs via api here, which can't attach); path is provider-agnostic, production pins gemini. **Closes the last open piece of the scanned-book story** (WISHLIST line 79).

## [0073] Retry book TOC extraction — re-prepare a failed/stuck book — 2026-06-18 (feat/toc-retry → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** A first-class retry for book **preparation** (TOC extraction), mirroring the existing job-retry. Motivated by `a0173601`: its TOC extraction failed (claude weekly-limit) and there was **no way to re-trigger it** — it needed a hand-run script. New `POST /books/{book_id}/toc/retry` re-fires the same background extraction; FE **Retry** button on failed/stuck books.

**Shape:** retry allowed from `failed` + a stuck `toc_extracting` only (user-chosen); `toc_ready`/`uploading` → 409. Guards: 404 missing book, 409 wrong status, 409 source PDF missing on disk. Endpoint optimistically flips status to `toc_extracting` (clears the stale `error_message`) before returning, so the UI shows "re-preparing" immediately; the existing `GET /{id}/toc/stream` SSE shows live progress on retry — no new plumbing. The duplicated upload/from-notion trigger block was DRY'd into a shared `_start_toc_extraction(book_id, pdf_path, subject)` helper used by all paths.

**Idempotent re-extract (gatekeeper catch):** `toc_repo.bulk_create` is a **naive append** (no upsert, no unique constraint on `toc_entries`) — the reason a re-run didn't dup *today* was purely the status guard (a failed/stuck book has 0 persisted entries). Took the data-layer fix: new `toc_repo.delete_for_book(book_id)` called immediately before `bulk_create` in `toc_extractor.run` → first-extract is a no-op, retry replaces. **FK caveat (noted in plan):** safe because retry excludes `toc_ready` (no `homework_jobs.toc_entry_id` references those rows); allowing retry-from-`toc_ready` later would orphan job FKs → needs cascade/block handling. Also fixed a latent bug: `books_repo.set_status` now always assigns `error_message`, so a non-`failed` status no longer carries a stale error.

**Files:** `app/api/v1/books.py` (`_start_toc_extraction` helper + retry endpoint), `app/repositories/toc_entries.py` (`delete_for_book`), `app/services/toc_extractor.py` (clear-before-insert), `app/repositories/books.py` (`set_status` null-clear), `web/src/lib/api.ts` (`retryBookToc`), `web/src/routes/book.tsx` + `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx` (Retry buttons). 2 new/extended test files.

**Verified:** gated pipeline (approved plan + gatekeeper correction → per-task subagents → controller stress-tested each commit: `set_status` caller audit confirmed the always-clear is safe across all 4 book callers; refactor proven clean by the from-notion regression). **756 passed / 51 skipped**; tsc clean + vite build OK. No real-CLI gate (re-triggers extraction, doesn't change generation output). Honest env note: on *this* Mac a retry still fails (gemini-cli OAuth down + claude weekly-limit until Jun 18 11pm ET) — environment, not the feature; the actionable error surfaces. Works wherever a vision provider has quota (the production server).
## [0074] History variant label — distinguish Jahon vs O'zbekiston tarixi in the UI (R17) — 2026-06-18 (feat/history-variant-label → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** "history" is one coarse app-subject, but it splits into two textbooks per grade — **Jahon tarixi** (World) and **O'zbekiston tarixi** (national). The batch funnel + launcher cards rendered both identically as **"History · grade N"**, so an operator couldn't tell which history book a batch/lesson belonged to (R17). Now a backend-derived `subject_variant` lets the FE render **"History · Jahon · grade N"** / **"History · O'zbekiston · grade N"**.

**Audit first (the part that reframed the work):** swept Notion all grades — **G8/9/10/11** cleanly carry both `O'zbekiston tarixi` + `Jahon tarixi`; **G7** has both but the national page is mis-titled `Tarix Uzb` (its PDF is correctly `7-sinf_Ozbekiston_tarixi_2022…`); **G5/G6** are a single combined `Tarix (Qadimgi dunyo)`; **G1–4** none. Then verified the live `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES` env (read **only** that one line — never the rest of `.env`): the per-grade `{jahon, ozbekiston}` archive split is **already correctly configured for G6–11** (every page-id checked against Notion), so backend *routing* was fine — the gap was purely the **display**. (`history|5` absent — deferred by user as intentional.)

**Approach (chosen / rejected):** backend **detects**, FE **presents**. `subject_variant` is a **computed, non-persisted** field (`"jahon"|"ozbekiston"|null`) derived from `original_filename` — no DB migration, no generation impact. *Rejected:* FE-derives-from-filename (duplicates fold logic in TS + the batch card has no filename) and persist-the-Notion-title / split-the-`history`-code (a migration + contradicts the deliberate coarse subject; would also fix R16, but R16 is out of scope here).

**Key invariant — fold parity:** `subjects.history_variant` folds the filename with the **same** apostrophe set + `lower()` as the Notion archive split (`notion_archive._fold`), so the UI label and the archive page-routing can never disagree. A drift-guard test (`test_fold_agrees_with_archive_fold`) cross-checks every glyph against `notion_archive._fold` — fails loudly if either fold drifts. Keyword order is **jahon-first** (mirrors the majority of the per-grade `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES` dicts; a both-keyword filename doesn't exist in practice).

**Files:** `app/services/subjects.py` (`history_variant` + keyword/apostrophe constants), `app/schemas/book.py` (`subject_variant` `computed_field` on `BookOut` → covers GET /books + /books/{id}), `app/api/v1/batch.py` (`_rollup_payload` gains the field; 3 callers pass the filename), `app/repositories/batches.py` (`list_with_rollups` joins `books.original_filename`), `web/src/lib/types.ts` (Book + BatchSummary), `web/src/lib/subjects.ts` (`VARIANT_LABELS` + `subjectLabelWithVariant`), `web/src/components/fleet/batch-funnel.tsx`, `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx` (3 cards), `web/src/routes/library.tsx`. 3 new backend test files (18 cases).

**Verified:** gated pipeline (approved plan → fresh subagent per task → controller stress-tested every commit: re-read each diff AND re-ran each test). **762 passed / 51 skipped**; `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` clean + `npm run build` OK. No CLI smoke — display-only, generation untouched (acceptance gate N/A). **Plan deviation caught in review:** Task 5 assumed all `subjectLabel` sites in `launcher.tsx` would vanish — wrong (3 unrelated avatar/group-label uses remain), so the subagent correctly *added* `subjectLabelWithVariant` rather than swapping the import. **Pre-existing, unrelated:** 5 notion-router tests fail with `503` in this worktree — they need an ambient `NOTION_API_KEY` (guard `_client()` raises 503; tests patch the client but not the setting); this branch touches **zero** notion files. Worth a WISHLIST note (those tests should patch `settings.notion_api_key`), not this PR's job. **Still OPEN:** R16 (backend keyword robustness) + `history|5` config.

## [0075] Hotfix — `delete_for_book` name collision crashed TOC re-extract — 2026-06-18 (fix/toc-delete-collision → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Hotfix for a runtime bug shipped in 0073. `app/repositories/toc_entries.py` imports SQLAlchemy's `delete` AND defines a public `async def delete(session, toc_entry_id)` single-entry function that **rebinds the module name** — so the new `delete_for_book`'s `delete(TOCEntry)` resolved to the single-entry function and raised `TypeError: delete() missing 1 required positional argument: 'toc_entry_id'` at the clear-before-insert step.

**Live impact (observed):** a real retry of the scanned Algebra-g9 book ran an **8.5-minute claude vision extraction that succeeded (60 entries)** and then crashed at persist → status `failed`, **the 60 entries (and the claude quota) wasted**. Every retry would repeat it.

**Fix:** alias the import — `from sqlalchemy import delete as sa_delete` — and use `sa_delete(TOCEntry)` in `delete_for_book` (the public single-entry `delete()` stays). One-line change + a DB-free regression test (`tests/repositories/test_toc_delete_for_book.py`) that runs the **real** `delete_for_book` body and asserts it emits a SQLAlchemy `Delete` — **proven red on the bug** (reproduces the exact `TypeError`), green with the fix.

**Why it slipped through 0073:** the real `delete_for_book` body was never executed in tests — the repo-level test was `RUN_DB_INTEGRATION`-gated/skipped and the extractor idempotency test *mocked* `delete_for_book` (call-order only). Lesson: a function whose only coverage is a mock of itself is untested; the new test executes the actual statement-building.

**Files:** `app/repositories/toc_entries.py` (1-line fix + docstring note), `tests/repositories/test_toc_delete_for_book.py` (new). **775 passed / 51 skipped.** Backend-only, no FE rebuild needed — deploy = `git pull` + restart workers. (Controller-direct small fix per CLAUDE.md — verified root cause from the live traceback, no plan.)

---

## [0076] Fix — `crypto.randomUUID` undefined on HTTP LAN IP broke section retry/generate — 2026-06-18 (fix/safe-uuid-nonsecure → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** The section page's Generate/Retry minted a per-click idempotency key via `crypto.randomUUID()` (`section.tsx:104`). `crypto.randomUUID` exists in modern browsers but **only in a secure context** (HTTPS or `localhost`); served over plain HTTP on a LAN IP (the fleet head, e.g. `http://192.168.x.x:8000`) it is `undefined` → `TypeError: crypto.randomUUID is not a function`, thrown *before* the API call. Symptom: section-page Retry "didn't respond at all" / console TypeError on multiple failed lessons (Adabiyot-g9 Alpomish + another). The **Monitor-page retry worked** because it uses `api.retryJob(jobId)` (reuses the job, no new key) — which is what isolated it to the key-minting path.

**Fix:** new `web/src/lib/uuid.ts::safeUUID()` — prefers native `crypto.randomUUID`, falls back to `crypto.getRandomValues` (which IS available in non-secure contexts) building an RFC4122 v4, then `Math.random` as last resort (it's an idempotency key, not security-sensitive). `section.tsx` now calls `safeUUID()`. Only usage site (grep-confirmed single occurrence).

**Files:** `web/src/lib/uuid.ts` (new), `web/src/routes/section.tsx` (import + 1-line swap + comment). FE-only — controller-direct small fix (root cause verified from the live `TypeError`). tsc clean, vite build OK, `getRandomValues` fallback confirmed in the bundle. **Deploy = rebuild the FE (`cd web && npm run build`) + hard-refresh** — the served `web/dist` is gitignored, so a `git pull` alone won't update it. Note: this is independent of the book-TOC retry (0073); it's the homework-generation retry on the section page.

## [0077] Cluster 1 — Quick-win hardening (8 independent fixes) — 2026-06-18 (cluster-1-hardening → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** A batch of 8 small, high-value, mostly-independent hardening fixes (one commit + TDD test each), the first of the 6 remediation clusters (`docs/memory/REMEDIATION_CLUSTERS.md`):
1. **`dep-cve-1`** — bumped `pypdf` 6.10.2→6.13.3, `python-multipart`→0.0.32, added explicit `starlette>=1.3.1` floor (was transitive) past reachable upload/PDF-parse CVEs; `npm audit fix` carried react-router 7.1.1→7.18.0 + vite (lockfile only — within existing semver ranges, `package.json` untouched).
2. **`flow-1`** — the pipeline "Phase scheduler stuck" `RuntimeError` embedded the resolved-deps dict as a literal double-brace f-string (`{{p: … for p in …}}`) → rendered as static text; extracted a pure `_scheduler_stuck_message()` helper that interpolates the real dict.
3. **`concurrency-knob-1` (read-the-right-knob half only)** — the live CLI semaphore (`agent.py`) read the **deprecated** `settings.gemini_max_concurrency` while the documented-live `agent_max_concurrency` had zero readers (inverted since Wave 3E). New `_effective_concurrency()` reads `agent_max_concurrency` (live), falls back to `gemini_max_concurrency` only when the new one is left at its default 8 (so existing `.env`s don't break). `docs/DEPLOY.md` de-staled (flipped the live/deprecated rows; kept the **per-process** caveat). The fleet-wide token-bucket half is Cluster 5 (deliberately out of scope).
4. **`extract-2`** — a blank `EXTRACT_PROVIDER=` in `.env` was read by pydantic as `""` → `get_provider("")` KeyError; added a `field_validator(mode="before")` mapping blank/whitespace→default `"gemini"`, scoped strictly to that field.
5. **`judge-timeout-1`** — the two `phase_judge.judge()` calls had no per-attempt timeout (a hung judge stalled the phase to the whole-job timeout). New `_judge_with_timeout()` wraps both in `asyncio.wait_for(timeout=settings.per_attempt_timeout_seconds)`; on `asyncio.TimeoutError` it returns the same `JudgeOutcome(available=False, passed=True, warnings=["judge-unavailable: TimeoutError"])` shape the judge already produces on error → phase completes `done`, job NOT killed. Catches **only** `TimeoutError`, so the existing api-auth re-raise (in `phase_judge.judge`) still propagates.
6. **Stale-pending sweep** — a `pending` job with `attempts >= max_attempts` was skipped by the claim query yet never failed (`mark_failed_with_retry` runs only for claimed jobs) → wedged forever. New `jobs.fail_exhausted_pending_jobs()` marks them `failed`; wired into `main.lifespan` startup + `worker._sweep_stuck_jobs` periodic.
7. **`test-hygiene-1`** — **5** api test modules (the 4 named + a discovered `test_toc_retry.py`, which was the actual cause of `test_401_without_token` failing) installed a module-level `app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user]` at import and never cleared it → suite-wide auth bypass leak; converted each to an autouse fixture with teardown and removed the band-aid `monkeypatch.delitem` in `test_book_source_pdf.py`.
8. **`db-check-constraints-1`** — migration `0028_enum_check_constraints` + matching model `CheckConstraint`s for `homework_jobs.status`/`transport`/`extract_transport`/`judge_transport` and `batches.transport`/`extract_transport`/`judge_transport` (7 total), so an out-of-range enum is rejected at the DB instead of wedging a job deep in the pipeline.

**Why:** clears low-risk noise so the bigger clusters stand alone; closes 8 verified backlog items (security CVEs, a silently-dead concurrency knob, an un-timed judge, a wedged-job class, a test-isolation leak, two config/diagnostic footguns).

**Files:** `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock`, `web/package-lock.json`; `app/services/pipeline.py`, `app/services/agent.py`, `app/config.py`, `app/repositories/jobs.py`, `main.py`, `app/services/worker.py`; `app/models/homework_job.py`, `app/models/batch.py`, `alembic/versions/0028_enum_check_constraints.py`; `docs/DEPLOY.md`; tests: `tests/services/test_pipeline_flow1.py`, `test_agent_semaphore.py`, `test_judge_timeout.py` (+ `test_execute_phase_judge.py` source-assert updated), `tests/test_config_extract_provider.py`, `tests/repositories/test_fail_exhausted_pending.py`, `tests/integration/test_check_constraints.py`, and the 5 converted api test modules + `test_book_source_pdf.py`.

**Verified:** full suite **791 passed / 56 skipped** (`NOTION_API_KEY=dummy`; note the real env var is `NOTION_API_KEY`, not `NOTION_TOKEN`); `pip-audit` clean on all three bumped deps (residual `idna 3.13`/PYSEC-2026-215 is a transitive dep, out of scope); `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; migration `0028` applied to a real scratch Postgres with **44 DB-integration tests green** incl. 8 new CHECK-constraint reject/accept cases (RED→GREEN confirmed: bad inserts accepted pre-migration); single alembic head `0028_enum_check_constraints`. No generation-path change → no CLI smoke. Final whole-branch review (opus): clean, no Critical/Important.

**Notes:** `judge-timeout-1` shares the two `phase_judge.judge` call sites with Cluster 3's `judge-softfail-1` — flagged to the gatekeeper to sequence (whichever merges second rebases). The `AGENT_MAX_CONCURRENCY=8`-indistinguishable-from-unset edge of the fallback is documented in the docstring + DEPLOY.md (accepted trade-off). Cf. [[per-job-selection-overrides-env]]. Pre-assigned worklog ID per `REMEDIATION_CLUSTERS.md`.

## [0078] Cluster 2 — cancel/resume correctness — 2026-06-18 (cluster-2-cancel-resume → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Reliable batch cancel, first-class batch resume, and the end of the silent relaunch data-loss/re-bill. Sequential on the cancel-reliability keystone.

- **cancel-race-1 (keystone):** `jobs.set_status` was an unconditional ORM assignment; the pipeline rewrites `running` every phase, so a one-shot `running→cancelling` got clobbered before the ~30s heartbeat sampled it (observed live: 4 algebra-g9 jobs bounced `cancelling→running`). Now a guarded `UPDATE` — terminal {done,failed,cancelled} frozen; from `cancelling` only `→cancelled` (pure predicate `status_write_allowed` + matching WHERE). `phase_repo.set_status` freezes `done` rows (protects the resumable set). claim / `reset_for_retry` use their own UPDATEs → unaffected. ORM-staleness caller sweep clean.
- **fleet-ctrl-1:** `POST /jobs/batch/{id}/cancel` — `cancel_all_in_batch` (pending→cancelled + running→cancelling in one txn) + instant local task-kill for same-process jobs.
- **fleet-ctrl-2:** `POST /jobs/batch/{id}/resume` — `resume_failed_in_batch` loops `reset_for_retry` over failed+cancelled (reuses `done` phases).
- **fleet-relaunch-dataloss-1 — deliverable CHANGED (steer-to-resume; supersedes the brief's "confirm-then-discard"):** `launch_batch` now RESUMES a failed/cancelled section (reuse saved phases) instead of `create` (which discarded + re-billed). Added a STRICT zero-write `preview` mode (disposition computed before any batch write — no phantom batch row) + `relaunch_mode` resume|discard; discard is the confirmed escape hatch, not the default.
- **FE:** api client (`cancelBatch`/`resumeBatch`/`previewBatch` + `relaunch_mode`); ReadyCard three-way — **Launch remaining** (preview → if saved work, chained-confirm Resume primary / Discard&regenerate escape hatch re-billing X), **Resume failed/cancelled** + **Cancel all** buttons (gated on the batch rollup), **Re-run all** moved to a ⋯ kebab (re-bills all incl. completed).

**Tests:** real-body integration tests (`tests/integration/`, RUN_DB_INTEGRATION on edu_homework) for the guard / cancel-all / resume / relaunch; mocked endpoint unit tests; FE tsc+build. **804 unit pass / 5 pre-existing notion-503 env fails; integration green** — note `test_claim_contention` flakes ONLY in the full integration run (pre-existing shared-DB isolation issue; passes isolated + paired; C2 touches no claim code). Per-task: fresh implementer subagent + controller diff-read + test-rerun; a test-hygiene override leak and a missing FE discard path were caught and fixed in-loop.

**Acceptance (live cancel→resume run) is the gatekeeper's PR checklist** (no worker on the Mac head; consistent with repo precedent for live gates). Closes cancel-race-1, fleet-ctrl-1/2, fleet-relaunch-dataloss-1, fleet-test-4, and cancel-1(a) once the live run passes. **Merge BEFORE Cluster 4** (its kill-switch builds on this set_status guard).

## [0079] Cluster 3 — Judge Quality (fidelity, softfail, claimgate) — 2026-06-18 (cluster-3-judge-quality → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Three complementary judge fixes that together close `judge-fidelity-1`, `judge-softfail-1`, and `judge-claimgate-1`. 9 code commits `4d9f5a6..41d4881`; 828 passed / 71 skipped; 17 cross-cluster DB-integration tests green on scratch DB; single alembic head `0029_judge_status`.

- **judge-fidelity-1 — KEY MECHANISM CORRECTION:** the prior backlog text said "the judge never sees the source lesson text" — **this was wrong**. The source already reached the judge model via `run_phase`→`_build_master_prompt`'s `--- LESSON CONTEXT ---` block. The real gap was twofold: (1) the judge was never *instructed* to treat that block as ground truth for fact-checking, and (2) there was no deterministic check at all. Fix: `_FIDELITY_RULE` prompt reframe in `phase_judge.py` — the judge is now explicitly told to treat the LESSON CONTEXT as the authoritative source and to cite any output claims that contradict it. Additionally, a conservative **warning-only deterministic** year-signal (`_fidelity_flags`) does a post-hoc cross-check: it extracts 4-digit years from the source and from the output, and flags any year in the output that is *absent* from the source (skips math/exercise lines to avoid false-positives on formula numbers; never gates a regen — `WARNING` only). Source text is kept full (no trimming). **Proven by a real two-sided CLI smoke** (`scripts/smoke_judge_fidelity.py`): flagged an invented year "1991" not present in the source; did NOT flag math worked-example numbers.
- **judge-softfail-1:** new `phase_outputs.judge_status` column (migration `0029_judge_status`, nullable, no CHECK constraint) + ORM model field + `phase_repo.set_status` write-through; `settings.max_judge_regens` (default 1 = unchanged behavior from before). Pipeline (`pipeline.py`) now: (a) retries the judge once on a transient `judge-unavailable` (before giving up), (b) runs the regen loop bounded by `max_judge_regens`, (c) re-checks the post-regen verdict, and (d) records `judge_status` = `ok` / `major_shipped` / `major_regen_failed` / `unavailable`. Built on cluster-1's `_judge_with_timeout`.
- **judge-claimgate-1:** `worker._compute_capabilities` + `jobs.claim_next_job` now evaluate the worker's per-provider API capability against the **JOB's** resolved judge/extract provider (`COALESCE(job column, settings default)`), not just the global settings defaults. This fixes a real stranding bug: a job with `judge_provider=gemini` + `judge_transport=api` was evaluated against the default JUDGE_PROVIDER=claude caps, so it was never claimed on a Vertex-only (no ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) worker. **Full audit result**: the claim gate was the ONLY drift point — `model_tiers.resolve_judge` and `pipeline._resolve_extract` already honored per-job overrides; `toc_extractor` is job-less; `worker.py`'s self-fallback capability check is advisory-only.

**Deferred Minors (NOT fixed, logged for PR triage):** A2 `_MATH_CUES` over-broad suppression (false-negative-only risk in edge cases); B4 a successful-regen followed by unavailable judge records "unavailable" rather than "major_shipped" (observability only, content is correct); `_unavail()` test relies on a dataclass default (fragile but not broken); C1 `judge_api_ok`/`extract_api_ok` caps keys are now SQL-unused (the claim gate reads per-role resolved values) — a legacy comment would clarify.

**R20 (golden-eval harness) remains OPEN** as a deliberate follow-on; it now builds on the shipped fidelity check and the queryable `judge_status`.

**Files:** `app/services/phase_judge.py`, `app/services/pipeline.py`, `app/services/worker.py`, `app/repositories/jobs.py`, `app/repositories/phase_outputs.py`, `app/models/phase_output.py`, `app/config.py`, `alembic/versions/0029_judge_status.py`, `scripts/smoke_judge_fidelity.py`, tests across all touched modules.

**Verified:** full suite 828 passed / 71 skipped; 17 DB-integration tests green; two-sided fidelity smoke on real CLI (claude); single alembic head confirmed.

## [0080] Cluster 4 — Cost Safety (ledger + pause-claim kill-switch + cache-write billing) — 2026-06-18 (cluster-4-cost-safety → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** make the paid `transport=api` path safe to scale — nothing previously read `cost_usd` to gate spend, a re-run silently re-billed, and claude cache-writes were unpriced. Closes `fleet-api-3`, `fleet-api-4`, `pricing-1`. 8 TDD tasks, subagent-driven (fresh implementer + task-review + fix loop per task; final whole-branch review on opus).

**Locked decisions (user):** BOTH caps — per-batch $ (computed-on-read, `agent_usages → homework_jobs.batch_id` join) + fleet-day $ (rolling 24h); block a job if EITHER trips. Halt = **pause-claim** (stop claiming; in-flight jobs finish; never hard-cancel paid work).

**Shipped:**
- **pricing-1** — new `agent_usages.cache_creation_tokens` (migration `0030`, additive + backfill from `raw_envelope`); claude cache-write captured (api + cli envelopes) and priced at **1.25× input** in `cost_usd`. Per-provider cached-token semantics (gemini `prompt-includes-cached` vs claude disjoint) left intact — a regression test pins both branches (the no-collapse hard rule).
- **fleet-api-3** — cost ledger `app/repositories/cost.py` (`batch_api_cost_usd`/`fleet_api_cost_usd`/`section_prior_api_cost`); the **batch-pause primitive** (`batches.paused_at`/`paused_reason` + `pause_batch`/`unpause_batch`/`unpause_by_reason`, migration `0031`) — **built once, C5's `fleet-ctrl-3` reuses it**; the **fleet-daily global gate** (`budget_state` singleton, migration `0032`) which catches batchless `/generate` api jobs a per-batch pause can't; the **budget monitor** (`worker._budget_monitor`, periodic like `_sweep_stuck_jobs`) trips/clears both pauses, strictly reason-scoped (`batch-cap`/`fleet-daily-cap` only — never disturbs a manual pause), early-returns when both caps 0. Claim-gate predicates AND-compose with C3 in `claim_next_job` — the batch predicate's `IS NULL` arm keeps batchless jobs claimable; `fleet_api_paused` threaded from `worker._claim_one`.
- **fleet-api-4** — never-pay-twice: a force re-run over a section with a prior done api job surfaces `prior_api_cost_usd` + `would_rebill` (detect+warn, force still proceeds; reconciles with the free resume/done-phase reuse). To reconcile with C2's relaunch-confirm shape later.
- **Observability/FE:** `GET /jobs/batch/{id}/cost` + `paused_at`/`paused_reason` on the batch rollup; a minimal amber "Paused — budget cap reached" badge (polished $ dashboard deferred to C6). Config: `COST_CAP_BATCH_USD`/`COST_CAP_FLEET_DAILY_USD` (0=disabled) + `COST_CHECK_INTERVAL_SECONDS`. Reference docs de-staled (DEPLOY/HOW_IT_WORKS/CODE_MAP/DATABASE).

**Verified:** full offline suite 912 passed / 5 pre-existing notion-503 (env, C4 touches no notion). **Acceptance gate — real Postgres:** migration chain `0030–0032` applied clean; **102 C4 integration/behavioral tests green against real rows** (incl. `test_batch_pause_claimgate` proving the claim predicate excludes paused-batch jobs and keeps batchless jobs claimable — settling the offline-only test concern; gate also caught + fixed a dead Task-3 integration test). Migrations linear, single head `0032`. Built after C1/C2/C3 (all in base); pause-claim does NOT need C2's set_status guard (it writes `batches.paused_at`, not a job status).

## [0083] PR #37 "Habibullo" integration — custom prompts + phase-picker + monitor redesign, onto C1–C4 — 2026-06-19 (Habibullo → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** integrate contributor PR #37 (VenEl101, one commit branched after C3 / before C4). It ships three genuinely-new features — **custom-prompt upload** (per-phase prompt override), **per-job phase-picker** (run a chosen subset), and a **monitor redesign** (per-transport `TransportRow` grouping + whole-book `not_started` rollup + subject drilldown) — but couldn't merge as-is: two Alembic heads, textual conflicts with C4, and a silent drop of C4's budget-pause UI/payload. Gated-pipeline plan → gatekeeper-approved → subagent-driven-development. **Guiding principle (user):** preserve our shipped C1–C4, adopt the new features, graft C4 back into the files PR37 rewrote. **Shipped (rebased onto `Nggaev-v2` as `pr37-integration`):**

## [0081] Cluster 5 / P1 — Fleet control & safety (peer-aware reclaim + manual pause + PC drain) — 2026-06-19 (cluster-5-fleet-scale → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** first of a 3-way split of Cluster 5 (P2 = shared token-bucket + Retry-After backoff; P3 = multi-pod SSE LISTEN/NOTIFY — both follow-on). Three independent, non-generation-affecting fleet items, all Postgres-native, **no migration**. Gated-pipeline plan → gatekeeper-approved → full subagent-driven-development (8 tasks, fresh implementer + task review + fix per task; opus whole-branch review = Ready to merge).

**Shipped:**
- **`fleet-restart-reclaim-1` — peer-aware startup reclaim.** `main.lifespan` reset EVERY `running` job on boot (`reclaim_stuck_jobs(stale_after_seconds=0)`) → double-ran a live peer's heartbeated job in a multi-PC fleet (real $). New `jobs.reclaim_orphans_on_startup` + `workers.has_live_workers`: reset-all (stale=0, instant single-host recovery preserved) only when no live peer is registered, else the lease window (`reclaim_stale_seconds`) so a peer's fresh-beat job is never yanked. The lifespan sweep runs BEFORE the embedded worker registers, so the registry shows only peers. Best-effort caveat documented (a sub-window restart may read the process's own old row as a peer → lease path that once; correctness unaffected).
- **`fleet-ctrl-3` — manual batch pause/resume.** `POST /jobs/batch/{id}/pause|unpause` reusing C4's `batches.pause_batch` primitive with reason **`"manual"`** + FE toggle button + reason-aware badge ("Paused by operator" vs C4's "budget cap reached"). Manual and C4-auto pause are clobber-proof in BOTH directions (monitor pauses only `active_batch_ids`=unpaused, reconciles only reason `batch-cap`) — real-DB RED-proved.
- **`fleet-ctrl-4` — PC-level drain.** `POST /workers/{pc_id}/drain|undrain` sets `workers.status="draining"`; the worker reads its own status each registry beat (`_drain_check_and_beat`) and, when draining, calls `stop()` and SKIPS the `upsert_heartbeat("online")` that would otherwise clobber the signal → stops claiming, in-flight finish via `_drain()`. New `workers.get_status/set_status`. FE Drain/Undrain button + amber "draining" chip on worker cards.

**Verified:** full suite **1042 passed / 5 fail** (the 5 are pre-existing notion-503 env failures; branch touches no notion files); real-PG integration green for has_live_workers, peer-aware reclaim, reason-scoping, get/set_status, worker self-drain, and the drain endpoint (each guard RED-proved). tsc + build clean. Single Alembic head `0034` (no migration). **No merge — routed to gatekeeper.** Closes `fleet-restart-reclaim-1` + the manual-pause slice of `fleet-ctrl-3` + `fleet-ctrl-4`; the token-bucket (`concurrency-knob-1` fleet half) + `sse-multipod-1` remain as P2/P3.

## [0084] Pre-mass-gen de-risk — log hygiene + api-error capture + reactive 429 backoff — 2026-06-25 (Nggaev-v2)

**What:** three independent, low-risk hardening fixes surfaced doing the 2026-06-24 live-run log verification, ahead of the next mass-gen. No migration; all gated-pipeline/TDD-planned.

**Shipped:**
- **`log-hygiene-1` (`b10f12c`).** `app/log.py` `_FMT` now carries the **date** (`{time:YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss.SSS}`, was time-only) and the `var/server.log` file sink rotates **daily** at midnight (`rotation="00:00"`, was 20 MB size; retention still 7 days) — so consecutive runs don't conflate across a midnight boundary and a run is greppable in isolation.
- **`api-error-capture-1` (`a84f4c9`).** A failed call now records the *real* error. New pure helper `agent._spawn_failure_message(provider, transport, rc, stderr, text)` builds a transport-aware `error_message` (says "api" not "CLI" on the SDK path + embeds the actual 429/DNS/auth preview), wired into the `run_phase` rc≠0 branch and the `summarize_lesson` failure path; both also write the raw error into `agent_usages.raw_envelope` via `extra_envelope["error"] = stderr[:2000]`. Previously the DB got a generic `"<provider> CLI exited rc=N"` and lost the real cause to `server.log`, so `/agent/stats` + cost/quality reports couldn't categorize api failures from the DB alone.
- **`concurrency-knob-1` Phase 1 — reactive 429 backoff (`90b714d` helpers + `c064040` wrapper + `b2be9aa` acceptance smoke).** `agent._spawn` is now a **thin retry-on-rate-limit wrapper**: the old single-attempt body is renamed `_spawn_once` (unchanged — still acquires the semaphore + `kill_tree` cancel handling), and `_spawn` loops, retrying a *transient* rate-limit (`_is_rate_limited` → `429`/`RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`/`rate_limit`/`overloaded_error`/`too many requests`; deliberately **not** auth 401/403 or `MAX_TOKENS`) with exponential delay + jitter (`_rate_limit_delay`) up to `settings.rate_limit_max_retries` times, then returns the failure unchanged for phase-level failover. The `asyncio.sleep` between attempts holds **no** concurrency slot. New config/env in `app/config.py`: `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_RETRIES` (4), `RATE_LIMIT_BASE_DELAY_SECONDS` (2.0), `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_DELAY_SECONDS` (30.0). This is the **reactive (Phase 1)** half; the **proactive Phase 2** shared Postgres token-bucket (fleet-combined cap, needs Vertex quota numbers) stays OPEN.

**Verified:** TDD per change; real api acceptance smoke for the 429 backoff wrapper (`b2be9aa`). Suite green except **2 env-driven judge-config test reds** — these are **config, not defect**: the local `.env` pins the judge provider/model away from the test's default expectation; they fail identically on the unmodified base and touch none of these files.

**Docs de-staled (this entry's finish):** README config table + failover bullet; `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md` §8 (`_spawn`/`_spawn_once` retry wrapper) + §11 (real-error capture); `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (`agent.py` + `log.py`); `docs/DEPLOY.md` (three `RATE_LIMIT_*` rows + daily-log-rotation note). WISHLIST: `log-hygiene-1` + `api-error-capture-1` struck shipped; `concurrency-knob-1` split into Phase-1-done / Phase-2-open.

## [0085] Cluster 5 slice 1 — launcher capability gate (fleet-serveable model picker) — 2026-06-26 (launcher-capability-gate → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** `launcher-capability-gate-1` (campaign-readiness). The launcher offered provider/transport combos the live fleet couldn't actually serve → jobs launched and sat `pending` forever (the api-job-claim-gate footgun: a `claude·api` pick with no `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` on any worker, or a provider whose CLI is installed nowhere). Now the picker **greys out unservable `(provider × transport)` picks** with a visible reason. Full subagent-driven-development (6 build tasks + finish; fresh implementer + task review per task; opus whole-branch review = Ready to merge). Migration `0035`.

**Shipped:**
- **`workers.capabilities` JSONB column** (migration `0035`, nullable — legacy/never-published rows read as "unknown").
- **Worker publishes its capability blob on the registry heartbeat.** New `worker._capability_blob(env)` → `{"cli": {<5 providers>: bool}, "api": {"claude": bool, "gemini": bool}}` (cli via `agent.provider_cli_installed`, api via a new shared `_api_capable(env)` that `_compute_capabilities` also calls — so the claim-gate readiness dict and the published view **can't drift**, locked by `test_api_capable_and_compute_capabilities_agree`). Computed once at module load as `CAPABILITY_BLOB`; `upsert_heartbeat` widened with `capabilities=` and a **no-clobber guard** (status-only beat with `capabilities=None` does NOT null a published blob). **`CAPABILITIES` + `claim_next_job` untouched** — purely additive selection-UX.
- **`aggregate_fleet_capability(session, *, stale_after_seconds)`** — union over **online** workers (same staleness predicate as `has_live_workers`, DB clock). Zero online → `{"online": False, …}` (fail-open); a NULL-caps online worker counts toward `workers_online` but contributes no true flags (banner fires only at zero online).
- **`/agent/models` gains a `fleet` block** (now DB-touching — one bounded `SELECT` over the tiny `workers` table; unauthenticated as it already was).
- **FE:** `FleetCapability` type + pure helpers (`serveability`, `providerServeableAnyMode`, `resolveRoleTransport`) in `web/src/lib/serveability.ts`. `launcher.tsx` + `routes/section.tsx` + `RoleAgentControls.tsx` grey out unservable provider options (**label-suffix, not a `title` tooltip — a disabled Radix `SelectItem` is `pointer-events-none` so a hover tooltip is dead**), gate the api transport (AND `apiSupported` with fleet creds + amber reason line), render an **offline banner** at zero online, **5s `refetchInterval`** so a worker connecting later un-greys, and reset-guards so a now-unservable selection can't submit. Per-role `inherit` resolves to the **job** transport before the serveability check (role provider may differ from job provider). Fail-open everywhere: offline/unknown fleet greys nothing.

**Locked decisions (user):** grey-out + visible reason (label-suffix); fail-open + offline banner at zero online; **provider×transport granularity** — per-model id + preview-model QUOTA explicitly out of scope (undetectable without a billed call).

**Known edge (non-blocking, opus review):** if the ONLY online workers are legacy pre-`0035` (NULL caps), every provider greys with no banner — transient (current workers publish on their first beat) + non-blocking (the selected `provider` state persists even when its dropdown item is disabled, so the launch still submits). Possible future hardening: also disable a role's `CLI` item when its cli is on no worker (today cli is advisory-only in the role select).

**Verified:** full offline suite **994 passed / 0 fail** (109 skipped); **15 real-PG capability tests green** on an isolated scratch DB (`createdb`→`alembic upgrade head`→test→`dropdb`); **one acceptance-gate regression fixed** (`87580c0` — Task 4's new `get_session` dep broke an offline `/agent/models` shape test that assumed pure-Python; fixed with a no-workers `get_session` override). `tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean. Single Alembic head `0035`. **No merge — routed to gatekeeper.**

**Docs de-staled (this finish):** `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md` (fleet capability publish/aggregate + launcher serveability gating), `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (`workers.capabilities`, `_capability_blob`/`CAPABILITY_BLOB`, `aggregate_fleet_capability`, `/agent/models` fleet block, `serveability.ts`), `docs/DATABASE.md` (`workers.capabilities` column). WISHLIST: `launcher-capability-gate-1` struck shipped.
## [0086] Cluster 6 — notion-archive-1 (R15): visible + recoverable Notion archive failures — 2026-06-26 (feat/c6-notion-archive-retry → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** A failed Notion archive push no longer vanishes (it used to leave `notion_archived_at` AND `notion_skip_reason` both NULL — indistinguishable from "never attempted"). Three layers:
- **Service** (`app/services/notion_archive.py`, `2fa273c`): the push runs through `_push_with_retry` (`_PUSH_MAX_ATTEMPTS=3`, exponential `_PUSH_BACKOFF_BASE_SECONDS` backoff). On exhaustion `_record_skip` persists `notion_skip_reason="push error: <Type>"` in a fresh session (best-effort, never raises); the outer catch-all now also records `"archive error"` instead of `log.warning`-only. Push errors are thus distinguished from logic errors, and the both-NULL invisible state can no longer arise from a push failure. Retry is safe because `_push_to_notion` skips already-populated pages and `archive_job` short-circuits when `notion_archived_at` is set.
- **API** (`app/api/v1/jobs.py`, `8b45dbc`): `POST /jobs/{id}/retry-archive` re-runs `archive_job` for a `done` + not-yet-archived job (404 missing / 409 non-done / 409 already-archived; `session.expire_all()` before `_job_out` because archive_job commits in its own `SessionLocal` → the request session's cached row is otherwise stale). The re-archive affordance R15 asked for.
- **FE** (`web/src/lib/api.ts` + `routes/job.tsx`, `7bc33f3`): `retryArchiveJob` client + a "Retry archive" button in the existing "Not archived to Notion" notice; success inferred from a cleared `notion_skip_reason` (`set_notion_archived` nulls it on success).

**Why the rest of C6 was NOT shipped (90% bar):** the cluster's "most subjects silently fail to archive" premise is **stale** — the subject-page map now has **38** `(subject|grade)` entries (not ~7), and unmapped subjects ALREADY set `notion_skip_reason` (`notion_archive.py:191-199`) and render in the FE (`job.tsx`). So R16 aliases (latent — no O'zb-history book), the crawl rewrite (greenfield, needs a brainstorm), the validator (parked), and the fe-redesign / fleet-ui items (blocked) are correctly deferred. Recorded in `REMEDIATION_CLUSTERS.md` (`d962c31`).

**Process:** gated plan (one approval) → `subagent-driven-development` (fresh subagent per task; controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran tests) → per-task reviews → opus whole-branch review (**READY TO MERGE**). Review caught one vacuous guard-test (the happy-path didn't assert `expire_all`); strengthened + RED-proved (`6528fcf`). Three review Minors cleaned (`e0dcb28`): unused `monkeypatch` param + `retryArchiveJob` JSDoc; the `assert last_exc is not None` kept (it type-narrows the following `raise`).

**Left intentionally:** `pipeline.py:307` still double-wraps `archive_job` in a bare `except` — now **redundant** (archive_job is fully best-effort internally) but harmless/defensive; left out of the `notion_archive.py`-only scope. R16 / crawl auto-resolve / validator stay open in C6.

**Verified:** TDD per task; full suite **977 passed / 99 skipped**; tsc + build clean. The 2 `test_judge_resolution.py` reds are the known config-driven (operator `.env JUDGE_MODEL`) failures, unrelated.

**Docs de-staled (this entry's finish):** ROADMAP R15 closed (shipped); WISHLIST `notion-archive-1` struck shipped; `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (`notion_archive.py` retry + `jobs.py` retry-archive endpoint); `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md` (retry-archive endpoint); plan `git mv`'d to `plans/shipped/`. No migration; no schema/deploy change.

## [0087] Cluster 7 — Judge quality residuals (self-fallback, refusal, judge_status surfacing) — 2026-06-26 (cluster-7-judge-quality → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** the three residual judge-quality gaps after cluster-3 ([0079]). No migration (`judge_status` is `String(24)`; `"refused"` fits).
- **`judge-self-fallback-1` — Option B, generator-aware fallback** (`app/services/model_tiers.py`): a *fixed* `_SELF_FALLBACK` constant can never be "non-self for any generator" — whatever it is, a generator equal to it self-matches (retargeting to claude-opus-4-7 alone just relocates the hole to a claude-opus generator). Replaced with two distinct frontier peers (`_PRIMARY_SELF_FALLBACK=claude-opus-4-7`, `_ALT_SELF_FALLBACK=gemini-3.1-pro-preview`) + `_self_fallback(resolved_gen)` returning whichever ≠ the generator → provably non-self for ANY generator. **Also fixed `worker._compute_capabilities`** (`app/services/worker.py`), which read the now-removed `_SELF_FALLBACK` at module load (would crash the worker at import): `judge_fallback_api_ok` now tracks `_self_fallback((judge_provider, judge_model))` — the `job_is_judge_pair` claim-gate (`jobs.py:342-346`) pins the generator to the judge pair, so it's a single startup value. Preserves today's behavior (default claude judge → fallback gemini = the old fixed value) and corrects the campaign config (gemini-3.1 judge → fallback claude). Rewrote the 2 ambient-`settings.judge`-dependent tests config-independent (the known reds) + added a claude-opus-generator regression test + a worker-capability test.
- **`judge-refusal-1`** (`app/services/phase_judge.py` + `pipeline.py`): a content-policy refusal makes `run_phase` raise (schema retries exhausted), and the refusal prose rides in the exception via `_failure_preview`. Added anchored `_REFUSAL_SIGNALS` + `_is_refusal` (deliberately does NOT match a verbose-but-substantive judge answer like "the output violates requirement 3", nor schema/CLI errors) + `JudgeOutcome.refused`; the `except` returns a distinct refused outcome. Pipeline: retry-once guard skips refusals (`not outcome.available and not outcome.refused`), `judge_status` compute records `"refused"` first.
- **`judge-unavailable` FE rendering + de-comingle** (`schemas/job.py` + `pipeline.py` + `web/`): `judge_status` serialized on `PhaseOut` (auto via `from_attributes`); `warnings = outcome.warnings if outcome.available else []` so infra states (`unavailable`/`refused`) keep their signal in `judge_status` only and out of `validation_warnings` (genuine content warnings survive via `available=True`). FE: `judge_status` type field + a distinct amber chip in the preview console (`types.ts` + `routes/preview.tsx`; `ok`/`null` render nothing).

**Commits (post-rebase):** `…640c999` self-fallback+worker, `874290e` refusal-detect, `5ea80f5` pipeline-refused, `1d21c72` serialize+de-comingle, `6330ab5` FE chip, `eddeb7a` acceptance smoke.

**Process:** gated plan (one approval; user chose Option B over the cheaper "keep constant + document the hole" A) → `subagent-driven-development` (fresh subagent per task; **controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran tests**). The Task-1 subagent correctly STOPPED at the `worker.py` import-crash (out of my plan's stated scope); I verified the fix was mechanical (not a design call), expanded the lane, and authorized it. Acceptance: real Vertex judge smoke (`scripts/smoke_judge_c7.py`) → `available=True, refused=False` through the new code path.

**Rebase:** cut off `origin/Nggaev-v2`; rebased twice as PRs #41 (launcher-capability-gate) and #42 (cluster-6 notion-archive) merged. #41 refactored `_compute_capabilities` (→ `_api_capable`) — hand-merged: kept #41's structure + my `_self_fallback` line. Worklog-number reconciled to **0087** (#42 took 0086).

**Verified:** TDD per task; post-rebase full suite **1018 passed / 109 skipped / 0 failed**; FE tsc clean; real api smoke PASS. The 2 `test_judge_resolution` reds noted in [0084]/[0086] are now GREEN (this fix). No migration.

**Docs de-staled (this entry's finish):** WISHLIST `judge-self-fallback-1`/`judge-refusal-1`/`judge-unavailable` co-mingle all struck shipped; `REMEDIATION_CLUSTERS.md` Cluster 7 marked ✅ SHIPPED; `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (`model_tiers` generator-aware fallback, `phase_judge` refusal, `phase_output.judge_status` adds `refused`); `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md` (judge_status `refused` + de-comingle + FE chip); plan `git mv`'d to `plans/shipped/`.
