## [0088] Cluster 8 (slice) — Notion-fetch correctness: fetch-2 + r13-fetch-1 — 2026-06-26 (cluster-8-book-ingestion → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** the two clean fetch-lane items of Cluster 8. The two expensive ones — `fetch-1` (>50 MB giants) and `extract-1`/R10 (glyph-loss `/Gxx` TOC) — were **deferred to the operator escape hatch by user decision** (both are not-clean drop-ins flagged in the backlog, each one rare known book, with working workarounds: manual shrink / re-source a cleaner-font PDF). No migration, no generation/LLM path → unit tests are the proof (no CLI smoke).
- **`fetch-2` — textbook over workbook** (`app/services/notion_fetch.py`, `d220090`): `_first_pdf_block` took the FIRST PDF in page order, so a subject page listing the workbook (`ish daftari`) before the textbook (`darslik`) made the **workbook** the book for the whole batch — a silent, whole-subject content-quality failure under mass-gen. Now ranks candidates `textbook(0) ▸ neutral(1) ▸ workbook(2)` via new pure helpers `_pdf_rank`/`_pdf_name` on the `_fold`-normalized filename, selecting `min((rank, page_index))` — **preference, not exclusion** (a workbook-only page still ingests). The bare `"daftar"` marker catches `ish_daftari.pdf` (underscore) where the spaced `"ish daftari"` wouldn't. `list_subjects`' `has_textbook` (presence-only) unchanged.
- **`r13-fetch-1` — per-book download dedup** (`app/services/book_fetch.py`, `a9391d2`+`c9bbfbe`+`da385a1`): the sync `ensure_book_pdf_sync` (called via `asyncio.to_thread`) had N concurrent lessons of one book each download the full PDF. Added a per-`book_id` `threading.Lock` (module dict guarded by a master lock) → lock-free fast path → double-checked `_cached_ok` re-check → fetch; first downloads, the rest wait then cache-hit. **All prior behavior preserved verbatim** (head-is-canonical, `r13-integrity-1` wrong-size re-fetch, raise-if-no-head); the fetch+promote block moved unchanged inside the lock. RED-proved 5 downloads → 1; `_cached_ok` tolerates a concurrent-unlink TOCTOU (`OSError`→not-cached).
- **Cross-task fix** (`95d95bd`): the lock made an out-of-diff test (`test_concurrent_same_book_fetches_use_distinct_temps`) impossible by design (both threads no longer download concurrently) — **caught by the full suite**, rewritten to assert the new serialization contract (one download, no leaked temp).

**SDD:** fresh implementer + spec/quality review per task; controller re-ran every commit + RED-proved the dedup test against pre-lock code. Opus whole-branch review **CLEAN / merge-ready** (M1 TOCTOU + M3 stale comment fixed; M2 bounded `_book_locks` dict accepted for a bounded fleet; M4 `darslik javoblari` answer-key naming = documented limitation, out of approved scope). Rebased onto `origin/Nggaev-v2` past #41/#42/#43. **No migration (single head `0035`); 1020 passed / 5 pre-existing notion-503 env fails (PROVEN pre-existing by reverting `notion_fetch.py` to base).** Closes `fetch-2` + `r13-fetch-1` (redundant-download half).

**Docs de-staled:** WISHLIST `fetch-2`/`r13-fetch-1` struck shipped (+ `fetch-1` operator-deferred note); `REMEDIATION_CLUSTERS.md` Cluster 8 items 1–2 annotated; `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (`notion_fetch` darslik-preference + `book_fetch` per-book lock); plan `git mv`'d to `plans/shipped/`. No README/DEPLOY/DATABASE change.

## [0089] Cluster 5 (resilience trio) — limited-worker backoff + session-limit autopause + net retry — 2026-06-26 (cluster-5-fleet-resilience → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** the campaign-protecting C5 host-health trio (`fleet-net-1` code-half + `fleet-limited-worker-hogs-1` + `fleet-session-limit-autopause-1`) as one workstream — makes unattended Max-CLI mass-gen survive Claude session-limits + flaky networks instead of fail-fast **vacuuming the queue**. Full subagent-driven-development (6 build tasks + finish; fresh implementer + task review per task; opus whole-branch review = code-correct). Migration `0036`.

**Shipped:**
- **`fleet-net-1` (code half):** `agent._spawn` now retries **transient connection/DNS** errors (`_TRANSIENT_NET_TERMS`: `getaddrinfo`/`NameResolution`/`HTTPSConnectionPool`/`WinError 10053/121/1232`/`connection aborted`/`timeout`) with the existing 429 backoff (sleep holds no slot). Auth/`MAX_TOKENS`/**session-limit** stay terminal — a defensive test locks the verbatim session-limit string OUT of the net terms (else the whole pause/switch path would never trigger). The Ethernet/NTP/resolver half stays OPEN (ops).
- **Session-limit detect + parse:** `failure_classifier.is_session_limit` keyed on the parseable **`resets <clock-time>` clause** (NOT a literal phrase — robust to `"usage limit reached · resets …"` phrasings that already match `_WALL`) + `parse_session_limit_reset(text, *, now)` → next-future-occurrence **UTC** (injected clock, `settings.session_limit_default_tz`). Verified against the verbatim captured string in `var/server oliver worker.log`.
- **Per-batch strategy:** `batches.session_limit_strategy` (migration `0036`, CHECK `pause|switch|inherit`) + `SESSION_LIMIT_STRATEGY` env default + `resolve_session_limit_strategy` (batch override else env) + a launcher "On limit" Select + `/jobs/batch` field (clean **400** on a bad value, mirroring `validate_transport`).
- **Failover wiring (the heart):** `_run_with_failover` checks `is_session_limit(str(exc))` **BEFORE** `failure_classifier.classify` (load-bearing — else a `"usage limit · resets"` pre-classifies `_WALL` and the pause strategy silently never fires). **switch** → treat as `wall` (budget 0 → immediate failover off claude down `failover_provider_order`); **pause** → raise `SessionLimitPause(reset_at)` (new `app/services/errors.py`), aborting the chain. The pause propagates **uncaught** through 5 guarded `except SessionLimitPause: raise` blocks to the worker.
- **Worker (the `hogs-1` fix):** catches `SessionLimitPause` → `requeue_session_limited` (status `pending`, `attempts=GREATEST(attempts-1,0)` so a session-limit does **not** burn the retry budget, `scheduled_at=now()` so a **healthy peer claims it immediately**) + **whole-worker self-cooldown** until the parsed reset (`_in_cooldown` gates `_claim_one` — skips claiming, auto-resumes; host clock w/ NTP caveat). Both strategies kill the queue-vacuum: switch → no-fail; pause → cooldown.

**Locked decisions (user):** whole-worker self-cooldown; ship BOTH strategies, operator picks per batch (env default + per-batch override); switch reuses the failover chain.

**Verified:** full offline suite **1121 passed / 0 failed**; **real-PG subset 14 passed** on an isolated scratch DB; **one acceptance-gate regression fixed** (`5cd39ec` — Task 6's rollup field broke 5 `SimpleNamespace` batch fakes → completed them, consistent with `_rollup_payload`'s direct-read convention); a **behavioral worker-cooldown test** added (`3ab775d`, bite-confirmed) to retire the source-inspection catch-ordering gap (it would pass even if `except SessionLimitPause` were mis-ordered after `except Exception` — the new test fails if swapped). `tsc` + `npm run build` clean. **Single alembic head `0036`.** **Claim-gate SQL untouched** (cooldown is in-process, before the DB call). **No merge — routed to gatekeeper.**

**Docs de-staled (this finish):** `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md` (session-limit pause/switch + worker cooldown + net retry), `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (agent net terms, `failure_classifier` session-limit, `_run_with_failover` strategy + `SessionLimitPause`, `requeue_session_limited` + worker cooldown, `batches.session_limit_strategy`), `docs/DATABASE.md` (the column), `docs/DEPLOY.md` (`SESSION_LIMIT_STRATEGY`/`SESSION_LIMIT_DEFAULT_TZ`/`SESSION_LIMIT_DEFAULT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS` env rows). WISHLIST: the 3 items struck shipped (`fleet-net-1` = **code half only**); `REMEDIATION_CLUSTERS.md` C5 marked.

## [0090] Prompt optimization port — Class-A Infra_prompts → `_general` (surgical) — 2026-06-26 (feat/prompt-optimization-port → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** ported the genuinely-portable optimizations from the external `s1gmamale1/Class-A-Education-Development` `docs/Infra_prompts` spec repo into our live runtime prompts, as **surgical enrichments — not a wholesale replace** (5 parallel reviews established the source files are production specs for a *different* pipeline: JSON I/O, scoring economies, CBP-case expansions, "retrieve from address"; our `_general` + `prompts.py` injection were already a tighter distillation of the same lineage). Architecture unchanged (collapsed `{{SUBJECT}}` + `prompts.py` `FAMILY_RULES`/`LANGUAGE_RULES` injection). No new phases (Listening/Video/Quizlet-modes deferred). **No migration.**

- **Confirmed bug fix:** `flashcards.md` sized the deck off `Mode: Easy or Hard`, but `Mode` is never substituted (`get_prompt` does only `{{SUBJECT}}`/`{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}`/`{{FAMILY_RULES}}`) and the pipeline pins `difficulty=None` (classify removed, 0067) — a dead variable. Replaced with grade-band sizing (G5-6→6-8 / G7-8→8-10 / G9-11→10-12). (`cf2630e`)
- **Per-phase enrichments:** flashcards `source|inferred` provenance + union card types (`formula`/`grammar`/`vocabulary`) + `_FC_DEFAULT` fidelity line (`cf2630e`); CBP common-mistake distractor + answer-shape anti-leak + 4-part feedback + DPE rubric + "Needs Retry" (`179dfba`); error-detection per-pattern block counts (eqn/sentence/diagram split) + no-time-pressure + over-strict guard, dropped the `diagram_svg` SVG trap (`aa1794e`); RLC confidence-pattern feedback steer (Sure+wrong / Guess+right) + worked example (`1a63b6f`); memory-match +4 pair types, jigsaw/tictactoe distractor flavors, sentence kept CLOZE (`562436a`); boss soft grade→count steer + Pythagorean worked example + counterfactual "What", memory-check grade-calibrated distractors + no-consecutive (kept per-item feedback), reflection +2 perf-tied questions + "Needs Retry" + next-step (`07b9e36`); `_LANG_UZBEK` register/simplify-not-substance/calque/gloss/apostrophe rules (`dc8d9a7`).
- **Dropped everywhere** (contract-breakers): JSON schemas, scoring economies (HP/damage/100-300pt/XP), runtime/telemetry adaptation, pool minimums, Bloom/PISA tags, SVG fields, retrieve-from-address, per-family pinning. The Uzbek source is "boss-review, not ratified" → ported intent only, not the validator/confidence machinery.

**Process:** gated plan (one approval; user-locked: keep collapsed `{{SUBJECT}}`, port-only-the-11, keep Sentence as cloze) → 5 parallel reviews → `subagent-driven-development` (fresh subagent per task; **controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran the render gate**). New `scripts/check_prompt_render.py` no-LLM gate (55 subject×phase combos, zero leftover `{{...}}`) ran on every task.

**Verified:** render gate green; full suite **1122 passed / 115 skipped / 0 failed**; **real Vertex api smoke** (`scripts/smoke_prompt_port.py`, 3 phases) shows the optimizations live — grade-7 flashcards sized 7 cards with the new `formula` type, CBP placeholder + MCQ checkpoint, boss "What" firing the new counterfactual. No reference-doc de-stale needed (structure unchanged — `CODE_MAP`/`HOW_IT_WORKS` describe `prompts.py` shape + flow, both intact; the docs' "no easy/hard split" was already correct, which is exactly why the flashcards `Mode` was dead). Held un-merged pending the gate + the port-first campaign-ordering call.

**Grade wiring (gate-found gap, coupled in — `fe7255c`):** the gate caught that the ported grade-band rules had nothing to read — `book.grade` never reached content generation (`summarize_lesson` takes no grade; `lesson_context` was extract-output only; no `{{GRADE}}` token; nothing in `pipeline` injected it). The earlier prompt-port smoke passed only because it hand-fed "grade 7" into the lesson text — which masked the gap. **Fix (Option A):** `pipeline._inject_grade(lesson_context, grade)` prepends `"Student grade level: {grade}"` at the single fresh+resume convergence point (`pipeline.py:~277`), with `book_grade` captured inside the session block (no detached-ORM access); no-op on null grade; no `get_prompt`/prompt change. One signal feeds the whole grade-calibration theme (flashcards sizing, CBP reasoning-load, error-detection block counts, memory-check, boss question-count). **Acceptance (`scripts/smoke_grade_injection.py`, real Vertex, grade-free body so grade comes ONLY from the injection):** deck tracks grade — **grade 5 → 7 cards (G5-6 band), grade 11 → 12 cards (G9-11 band)** — would have failed before the wiring. Unit tests `test_pipeline_grade_injection.py` (prepend / null no-op / None-context no-op). The port and the wiring merge as one functioning unit.

## [0091] TOC extraction over Vertex API (no gemini-CLI OAuth) — 2026-06-26 (extract-toc-api → Nggaev-v2)

**Problem:** book-upload TOC extraction failed on a headless all-Vertex head with `FatalCancellationError: Authentication cancelled by user (initOauthClient)`. Root cause: book upload is a **job-less** gemini spawn → `_auth_env`'s cli baseline scrubs all gemini auth → the gemini CLI falls back to built-in OAuth (Code Assist), which isn't logged in on a head that only has Vertex service-account creds. So text-PDF uploads couldn't get a TOC at all.

**Fix (mirrors the `summarize_lesson` local-text→api pattern):** route the **text-usable** TOC read over the Vertex SDK. The text path already existed — `extract_toc` reads front/back page text locally into `lesson_context` and already dropped the PDF attachment for non-gemini/oversize; it just never ran over `transport="api"`. Three commits:
- `e603535` — new `EXTRACT_TOC_TRANSPORT` setting (`config.py`, `cli` default | `api`; validator mirrors `_blank_extract_provider_to_default`, additionally **rejects non-`cli|api` loudly** — a typo'd value silently running cli is the exact footgun the all-Vertex operator hits).
- `fb82051` — **the heart:** in the text-usable branch, AND `transport != "api"` into `keep_pdf` (`agent.py:1393`) so api sends `attachments=[]`. This is **mandatory, not cosmetic**: `api_transport.generate` raises `NotImplementedError("api transport is text-only in v1")` on any attachment — an api call with the PDF still attached would crash. The local TOC text rides in as `lesson_context` (set before the keep_pdf block), so nothing is lost. **Vision/scanned stays cli unconditionally** (`agent.py:1376`) — a scanned book launched `api` still vision-OCRs via cli (the SDK path is text-only); asserted by a dedicated test.
- `269ebff` — `toc_extractor.run` forwards `settings.extract_toc_transport` into `extract_toc` (was hardcoded cli default) + logs the chosen transport.

`_auth_env` is **not** bypassed (hard rule): the change only sets `transport`/`attachments`; the spawn flows `_spawn → _spawn_once → api_transport.generate` with `_auth_env`'s api/Vertex branch granting the SA creds. **No migration.**

**Process:** gated plan (one approval) → `subagent-driven-development` (fresh subagent per task; **controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran tests**). RED proved for each task.

**Verified:** full suite **1133 passed / 115 skipped / 0 failed**. **Real Vertex acceptance smoke** (`scripts/smoke_toc_api.py`, `f1a54f4`) re-extracted the previously-failing text book `860e86aa` over `transport=api`: **64 TOC entries via the text path, no `initOauthClient`**, and the `agent_usages` row recorded **`auth_mode='api'`** (`gemini/gemini-2.5-flash`, success) — the bug (the OAuth call) is provably gone. De-staled `README.md` (env table), `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md` (PDF-handling gotcha), `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (`toc_extractor.py`), `.env.example`. **Out of scope (noted):** scanned books still need a working gemini-CLI login — closing that needs an api image path.

## [0092] Persist launcher selections across navigation (launcher-persist-selections-1) — 2026-06-26 (launcher-persist-selections → Nggaev-v2)

**Problem:** the Fleet launcher's per-book card (`ReadyCard`, `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx`) held all 10 selections (provider/model/transport/extract·judge role transports+provider+model/on-limit) as plain `useState` with hardcoded defaults; navigating away unmounted the card → state discarded → remount re-initialized to defaults. No persistence, no read-back. FE-only, no migration.

**Fix:**
- `ac023a0` — new pure module `web/src/lib/launcher-config.ts`: `loadLauncherConfig(bookId): Partial<LauncherConfig>` / `saveLauncherConfig(bookId, cfg)`, keyed `launcher-config:<book_id>`, all access try/catch → `{}`/no-op (private mode, quota, corrupt JSON). Returns a `Partial` so the component merges onto its own defaults (forward-compat when fields are added).
- `7522a63` — `ReadyCard`: seed the 10 selection `useState`s from `loadLauncherConfig(book.id)` via lazy initializers (`saved.X ?? default`, no flash); a `useEffect` persists the 10 fields on change (never ephemeral UI state — `expanded`/`choosing`/`selected`/`launching`). **Load-bearing guards:** the apiSupported reset (`:639`) and model-seeding (`:663`) effects both treat "manifest not loaded yet" (`modelsQ.data` undefined) as "unsupported" → on a cold load they'd demote a restored `api` pick to `cli` and null+re-seed a restored model to the wrong one. Added `if (!modelsQ.data) return` to both (+ `modelsQ.data` to the first's deps) so validation acts on **loaded** data, not the undefined-default — restore THEN validate. **Not a gate bypass:** once the manifest+fleet load, an unservable restored pick is still reset/greyed exactly as before (serveability greying is render-time + `missingApiModel` still blocks an api launch with null model); this also fixes a latent bug where the default `transport="api"` transiently collapsed to `cli` on every cold refresh. The provider-reset effect (`:651`) was already fleet-guarded — left untouched.

**Process:** gated plan (one approval; gate verified all three clobber claims against source) → `subagent-driven-development` (fresh subagent per task; **controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran tsc/build**).

**Verified:** no FE test runner exists → structural gate `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` + `vite build` both **clean** (only the pre-existing >500 kB chunk advisory). Storage-layer behavioral test (tsx + localStorage shim, 6/6): round-trip, two-books-independent, separate per-`book_id` keys, unknown→`{}`, corrupt-JSON→`{}`, partial-blob forward-compat. The React-integration behaviors (navigate-away/return, **hard-refresh** [the case the guards make pass], unservable-greys, two-books-separate in-browser) are the gatekeeper's in-browser acceptance per agreement (controller can't faithfully drive a browser headlessly). De-staled `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (`lib/` + launcher capability-gate lines); no `README`/`HOW_IT_WORKS` change (no launcher-selection prose there).

## [0093] Monitor card clarity: honest transport chip + per-transport split note (monitor-card-clarity-1) — 2026-06-26 (monitor-card-clarity → Nggaev-v2)

**Problem:** on the monitor's multi-transport batch card (`web/src/components/fleet/batch-funnel.tsx`): (1) the per-transport status chip was binary `batch.complete ? "complete" : "in progress"`, and the server `complete` flag means "all jobs terminal" (`app/api/v1/batch.py:71-76`) — also true when **everything failed**, so an all-failed transport (e.g. English-CLI 22 failed/0 done) showed a **green "complete"** chip contradicting the red RollupBar below it. (2) A book split across CLI+API showed only `CLI + API · N transports` — no combined view, forcing mental math. FE-only, no migration; server `complete` flag + RollupBar untouched.

**Fix:**
- `8862fee` — **Item 1, outcome-aware chip:** new pure module `web/src/lib/batch-status.ts` (`RowStatus` + `transportRowStatus(b)`): `!complete`→`in_progress`; complete + no failed/cancelled→`complete` (green); complete + 0 done→`failed` (red); else→`partial` (amber). Placed in `lib/` (not inline in the `.tsx` per the spec sketch) — matches the repo's pure-helper pattern (`serveability.ts`/`launcher-config.ts`) and makes it reliably tsx-testable; presentational `ROW_CHIP` style map stays in the component. Chip now renders the 4-way verdict.
- `39beffc` — **Item 2(a), per-transport done note:** the multi-transport header appends `done {batches.map(b => `${b.rollup.done ?? 0} ${b.transport.toUpperCase()}`).join(" · ")}` → e.g. `CLI + API · 2 transports · done 18 CLI · 13 API`. **Never a summed total** — a lesson can be done on both transports (real overlap, book `62865c70`: cli 1 + api 2 > 2 distinct), so a sum over-counts >100%. Single-transport cards unchanged.

**Out of scope (item 2(b), logged to R18):** the accurate combined total (`X/31` = distinct lessons done on *any* transport) needs a backend book-level per-lesson **dedup** rollup — same dependency as the Fleet-tray "Complete" split. R18 refreshed to note this.

**Process:** gated plan (one approval; gate traced every `transportRowStatus` branch + approved the lib/ placement deviation) → `subagent-driven-development` (fresh subagent per task; **controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran tsc/build**).

**Verified:** no FE test runner → `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` + `vite build` both **clean** (pre-existing >500 kB chunk advisory only). Pure-helper behavioral test (tsx, **7/7**): pending/running→`in_progress`, 22-failed/0-done→`failed`, 18-done/13-failed→`partial`, all-done→`complete`, done+cancelled→`partial`, all-cancelled/0-done→`failed`. The in-browser chip/header eyeball (English-CLI red, Algebra amber, clean-batch green, multi-transport `done X CLI · Y API` no >100%) is the gatekeeper's per agreement. De-staled `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (batch-funnel chip note); refreshed `docs/memory/ROADMAP.md` R18.

## [0094] api-vision-1 — gemini vision over Vertex (scanned-book TOC + lesson-extract) — 2026-06-26 (api-vision-1 → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** scanned / image-only books (no usable text layer) extract their TOC + lessons through the **vision** branch, which previously hardcoded `transport="cli"`. On an all-Vertex head the gemini CLI hits `initOauthClient` and fails — so the `EXTRACT_TOC_TRANSPORT=api` fix (0091) only ever helped *text* books. This closes the gap: gemini vision now goes over the **Vertex API** (multimodal), no CLI/OAuth.
- **`api_transport` multimodal for gemini** (`api_transport.py`, `409ee06`+`634a2e9`): `generate` routes gemini *before* the attachment guard (claude + unknown-provider + attachments still raise — guard relocated, not removed); `_gemini(model, prompt, attachments=())` builds `types.Part.from_bytes(data=…, mime_type=_mime_for(a))` and calls `generate_content(contents=[prompt, *parts])`. Text-only path byte-identical (bare-string `contents`); usage/finish parsing unchanged. `_mime_for` by suffix (PDF/PNG/JPEG, default PDF, case-insensitive).
- **`extract_toc` vision keeps api for gemini** (`agent.py`, `9a795dd`): the vision branch forces cli only `if not (transport == "api" and provider == "gemini")` — so a gemini+api scanned book attaches the front+back TOC window over Vertex. The **text** branch's `keep_pdf` (drops the PDF under api, uses local text) is deliberately untouched.
- **`summarize_lesson_vision` + pipeline** (`agent.py`+`pipeline.py`, `4baa41b`+`45d1fde`): the function gains `transport: str = "cli"` (default preserves callers), used at the `_spawn` call AND `auth_mode` (correct `$` attribution); pipeline computes `vision_transport = "api" if (gemini and api) else "cli"`, warning only on a genuine api→cli downgrade (claude+api). The failure `error_message` + raise now use the transport-aware `_spawn_failure_message` (was the stale "CLI exited rc=N" — misleading for an api 429/DNS failure).
- **Out-of-diff fix** (`9bb6341`): `test_extract_toc_api`'s scanned case asserted the old "force cli" contract → updated to gemini-keeps-api (caught by the full suite).

**Cred path (verified):** `transport="api"` returns early in `_spawn` → `api_transport._gemini_client()` reads the Vertex SA straight from `os.environ` (GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS + GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT) — NOT via `_auth_env`/`_spawn_once`. Matches CLAUDE.md (api creds from `os.environ`).

**SDD + acceptance:** fresh implementer + spec/quality review per task; controller re-ran & RED-proved each (T2 `cli!=api`, T3 pipeline `KeyError`, the error_message test, the out-of-diff test). Opus whole-branch review **CLEAN / merge-ready** (Important error_message fixed; 2 cosmetic minors fixed in `ebb0003`). **Binding acceptance — REAL Vertex multimodal smoke PASSED** (`scripts/smoke_api_vision.py`): `_gemini("gemini-2.5-flash", prompt, attachments=[1-page PDF])` over Vertex (no `GEMINI_API_KEY`) → rc=0, **1315 prompt tokens** (PDF ingested as image), returned the page content. **No migration; 1139 passed / 5 pre-existing notion-503 env.** google-genai 2.8.0.

**Follow-up (gatekeeper/user):** the binding full-book confirmation — re-extract scanned book `e020d51f` with `EXTRACT_PROVIDER=gemini` + `EXTRACT_TOC_TRANSPORT=api` → valid `ExtractedTOC`, no `initOauthClient`, `agent_usages.auth_mode='api'`. (Window size pre-cleared: e020d51f 32-page TOC window = 5.43 MB, under the ~20 MB Vertex inline limit. A future >20 MB window would need the Files API — noted, out of scope.)

**Docs de-staled:** `api_transport` "text-only v1" docstring; `docs/CODE_MAP.md` (`api_transport` multimodal, `toc_extractor` scanned note); `docs/HOW_IT_WORKS.md` (api attachments, scanned TOC + lesson vision now api-capable for gemini); `README.md` (`EXTRACT_TOC_TRANSPORT` scanned note). Plan `git mv`'d to `plans/shipped/`.

## [0095] fetch-1 — raise ingest cap to 250MB + de-conflate oversize reject messages — 2026-06-27 (feat/fetch-1-ingest-cap → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** the 50 MB upload/notion-fetch reject was the only wall stopping >50 MB textbooks (the real book that motivated this was **67.5 MB**) from being ingested. It was **never an LLM limit** — an Explore audit confirmed every downstream PDF read is bounded (TOC = 60 KB text excerpt or a front/back vision window dropped >20 MB; lesson-extract = 600 K-char text budget or a `_subset_pdf` page window; fleet transfer streams chunk-by-chunk), so total book size never reaches a model. The 50 MB cap was therefore a pure ingest/RAM guard with no LLM rationale left. **Shrink was rejected** — no Ghostscript/pikepdf/PyMuPDF installed (only `pypdf`), adding one is a fleet-wide native dep, and image-downsampling is *lossy* for scanned pages the vision extractor must read.
- **`max_file_mb` default 50 → 250** (`config.py`) — textbook-sized (covers heavy scanned Uzbek textbooks with headroom), **not 600**: 600 × concurrent uploads would create a latent OOM (whole body is read + sha256'd before write), which is exactly what would *force* the streaming rework; 250 keeps per-upload RAM bounded. Still env-overridable via `MAX_FILE_MB`.
- **De-conflated both reject messages** (`books.py` upload 413 + notion-fetch 422): now name `MAX_FILE_MB` as the lever and state the cap is an ingest/RAM guard, not a model limit — dropping the stale, misleading **"shrink and upload manually"** (the message that burned the operator on the 67.5 MB book).

**Scope decision (user-locked):** Option 1 (config + proof), cap 250. Streaming-to-disk + incremental sha256 (only matters in the 300 MB+/high-concurrency regime) → **WISHLIST follow-up**, not now.

**Acceptance — REAL smoke PASSED** (`scripts/smoke_fetch1_giant.py`, no LLM/DB/$): synthetic 54.4 MB PDF (largest local book doubled) → accepted under the new 250 default (rejected under old 50); `pdf_page_count`=352, `read_whole_book_text`=30 140 chars (scanned→sparse, bounded ~600 K), `_subset_pdf`=1.8 MB window, `_toc_source_pdf`=5.7 MB window (<20 MB Vertex inline), **peak RSS 752 MB, no OOM**. Verified-fact: the controller-direct path (CLAUDE.md small-clear-fix exception) — root cause verified, low risk; TDD (3 tests RED→GREEN: cap default, both messages), real smoke, full finish.

**No migration; 1145 passed / 115 skipped** (the lone `test_default_is_cli` failure is the operator's un-reverted `.env` `EXTRACT_TOC_TRANSPORT=api` detour — proven by removing `.env` → passes; not this change).

**Observations logged (WISHLIST, not fixed here):** (a) streaming-ingest follow-up (above); (b) `read_whole_book_text` on a 352-page scanned book takes **~106 s** before the pipeline detects sparse-text and falls to the vision window — a pre-existing per-lesson cost on large scanned books (my change only raises the cap), a perf candidate.

**Operator follow-up:** the live head's `.env` pins `MAX_FILE_MB=50` — raise it to 250 (or drop the line to inherit the new default) before ingesting the 67.5 MB book. Binding real-book proof: upload/from-notion the 67.5 MB textbook → 201 (not 413/422) → TOC + one lesson extract succeed.

**BINDING REAL-BOOK PROOF — PASSED 2026-06-27 (operator raised `MAX_FILE_MB=250` + restarted head):** the actual book was **`7-sinf Geometriya 2022 (@elekton_darslikbot).pdf` = 67.5 MB, grade 7, `geometriya-g7-11`** (book `6c9a29c6-52e8-45d4-813d-d68f1af1a1dc`; NOT the small 3.8 MB grade-8 2019 glyph-loss book `860e86aa`). (a) **Ingest:** accepted under the new cap → `toc_ready`, **26 TOC entries**. (b) **Lesson generation** (job `fca13784`, gemini / transport=api): lesson **extract** ran on the 67.5 MB book over Vertex API at **147 K prompt tokens** (gemini-2.5-flash) `success=t` — the bounded read working as designed (full 67.5 MB never reached a model, no OOM); content phases (gemini-3-flash-preview) + judge (gemini-3.1-pro-preview) all `success=t`, all `auth_mode=api`. **Job completed `done`: 12/12 phases done, 0 failed; 31 `agent_usages` rows, 31/31 `success=t`, all `auth_mode=api`, 324 K prompt + 110 K output tokens.** fetch-1 verified end-to-end on the real book across ingest → extract → content/judge — no OOM, no size-related failure anywhere.

**Docs:** reference docs not stale — `CODE_MAP.md` references `max_file_mb` generically (still accurate); no hardcoded "50 MB" or "shrink" text in README/HOW_IT_WORKS. Controller-direct (no plan file to `git mv`).

## [0096] Notion archive — always file under "Generated Homeworks"; drop human-page matching — 2026-06-27 (feat/notion-generated-homeworks → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** `notion_archive._push_to_notion` used to call `match_lesson(lesson_title, get_child_pages(subject))` to **adopt** a human-built Notion lesson page (strict unique content-word superset), falling back to an app-owned `"Generated Lessons"` container on a miss — scattering homeworks unpredictably. Per the gatekeeper plan (2 user-confirmed changes): (1) **stop adopting human pages entirely** — always use the container path; (2) **rename** the container `"Generated Lessons"` → `"Generated Homeworks"`. Every homework now files unconditionally under one predictable app-owned bucket per subject.
- **`notion_archive.py`:** replaced the `human_pages`/`match_lesson`/`if hit … else …` branch with the unconditional 3-line path `find_or_create(subject, CONTAINER_TITLE)` → `find_or_create(container, lesson_title)` → `find_or_create(lesson, "Homework")`; dropped the `import … lesson_match`; `CONTAINER_TITLE = "Generated Homeworks"` now a module constant in `notion_archive.py`; both docstrings updated. Idempotency unchanged (find-or-create by title). Bonus: one fewer Notion API call per archive (no `get_child_pages` pre-scan).
- **Deleted dead code:** `app/services/notion/lesson_match.py` + `tests/services/test_lesson_match.py` (matcher had no importer outside that one call). grep across `app/`+`tests/` clean.
- **Tests rewritten** (`test_notion_archive.py`): dropped the human-page-adoption test; added `test_push_ignores_matching_human_child_always_routes_via_container` (even when a subject child's title **equals** the lesson title, the archive still routes via `"Generated Homeworks"` AND `get_child_pages.assert_not_called()`) + `test_push_unconditional_container_path`; renamed the existing `"Generated Lessons"` call-arg assertions to `"Generated Homeworks"`; removed now-obsolete `get_child_pages.return_value=[]` setup from the 3 untouched tests.

**Workflow:** gatekeeper-authored plan (already had `## Approach & key decisions` + TDD) handed to me as implementer → I pre-flight-verified every code claim against the live source (all accurate, line refs hadn't drifted past my 0086 work) → subagent-driven-development (one implementer, TDD RED→GREEN). **Controller stress-test caught an out-of-scope edit** (implementer normalized a curly `O‘zbekiston` apostrophe → straight in an unrelated subject-page test whose purpose is curly-variant folding) → reverted; squashed into one clean commit. Independent task review: **spec ✅ / quality Approved** (only Minor = the noise commit, now squashed away).

**Acceptance:** mock-client archive proves the unconditional `Generated Homeworks → <lesson> → Homework` tree even when a matching human child exists; `get_child_pages` never called. **No migration; full suite 1134 passed** (lone `test_default_is_cli` fail = operator `.env` `EXTRACT_TOC_TRANSPORT=api` artifact, not this change).

**Out of scope — gatekeeper-run AFTER merge:** a one-time Notion script renaming existing `"Generated Lessons"` pages → `"Generated Homeworks"` so past homeworks unify under the new name (else each subject carries both). NOT scripted here.

**Docs de-staled:** `CODE_MAP.md` (`notion/` section — dropped the `lesson_match.py` bullet, noted the unconditional `"Generated Homeworks"` container). Plan `git mv`'d to `plans/shipped/`.

## [0097] Global launch defaults — DB-managed model selection, retire .env model vars — 2026-06-27 (feat/global-launch-defaults)

**What:** Moved judge/extract/TOC model selection out of `.env`/`settings` into a DB-backed singleton (`launch_defaults`, id=1 CHECK, migration `0037_launch_defaults`), seeded with gemini/gemini-2.5-flash/inherit/cli defaults and an **unconditional backfill** of every existing job's NULL judge/extract columns. Launch endpoints (`batch.py`/`jobs.py`) resolve each role (explicit pick → else global default) and **stamp concrete provider/model** onto each job + batch row at launch time; changing a default never mutates already-launched jobs. Runtime readers (`pipeline.py`, `model_tiers.py`, `toc_extractor.py`, the claim gate in `repositories/jobs.py`, `worker.py`) **stopped reading** `settings.judge_provider/judge_model/extract_provider/extract_model/extract_toc_transport` — those 5 fields were DELETED from `app/config.py`. Model selection now lives ONLY in the DB row, edited at a new `/settings` SPA page (`GET`/`PUT /api/v1/settings/launch-defaults`). Claim gate rebuilt as **job-column-based + credential-only** (reads stamped `job.judge_provider` directly; worker capability is `{can_claude_api, can_gemini_api}`; no COALESCE with deleted settings).

Gate-hardening: `PUT /settings/launch-defaults` rejects null provider/model with HTTP 422; a `toc_transport=api` guard confirms the Vertex SDK path is wired; two FE guards ensure the "Auto" option is never selectable with a null pair. Deferred to WISHLIST `claim-gate-null-judge-hardening`: loud-fail non-NULL assert on `jobs_repo.create` + claim-gate fallback handling for legacy NULL rows.

**Why:** The old `.env`-based model pins (`EXTRACT_PROVIDER`/`EXTRACT_MODEL`/`JUDGE_PROVIDER`/`JUDGE_MODEL`/`EXTRACT_TOC_TRANSPORT`) required a server restart and operator access to change; they were invisible to the operator console and not tracked per-job. The `/settings` page makes model selection self-service, DB-tracked, and visible — and gives jobs complete self-describing provenance (`agent_usages.model_name` now reflects a launch-time decision, not a runtime config that may have drifted).

**Files:** `app/models/launch_defaults.py` (new), `app/repositories/launch_defaults.py` (new), `alembic/versions/0037_launch_defaults.py` (new — create table + seed row + unconditional NULL-column backfill), `app/api/v1/settings.py` (new — GET/PUT launch-defaults), `web/src/routes/settings.tsx` (new — `/settings` page), `app/services/agent_models.py` (`resolve_role_selection`/`resolve_role_transport_default` helpers), `app/api/v1/batch.py` + `jobs.py` (resolve at launch + stamp), `app/api/v1/__init__.py`, `app/services/pipeline.py`, `app/services/model_tiers.py`, `app/services/toc_extractor.py`, `app/repositories/jobs.py` (claim gate v3), `app/services/worker.py` (`_compute_capabilities` → credential-only), `app/config.py` (deleted 5 fields + 2 validators).

**Verified:** Full suite **1130 passed (non-DB) / 1277 with RUN_DB_INTEGRATION=1** on a real Postgres scratch DB (`edu_gld_test`). Controller caught a **use-before-def `_ld` bug** in `pipeline.run` that all prior tests missed → fixed + regression test added (RED on the bug, GREEN after fix). An independent **opus adversarial review** of the claim-gate SQL found it **SOUND** (no vacuous-assertion gaps, no silent unclaim paths). Whole-branch review caught and fixed: a null-global-default 500 footgun (PUT now 422s null provider/model), a `toc_transport=api` guard, and two acceptance smoke scripts reading deleted settings. **Real claude judge acceptance smoke:** set judge_provider=`claude`/judge_model=`claude-opus-4-7` at `/settings` → homework job completed → `agent_usages.model_name='claude-opus-4-7'` confirmed — the UI default reached generation.

**Notes:** Branch rebased onto `origin/Nggaev-v2` after PR #52 (worklogs 0095+0096) merged. The seed `toc_transport='cli'` is **NOT behavior-neutral on an all-Vertex head**: an operator with only Vertex SA creds and no gemini CLI OAuth must flip `toc_transport→api` at `/settings` after first deploy, or TOC extraction fails. Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.

## [0099] Multi-language output (UZ/EN/RU medium of instruction) — 2026-06-29 (feat/multi-language-output → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** added a concrete **output_language** (medium of instruction) axis: `uz` (Uzbek), `en` (English), `ru` (Russian). Before this, every homework was generated in Uzbek with no operator choice. Now:
- **Global default** at `/settings` (`launch_defaults.output_language`, server_default `'uz'`).
- **Per-launch override** on both `POST /jobs/batch` and `POST …/sections/{toc}/generate` (`output_language` field; `null` ⇒ inherit the global default).
- **Byte-identical UZ:** `MEDIUM_RULES["uz"]` is the old `_LANG_UZBEK` constant — no content change for the default path; proven by smoke.

**Prompt seam (`app/services/prompts.py`):** `get_prompt(subject, phase, provider_suffix="", output_language="uz")` now resolves `{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}` via `_resolve_language_rule(subject, output_language)` against `MEDIUM_RULES` (three blocks: `uz` = formal "Siz" Uzbek / `en` = English-medium, Uzbek "Siz" phrasing where obligatory / `ru` = Russian-medium, formal "Вы"). **Critical L2 carve-out:** the two language-class subjects whose `subjects.language ∈ {english, russian}` keep their existing **Uzbek-bridged L2 rule** regardless of the chosen medium. "Medium" switches every *other* subject — a Russian-medium school's math class generates in Russian, but its English class keeps the Uzbek-scaffolded L2 rule unchanged. The bridged-L2-follows-medium follow-up is logged to WISHLIST (`l2-bridge-follows-medium`).

**Domain helpers (`app/services/agent_models.py`):** `OUTPUT_LANGUAGES = {"uz", "en", "ru"}`, `validate_output_language(v)`, `resolve_output_language(explicit, global_default)`.

**DB (migration `0038_output_language`):** `output_language` column (String(16) NOT NULL server_default `'uz'`, DB CHECK `uz|en|ru`) added to `homework_jobs`, `batches`, and `launch_defaults`. The `batches` UNIQUE constraint was upgraded: `uq_batches_book_id_transport` → **`uq_batches_book_id_transport_output_language`** (`(book_id, transport, output_language)`) — a different-language re-launch forks a new batch (same semantics as a different-transport re-launch; prevents an EN batch from being treated as a UZ batch).

**Language scoping in repos:** `batches_repo.get_or_create_for_book`, `jobs.create`, `batches_repo.find_active_for_section`, and `batches_repo.latest_for_section` are all language-scoped. An EN re-launch never adopts or resumes a UZ job.

**Pipeline threading:** `pipeline.run` captures `job.output_language` once at the start and threads it to every `get_prompt` / `get_prompt_hash` call for content phases, and to all three judge call sites → `phase_judge.judge(output_language=...)` → its internal `get_prompt`. Generator and judge always use the **same-language** prompt contract — a judge can't grade an EN-medium homework against a UZ contract.

**Extract phase:** UNCHANGED (language-neutral; the extract cache key / `prompt_hash` is untouched — extract reuse is cross-language, which is correct since the extract is a raw factual distillation of the source PDF).

**Claim gate / worker capabilities:** UNCHANGED (output_language doesn't affect auth or provider routing).

**FE:** Settings page gains a concrete **Output Language** select (uz/en/ru). Fleet launcher (`ReadyCard`) and the single-section launcher gain an inherit-defaulted select that omits the field when inheriting (same pattern as per-role transport overrides).

**Acceptance:** real claude smoke `scripts/smoke_output_language.py` — matematika uz → Uzbek "Siz", en → English prose, ru → Russian Cyrillic; english-subject under ru → stays Uzbek-bridged L2 (carve-out verified). **Full DB suite green.** Migration 0038 applied clean. `uq_batches_book_id_transport_output_language` constraint in place; DB CHECK verified.

## [0098] Claim jobs in ascending lesson order (fix randomized lesson generation) — 2026-06-29 (feat/claim-order-lesson-ascending)

**What:** Added an ascending lesson-order tiebreaker to `claim_next_job`'s pick `ORDER BY` in `app/repositories/jobs.py`. Old clause `priority DESC, scheduled_at ASC` → new `priority DESC, lesson_order ASC, scheduled_at ASC`, where `lesson_order` is a **correlated scalar subquery** `select(TOCEntry.order_index).where(TOCEntry.id == HomeworkJob.toc_entry_id).scalar_subquery()`. Imported `TOCEntry` into `jobs.py`; docstring "Order:" note updated.

**Why:** Within a batch every job is launched together → same `priority` and ~identical `scheduled_at` → the tiebreak collapsed and `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` returned an **arbitrary** pending row, so workers claimed lessons in effectively random order. Notion orders child pages by creation time, so lessons archived scrambled. `toc_entries.order_index` (non-nullable `Integer`, already indexed `(book_id, order_index)`) is the canonical lesson sequence; ordering the pick by it makes worker A take lesson 1, worker B skip the locked row and take lesson 2, etc. → ascending claims across the fleet.

**Mechanism decision — scalar subquery, NOT a JOIN.** A JOIN would drag `toc_entries` into the `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` lock/skip-locked semantics; the correlated scalar subquery keeps the lock scope on `homework_jobs` only (verified in review). `toc_entry_id` is `nullable=False`, so the subquery never returns NULL in practice — the `.asc()` (Postgres NULLS LAST) is correct defensive ordering regardless.

**Files:** `app/repositories/jobs.py` (order clause + `TOCEntry` import + docstring), `tests/integration/test_claim_order.py` (new, real-DB).

**Verified:** New scratch-DB test seeds 6 jobs in scrambled insert order `[3,0,5,1,4,2]` (same priority+scheduled_at, one batch) and asserts repeated `claim_next_job` returns lessons `[0,1,2,3,4,5]`. **Controller re-ran the suite on a fresh scratch DB: 15 passed** (`test_claim_order` + `test_claim_gate_self_grade` + `test_claim_contention` — the tiebreaker doesn't disturb the capability gate or contention). **Controller-independent bite-prove:** stripping `lesson_order.asc()` → the new test FAILS with `Got: [3,0,5,1,4,2]`; restored → green. SDD (fresh implementer + task review): SPEC ✅, QUALITY Approved; 2 Minor (cosmetic `_FENCE_ATTEMPTS` comment, conservative test foreign-job guard) deferred. Out of scope: re-ordering already-archived Notion pages (only future claims are affected).

## [0100] Launch config centralization — Content launch-default + Fleet launcher simplification — 2026-06-29 (feat/launch-config-centralization → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Two UI changes and a bug fix that complete the launch-config ownership split: Settings owns all role defaults; the Fleet launcher owns only the content generator.

- **#2 — Content in Settings (`launch_defaults`):** Migration `0039_launch_defaults_content` adds `content_provider`, `content_model`, and `content_transport` to the `launch_defaults` singleton (chained off `0038_output_language` — rebased after the multi-language PR #57 merged). Seeded `gemini`/`gemini-2.5-pro`/`inherit`. The seed is **deliberately different** from the judge default (`gemini-2.5-flash`) to avoid the self-grade guard tripping on an all-gemini fleet. The `/settings` page (`web/src/routes/settings.tsx`) gained a **Content** row with a cli|api transport picker (matches the existing Judge/Extract rows); the `GET`/`PUT /api/v1/settings/launch-defaults` endpoints carry the three new fields.

- **#3 — Fleet launcher simplified:** `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx` now configures **only the content generator** (provider/model/transport + session-limit strategy), seeded from the global content default. The Judge and Extract per-role selectables were removed from the Fleet launcher — Settings owns them. The launcher now sends `judge_*`/`extract_*` as `"inherit"`/`null` so the backend resolves them from the `launch_defaults` singleton at launch time.

- **Bug fix `launcher-role-transport-default-1`:** The Fleet launcher previously hardcoded role transports to `"cli"` (`launcher.tsx` lines 581–582) and sent them verbatim, silently overriding the global Extract/Judge transport default from Settings even when the operator had set it to `api`. Campaign-critical: on an all-Vertex head (no gemini CLI OAuth) this forced CLI extract/judge and hit the OAuth-failing path. Fix: the launcher now sends `"inherit"` for judge and extract transports, letting `resolve_role_transport_default` in `agent_models.py` apply the Settings default correctly.

- **Section per-lesson launcher:** deliberately unchanged (scoped to the Fleet page only).

**Files:** `alembic/versions/0039_launch_defaults_content.py` (new migration — adds 3 columns, seeds content row), `app/models/launch_defaults.py` (3 new fields), `app/api/v1/settings.py` (GET/PUT carry content fields), `web/src/routes/settings.tsx` (Content row), `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx` (content-generator only; judge/extract → inherit/null), `tests/api/test_batch_inherit_resolves_default.py` (new bite-test).

**Verified:** Full DB suite **1313 passed**; bite-test `test_batch_inherit_resolves_default.py` confirms global default `cli` vs job transport `api` → job stamped `cli` (the `inherit`→default-wins path is not vacuous). FE `tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean. SDD (fresh subagent per task; controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran suite). Closes `launcher-role-transport-default-1`.

## [0101] Monitor overhaul — declutter badges + batch actions from Monitor + grade grouping/worker strip — 2026-06-29 (feat/monitor-overhaul → Nggaev-v2, PR #59)

**What:** Three FE-only UI changes to `/monitor`; no migration, no new backend endpoints.

- **#1 — Remove per-batch transport badges:** The Batch card's per-batch provider badge and the `cli`/`API` transport badge were removed (status chip + progress bar retained). Dual-transport books keep only a small muted `cli`/`api` caption. The Monitor page now de-emphasizes transport framing and focuses on batch progress.

- **#4 — Per-batch actions on Monitor (`batch-actions.tsx`):** New `web/src/components/fleet/batch-actions.tsx` renders Pause/Unpause/Cancel-all/Retry-failed buttons directly on each Monitor batch card. Reuses the existing `api.pauseBatch`/`unpauseBatch`/`cancelBatch`/`resumeBatch` endpoints — **no new endpoints**. Cancel shows `window.confirm`. Button visibility gated by `batchActionFlags` (from `lib/monitor-grouping.ts`). Previously, cancel/resume lived only on the Fleet launcher card; they are now accessible from the Monitor without a tab switch.

- **#5 — Grade grouping + compact worker strip:** Batch cards on `/monitor` are now grouped under `Grade N` subheaders (numeric ascending, `Ungraded` last) via new pure helper `web/src/lib/monitor-grouping.ts` (`groupBooksByGrade` + `batchActionFlags`). Worker cards collapsed into a compact strip (no longer large cards).

**Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; `npx tsx` helper test green; `/monitor` renders with 0 console errors (verified by headless screenshot). FE-only; no migration; no backend changes. Built via subagent-driven-development, controller stress-tested every commit (read diff + verified). Acceptance: `tsc` + `build` + `npx tsx` test + `/monitor` headless screenshot.

**Docs:** `CODE_MAP.md` updated (new `batch-actions.tsx` + `monitor-grouping.ts` entries; updated `batch-funnel` + `worker-cards` descriptions; `monitor-overhaul (0101)` note). `HOW_IT_WORKS.md` de-staled (`/monitor` description updated — actions now on Monitor, grade grouping + compact strip noted). ## [0102] Language-aware Notion archival — en/ru content files under its own page — 2026-06-29 (feat/notion-language-pages → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Follow-up to multi-language output (0099): the Notion archival path was language-blind, so an EN/RU homework would file into the same subject page → same "Generated Homeworks" container as UZ. Now `_resolve_subject_page_id` takes a `language` param: `uz` (default) uses the bare `"{subject}|{grade}"` key (**byte-identical** — existing config untouched); `en`/`ru` use a language-prefixed `"{language}:{subject}|{grade}"` key so non-Uzbek content roots at its OWN Notion page (same lesson/Homework structure, different page). `archive_job` threads `job.output_language`. A non-uz job whose language page is unconfigured resolves to None → **skip + record reason** (`notion_skip_reason="no Notion page for language=<lang> <subject>|<grade>"`) — non-Uzbek content is NEVER mis-filed into the uz page (locked decision).

**Config:** `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES` gains optional language-prefixed keys, e.g. `{"ru:geometriya-g7-11|8": "<ru-page-id>"}`. The history-split `{keyword: page-id}` object form composes under a language prefix. `.env.example` documents it.

**Process:** controller-direct (CLAUDE.md small-clear-fix path — root cause verified, decisions locked with the user, backward-compatible one-resolver change), NOT the full plan gate; PR still routed to the gatekeeper. Decisions (missing-page → skip+reason; separate follow-up PR) confirmed by the user.

**Files:** `app/services/notion_archive.py` (resolver `language` param + key prefix; `archive_job` threads `job.output_language` + language-explicit skip reason), `tests/services/test_notion_archive_language.py` (new — 6 cases: uz bare-key default, en/ru prefixed, non-uz never falls back to uz key, prefix composes with history-split, no-grade), `tests/services/test_notion_archive_skip.py` (completed the fake jobs with `output_language="uz"`), `.env.example`, `docs/CODE_MAP.md`.

**Verified:** new tests RED→GREEN (6/6); existing notion suite green after completing the fakes; full non-DB suite **1160 passed / 0 failed**. Stacks on #57 (merged); branched off `origin/Nggaev-v2` @ 423ac41.

## [0103] Language-aware Fleet/Section completion status — launch in another language — 2026-06-29 (feat/fleet-language-status → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Bug introduced by multi-language output (0099): the Fleet and per-Section launchers showed a book/lesson as "complete" and disabled the launch button **regardless of the selected output language** — so a book fully generated in Uzbek read "complete" and could not be re-launched in Russian/English (which had zero jobs). The data layer was already correct (batches are keyed `(book_id, transport, output_language)`); only the *status/gate* was language-blind.

**Root cause:** the per-lesson `latest_job_status` (which drives `doneCount`/`complete`/`remaining` + the disabled launch gate) came from `GET /books/{id}` → `_enriched_toc_entries` → `jobs_repo.latest_by_section(book_id)`, which had **no `output_language` filter** (returned the latest job per section across ALL languages). The launchers held an `outputLanguage` state but never passed it to `getBook`.

**Fix (surgical, non-breaking):** `latest_by_section(session, book_id, output_language=None)` — filters when given, `None` preserves the all-language aggregate (so upload/retry/book-detail callers are unchanged; it has exactly one caller). `_enriched_toc_entries` + `_book_out_with_toc` thread it; `GET /books/{id}` accepts a validated optional `output_language` query param (400 on off-domain). FE: `api.getBook(bookId, outputLanguage?)` appends `?output_language=`; the Fleet (`launcher.tsx`) and Section (`section.tsx`) launchers fetch status for the **effective** language (explicit pick, else the resolved global default — the same value shown as "Auto → <lang>") and key the React-Query cache on it, so switching the language picker refetches and recomputes the completion/gate.

**Process:** controller-direct (CLAUDE.md small-clear-fix path — root cause verified via an Explore map, scope locked with the user [Fleet + Section]); PR routed to the gatekeeper.

**Files:** `app/repositories/jobs.py`, `app/api/v1/books.py`, `web/src/lib/api.ts`, `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx`, `web/src/routes/section.tsx`, `tests/api/test_toc_status_enrichment.py` (thread-through unit test + updated fake signature), `tests/integration/test_latest_by_section_language.py` (new DB-gated scoping test).

**Verified:** enrichment unit tests 2/2; DB-gated `latest_by_section` language-scoping test PASS on scratch Postgres (uz→done job, ru→failed job, en→absent/launchable, None→all-language aggregate); full non-DB suite **1164 passed / 0 failed**; FE `tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean.

## [0104] Language-aware source books — UZ/RU/EN source textbooks surfaced end-to-end — 2026-06-29 (feat/language-aware-source-books P1 #64 · P2 #66 · P3 pending)

**What:** Made **language a source property of a book**, complementing the earlier output-language (medium-of-instruction) work ([0099]). Before, a "Russian" homework was the *Uzbek* PDF rendered in Russian; now RU content can come from the RU textbook. Shipped as three gated PRs through `superpowers:subagent-driven-development`:
- **P1 (backend source model + Notion RU/EN fetch, PR #64):** `books.source_language` (`String(8)` NOT NULL `server_default "uz"`, CHECK `ck_books_source_language IN ('uz','ru','en')`, migration `0040`). `SubjectDef.ru_keywords`/`en_keywords` + `notion_keyword_pairs(language)`; `_map_subject_for_language(title, language)` (`_map_subject` becomes the uz wrapper; `_fold` confirmed Cyrillic-safe). Per-language grade-container crawl `_LANG_CONTAINER_RE` (uz `sinf`, ru `класс|klass`, en explicit marker) + `_subjects_under`/`list_subjects_for_language`/`available_languages`. Ingest threads `source_language` (upload + from-notion `language` field). Dedup stays `(content_sha256, subject)` — a different-language PDF differs by sha.
- **P2 (launch coupling + API payloads, PR #66):** `resolve_output_language_for_book(explicit, book.source_language, global_default)` wired into **both** `launch_batch` AND single-section `generate` (the whole-branch review caught that only the batch path was specced — book property must apply on every launch path). `BookOut.source_language`; `GET /notion/grades/{id}/available-languages`. Tightened the `en` container regex to drop bare `grade` (closes WISHLIST `notion-en-container-regex-1`) before `available_languages` went live.
- **P3 (frontend, pending PR):** `Book.source_language` type + `web/src/lib/language.ts` (`LANG_LABEL`/`langBadge`/`langAccent`); Library badge + `All/UZ/RU/EN` filter; launcher source badge + "Auto → {source}" selector with translate hint; prepare-from-Notion language picker (availability chips, EN disabled w/ create-page-or-upload hint) + upload-form source-language Select. **Task 11 (Monitor card badge) dropped** — #65 already gave the Monitor language tabs.

**Why:** the campaign needs native RU content (and a path to EN), not just UZ-translated-to-RU. Language as a source label (not a top-level nav split or a one-row-multiple-PDFs model) reuses the existing per-sha book row; output language defaulting to source means a RU book "just works" while explicit override preserves translation.

**Verified:** P1 — 65 targeted + 1208 broad non-DB pass, migration up/down on scratch DB, every task bite-proved; opus whole-branch SHIP. P2 — 1225 non-DB pass, both Task-6 commits + the single-section fix bite-proved; opus review FIX-FIRST (single-section default) → fixed. P3 — built via **parallel git-worktree fan-out** (Stream A Library ∥ Stream B launcher/upload/api), integration `tsc`+`build` clean on the combined tree; opus review caught a **Critical** (non-UZ picks sent the UZ `page_id` → every RU/EN fetch would 422) → fixed to send the per-language klass page_id + availability-aware default language; re-verified RU pick now resolves to the klass page. Controller stress-tested every commit (diff read + re-run/tsc-build) and personally re-bite-proved guards (a stale-`.pyc` false-RED was traced and dismissed).

**Operator follow-ups (config/content, not code):** full `ru:<subject>|<grade>` map in `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES` for RU archival; re-fetch RU editions via the new prepare-with-language flow; English has no native Notion tree (upload the PDF directly or create an `english`-named container). Cyrillic TOC-extract is unproven at scale — run a real extract smoke on one RU textbook before bulk (honor the no-spam rule).

**Files:** `app/models/book.py` + `alembic/versions/0040_books_source_language.py`; `app/services/subjects.py`, `app/services/notion_fetch.py`; `app/api/v1/books.py`, `app/api/v1/notion.py`, `app/api/v1/batch.py`, `app/api/v1/jobs.py`; `app/services/agent_models.py`; `app/schemas/book.py`; `web/src/lib/types.ts`, `web/src/lib/language.ts`, `web/src/lib/api.ts`, `web/src/routes/library.tsx`, `web/src/routes/upload.tsx`, `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx`; + tests for each. ## [0105] Monitor curriculum-dashboard — Language tabs + status filters + lesson drawer + grade strip — 2026-06-29 (docs/monitor-dashboard-finish → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Reshaped the `/monitor` page from one long grade-grouped scroll into a **Language → Grade → Subject → Lessons** dashboard. Shipped as four independently-gated PRs:

- **Phase 1 (#62) — `output_language` on the batches-list response:** one line added to `_rollup_payload` in `app/api/v1/batch.py` exposes `output_language` on every batch object returned by `GET /jobs/batches`. No migration (field already on the model); updates the `BatchSummary` FE type. Unblocks the language-dimension pivot on the Monitor without any schema change.
- **Phase 2 (#65) — Language tabs + status filter bar + clickable stat tiles:** `monitor.tsx` gained scoped **language tabs** (UZ / RU / EN, driven by `summarizeByLanguage` from the new `lib/monitor-filters.ts`) + a **status filter bar** defaulting to **Needs attention** (backed by `bookMatchesStatus`). `apiBatches` flows through: language scope → `summarizeByLanguage` summary → status filter → `BatchFunnel`. Clickable stat tiles (counts of each status) flip the active filter. `langs` is `useMemo`-memoized off `apiBatches`. New pure helper `web/src/lib/monitor-filters.ts` (`summarizeByLanguage` / `bookMatchesStatus`) validated with `npx tsx` tests. Composes on top of the Phase 1 `output_language` field.
- **Phase 3 (#67) — Lesson drawer:** lessons within a batch card now open in a hand-rolled right-side **drawer** (`web/src/components/fleet/monitor-drawer.tsx` — CSS overlay + Esc-close, no new third-party dep) instead of inline expansion, so the card grid stays stable while drilling in. `langs` reference corrected to the memoized value.
- **Phase 4 (#69) — Grade filter strip:** `batch-funnel.tsx` gained a `gradeFilter` / `visibleGroups` strip ("All grades" + per-grade buttons) that composes with the language scope and status filter already in place. The complete pipeline: `apiBatches` → language tab (monitor.tsx) → status filter (monitor-filters.ts `bookMatchesStatus`) → grade filter strip (batch-funnel.tsx `gradeFilter`) → lesson drawer (monitor-drawer.tsx) on card click.

**Why:** the campaign generates content across multiple grades, subjects, and languages simultaneously. The old flat scroll made it impossible to see the state of a single grade at a glance; the language tabs and grade filter turn the Monitor into an actionable operations view for the multi-grade rollout.

**Files:** `app/api/v1/batch.py` (`_rollup_payload` +`output_language` — Phase 1); `web/src/lib/monitor-filters.ts` (new — pure `summarizeByLanguage` / `bookMatchesStatus`; `npx tsx`-tested); `web/src/routes/monitor.tsx` (language tabs + status filter bar + stat tiles + `langs` memo); `web/src/components/fleet/monitor-drawer.tsx` (new — hand-rolled right-side lesson drawer, overlay + Esc-close, no new dep); `web/src/components/fleet/batch-funnel.tsx` (grade filter strip `gradeFilter`/`visibleGroups`, drawer wiring). FE-only except Phase 1's single serializer line; no migration (migration 0040 already covers all needed columns).

## [0106] Notion batch re-archive from the website — 2026-06-30 (feat/notion-batch-rearchive → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** Operator can now re-push an entire batch's already-generated homework to Notion from the Monitor, in one click. Extends the per-job re-archive (R15/[0086]) to batch granularity for the common failure mode: a **worker that lacked `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES`** (or hit a transient push error) fails to archive a whole book at once, leaving ~30 lessons un-archived. New endpoint **`POST /jobs/batch/{batch_id}/retry-archive`** runs in the **API (head) process** — which carries the full Notion config — and re-runs the existing idempotent, best-effort `notion_archive.archive_job(job_id)` for each `done` lesson with `notion_archived_at IS NULL`. Backgrounded (`asyncio.create_task` → `_rearchive_sweep`, sequential because the Notion client is globally rate-limited; a full book is minutes of API calls, so a synchronous request would time out); returns immediately with `{queued, already_running}`. An in-process `_REARCHIVE_TASKS` guard makes a double-click a no-op. The batch rollup gains **`archived`/`unarchived`** counts (new `batches_repo.archive_rollup_for_batch`, mirroring `rollup_for_batch`'s DISTINCT-ON-`toc_entry_id` latest-per-lesson so retries don't double-count); the Monitor shows a "Notion archive · X/Y" chip per batch and a **"Re-archive (N)"** button (in `batch-actions.tsx`) whenever `unarchived > 0`.

**Why:** workers in the fleet don't all carry `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES`, so archival silently records a resolvable `notion_skip_reason` ("no Notion page…") for an entire book. The per-job retry button (on the job page) meant clicking ~30 lessons one at a time; the failure is per-book, so the fix belongs at the batch level on the Monitor the operator already lives in.

**Files:** `app/repositories/batches.py` (new `archive_rollup_for_batch` + `done_unarchived_job_ids`; `list_with_rollups` now emits an `"archive"` key); `app/api/v1/batch.py` (`_rollup_payload` gains keyword-only `archived`/`unarchived` defaulted args — keeps the `SimpleNamespace`-fake unit tests green — wired at all 3 call sites; new `_REARCHIVE_TASKS` guard + `_rearchive_sweep` + the endpoint); `tests/api/test_batch_rearchive.py` (new — repo split, unarchived worklist, endpoint sweep via monkeypatched `archive_job`, 404), `tests/api/test_rollup_archive_counts.py` (new — pure serializer keys); `tests/api/test_never_pay_twice.py` + `tests/api/test_batch_output_language.py` (added the companion `archive_rollup_for_batch` AsyncMock to the per-method `launch_batch` mock blocks — `launch_batch` now awaits it); `web/src/lib/types.ts` (`BatchSummary.archived`/`unarchived` + `BatchRearchiveResponse`); `web/src/lib/api.ts` (`retryArchiveBatch`); `web/src/components/fleet/batch-actions.tsx` (Re-archive button) + `web/src/components/fleet/batch-funnel.tsx` (archive-progress chip). No migration (`notion_archived_at` already exists).

**Verified:** built SDD-style, fresh subagent per task, controller stress-tested every commit (read diff + re-ran tests). New real-DB tests 4/4 + serializer 2/2 (17 incl. the launch-mock files); canonical default suite **1227 passed, 170 skipped, 0 failed**; FE `tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean. A broad batch-suite sweep caught a regression the listed tests missed — `launch_batch`'s new awaited `archive_rollup_for_batch` broke `test_batch_output_language.py`'s per-method `MagicMock` launch tests (controller-direct fix commit `850f1e7`, same companion-patch shape Task 2 applied to `test_never_pay_twice`). Built in isolated worktree `HCGA-notion-rearchive`; serialized AFTER the sigma redesign (#71) merged so the new button inherits the restyled `GHOST_BTN/PRESSABLE/FRAME_OFF` look (both edit `batch-funnel.tsx` BookCard lane).

**Notes / assumptions:** `_REARCHIVE_TASKS` is single-process — correct on the head (`WORKER_CONCURRENCY=0`); under `--workers N` the guard wouldn't span workers (harmless: `archive_job` is idempotent, just a redundant sweep). A head restart mid-sweep drops the in-flight task — safe (idempotent), the button reappears while `unarchived > 0`. **Operator prerequisite:** re-archive only succeeds if the **head's own** `NOTION_SUBJECT_PAGES` maps that `subject|grade|language`; otherwise it re-records the same "no Notion page…" skip — the lever is here, head-config completeness stays an operator concern. Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.

## [0107] SA-key web upload + worker auto-distribution — 2026-06-30 (feat/sa-key-web-distribution)

**What:** Operators upload GCP service-account `.json` keys through the web (Fleet → Keys panel) instead of manually copying files onto each worker PC and hand-editing each `.env`. The head stores **only metadata** in `sa_keys` and the raw bytes **on disk** at `<var_dir>/sa_keys/<key_id>.json` (the DB never holds the key bytes). A separate `sa_key_assignments` table maps each **bare hostname** to one key (many hostnames → one key allowed, for the shared-key case). Workers resolve their assignment from Postgres by `socket.gethostname()` at **startup-before-claim** and on a **throttled main-loop tick**, pull the bytes over the auth-gated HTTP download endpoint, and apply **live, no restart**: atomic `os.replace` of `active.json`, paired `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`+`GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT` into `os.environ`, a UTF-8 line-preserving `.env` upsert, and a reassignment of the frozen `CAPABILITIES`/`CAPABILITY_BLOB` worker globals so a freshly-keyed idle worker starts claiming gemini-api jobs. The swap is **idle-gated** (`len(self._tasks)==0`) so no concurrent agent spawn (which reads `{**os.environ}` fresh and raises on a torn Vertex pair) ever sees a half-applied state. Non-destructive unassign + an explicit scrub/revoke path.

**Why:** eliminates the manual "copy SA JSON + edit `.env` + restart worker" loop that was the main friction for provisioning fleet nodes (`docs/fleet/worker-pc-setup.md` called it a rough edge). Provisioning is now upload+assign in the web UI; the worker picks up the key on its next beat with no human intervention and no restart.

**Files:**
- `alembic/versions/0041_sa_keys.py` — migration (head `0041_sa_keys`, down_revision `0040_books_source_language`): `sa_keys` (UUID PK, `original_filename`, `project_id`, `client_email`, `sha256` UNIQUE for dedup, `byte_size`, `label` nullable, `created_at`) + `sa_key_assignments` (`hostname` PK, `key_id` FK→sa_keys nullable ON DELETE RESTRICT, `scrub_requested_at` nullable, `updated_at`).
- `app/models/sa_key.py` — `SAKey` + `SAKeyAssignment` ORM models.
- `app/repositories/sa_keys.py` — `create_or_get` (dedup on sha256), `get`, `list_keys` (+`worker_count`, never private material), `delete` (returns `assigned` to block delete-while-assigned), `assign`, `unassign` (delete row), `scrub` (key_id NULL + scrub flag), `get_assignment_with_key`, `list_assignments`.
- `app/services/sa_key_validate.py` — `parse_and_validate_sa_key(body) -> (project_id, client_email)` / `InvalidServiceAccountKey`; rejects non-`service_account` JSON and missing fields, auto-extracts project_id + client_email.
- `app/services/sa_key_apply.py` — `write_active_key` (same-dir temp + `os.replace`, atomic), `set_credentials_env`/`clear_credentials_env` (set/pop BOTH vars together), `pull_key_bytes` (local disk when `fleet_head_url` empty, else httpx GET the download endpoint with Bearer — `book_fetch` idiom), `upsert_env_file` (UTF-8, line-preserving; `None` value drops a key).
- `app/auth.py` — `get_current_user_strict`: header-only (rejects `?token=`), 503 when `valid_auth_tokens()` empty, 401 otherwise.
- `app/api/v1/sa_keys.py` — `POST /sa-keys` (upload+validate+dedup), `GET /sa-keys` (metadata, no `private_key`), `DELETE /sa-keys/{id}` (409 if assigned), `GET /sa-keys/{id}/download` (header-only strict auth, raw bytes), `GET /sa-keys/assignments`, `PUT`/`DELETE /sa-keys/assignments/{hostname}`, `POST /sa-keys/assignments/{hostname}/scrub`. `app/api/v1/workers.py` list gains an `assignments` field.
- `app/services/worker.py` — `_rebind_capabilities()` reassigns the module globals `CAPABILITIES`/`CAPABILITY_BLOB`; `_sync_sa_key()` (idle-gated apply/scrub/change-detection by sha) called from `run()` at startup-before-claim and throttled in the main loop.
- `web/src/components/fleet/sa-keys-panel.tsx` (+ `web/src/lib/api.ts`, `types.ts`, mounted in `web/src/routes/fleet.tsx`) — Fleet Keys panel: upload, pool list, per-host assign/unassign/scrub.

**Verified:** built SDD-style (fresh subagent per task, controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran tests on a scratch DB), opus whole-branch review **Ready to merge, 0 Critical/Important**. Full suite **1412 passed** on the rebased base (the lone failure, `test_book_from_notion_language::…ru_unrecognised…`, is pre-existing from the base's `b2c1466` RU-keyword sweep — proven not this branch: empty diff vs origin for that path). FE `tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean. Also fixed a latent suite bug surfaced by adding head `0041`: `tests/migrations/test_0039_content.py` upgraded only to a hardcoded `0039` and stranded the shared scratch DB below head, failing 14 downstream DB tests — added a restore-to-head `finally` (same fix as `test_0037_backfill`).

**Key decisions:**
- Key bytes live **on disk only** (`<var_dir>/sa_keys/<id>.json`), DB holds metadata + sha256. Same posture as book PDFs; encryption-at-rest is out of scope (operator-accepted plaintext, consistent with `.env`).
- The **capability rebind is the load-bearing gap-closer**: `CAPABILITIES`/`CAPABILITY_BLOB` are computed once at import and read by the claim gate (`_claim_one`) / heartbeat at call time — mutating `os.environ` alone would leave a keyed worker idle forever, so the apply path reassigns both globals.
- Swap is **idle-gated** at the worker's between-claim point; the worker is a single coroutine, so the only `await` between the idle check and the synchronous env mutation is the byte pull — no spawn can start mid-apply.
- Download is **header-only** (a `?token=` would leak into proxy/access logs) and **503s when auth is disabled** — a credential vault must never be served open; ship is gated on a real `AUTH_TOKEN`.
- Assignment keys on **bare hostname** (not `hostname:pid`) so it survives worker restarts.

**Notes:** plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`. Migration head: `0041_sa_keys`. Operator owes the live no-restart acceptance (boot keyless → assign → `gemini_api=True` within ≤30s, no restart) as the final gate.

## [0108] TOC vision validator (soft-gate) — 2026-06-30 (feat/toc-vision-validator → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** New capability (not a backlog item; unrelated to Cluster 6's Notion-archive validator). After `agent.extract_toc` produces entries, **`agent.validate_toc`** makes ONE Gemini-2.5-flash **vision** call comparing the extracted TOC against the book's printed contents-page image (front + back page window via `_toc_source_pdf`). The call **never raises** — any failure (no usable window, spawn error, parse failure, prompt-build error) returns `status="skipped"` (the prompt-build is inside try/finally so no partial state escapes; this was the one Important finding from the final opus review, fixed before ship).

**Soft-gate in `toc_extractor.run`:** `mismatch` verdict → book status set to **`toc_review`** (TOC entries persisted, `books.toc_validation`/`toc_validation_detail` recorded; generation blocked — `batch.py`/`jobs.py` already gate on `toc_ready`). `verified`/`skipped` → `toc_ready` (unchanged). Behind `settings.toc_validation_enabled` (default True); disabled → column stays NULL (distinct from `skipped`). Provider/model configurable (`settings.toc_validation_provider`/`_model`, default `gemini`/`gemini-2.5-flash`); transport follows `launch_defaults.toc_transport`.

**Migration `0042_books_toc_validation`** (chains off `0041_sa_keys`): adds `books.toc_validation` (String(16), DB CHECK `NULL | verified | mismatch | skipped`) + `books.toc_validation_detail` (Text NULL).

**API + FE:**
- `POST /books/{id}/toc/accept` (409 unless `toc_review` → flips to `toc_ready`, preserves validation columns as audit trail); `POST /books/{id}/toc/retry` now also accepts `toc_review` books.
- `BookOut` exposes `toc_validation`/`toc_validation_detail`; `_book_out_with_toc` returns the TOC for `toc_review` books; `stream_toc` has a `toc_review` SSE branch that emits `toc_validation_detail`.
- FE: `StatusBadge` amber `toc_review` chip; book-page review panel (issues from `toc_validation_detail`, **Accept anyway** + **Retry** buttons, `REVIEW_FALLBACK` ensures the panel renders even with null detail); Fleet launcher puts `toc_review` in the failed/attention bucket (distinct amber "Review TOC" card → book page, never vanishes); `Book` type + `api.acceptToc`.

**Acceptance:** `scripts/toc_validate_smoke.py` — real Gemini smoke PASSED: geometriya-g7-11 (75 entries) genuine TOC → `verified`/high confidence; scrambled TOC → `mismatch`/high (model identified the book as "GEOMETRIYA 8" and the entries as fabricated).

**Process:** built via `superpowers:subagent-driven-development` (8 tasks, fresh subagent per task, controller stress-tested every commit) + final whole-branch opus review (verdict SHIP, one Important finding → fixed before ship). Rebased onto `8345356`.

**Files:** `app/services/agent.py` (`validate_toc` + `_toc_source_pdf` reuse), `app/services/toc_extractor.py` (soft-gate), `alembic/versions/0042_books_toc_validation.py`, `app/models/book.py`, `app/api/v1/books.py` (accept endpoint + SSE branch + BookOut fields + retry allows toc_review), `app/repositories/books.py`, `app/schemas/book.py`, `web/src/lib/types.ts` (`Book.toc_validation`/`toc_validation_detail`, `api.acceptToc`), `web/src/components/...` (StatusBadge chip, review panel, fleet launcher bucket), tests. Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.
