## [0109] CQ-A content-quality prompt-layer fixes — curriculum boundary + reflection + L2 bridge — 2026-07-02 (cq-a-prompt-boundary)

**What:** The three prompt-layer fixes from the 2026-07-01 content audit (ROADMAP R21, Cluster 10 CQ-A). All three were provably invisible to the LLM judge (`judge_status=ok` on 5/5 audited packets).

1. **R21.1 curriculum-boundary leakage** — content phases saw only THIS lesson's extract, so at hard difficulty the generator reached for the concept's natural completion — the NEXT lesson's material (Pythagorean **converse** §18, parallelogram **alomatlari**, "asimptota"). Fix: `pipeline._inject_lesson_boundary(lesson_context, next_lesson_title)` prepends a `CURRICULUM BOUNDARY:` note naming the next lesson and forbidding its concepts/converse/criteria/generalization. Rides inside `lesson_context` (mirrors `_inject_grade`) so **every content phase** sees it via `_build_master_prompt`'s LESSON CONTEXT block, with zero call-site surgery; `extract` is untouched (its `lesson_context` is `None`). Next lesson = new **`toc_entries.get_next_in_book(session, book_id, order_index)`** — next `order_index` **skipping `section_number IS NULL` end-matter** (Ответы/Тестовые — 214 such rows in prod; gate R2). Fetched inside the open session in `run()` (no detached ORM); no-op at the last teaching lesson.
2. **R21.5 reflection** — `prompts/_general/reflection.md` stopped fabricating attempt outcomes: §2 no longer asks how "the student's answers handled well" (no attempt exists at gen time), §4 no longer mandates naming a "Needs Retry"/"not passed" verdict — the app owns pass/redo; the phase emits neutral structure only.
3. **`l2-bridge-follows-medium`** — `prompts._l2_rule(target_lang, bridge_medium)` makes the L2 scaffolding **bridge follow the chosen medium** (uz/en/ru) while the L2 **target** stays english/russian. Built by `.replace()`-substitution on the **frozen** `_LANG_ENGLISH`/`_LANG_RUSSIAN` bases (uz early-returns the base verbatim → **byte-identical by construction**); an f-string rebuild was rejected because the originals carry an authoring asymmetry (English governing line "in Uzbek" vs Russian "in formal Uzbek") a template would silently "fix". Reverses the prior L2 carve-out ([0099]).

**Acceptance (real generation, `transport=api`/Vertex):** `scripts/cqa_prompt_smoke.py` on the actual audit lesson (book `860e86aa` §17 "Pifagor teoremasi va uning turli isbotlari", successor §18 "…teskari teorema") — boss-arena stays inside §17 (no converse reach, no boundary-note echo); reflection §4 reads conditionally ("if the app marks a redo…"), no pre-asserted outcome; L2 english+`ru` renders a Russian bridge. **SMOKE PASS.** Full unit suite 1275 passed; Task-1 DB test 2 passed on scratch DB (`RUN_DB_INTEGRATION`).

**No migration.** Gate R1 (vacuous-test trap): the byte-identity guard asserts against a **test-local frozen literal**, not `LANGUAGE_RULES["english"]` (which is the function itself) — RED-proven by mutating the base → the test fails, revert → passes.

**Process:** `superpowers:subagent-driven-development`, 5 tasks, fresh subagent per task, controller stress-tested every commit (read diff + re-ran tests; RED-proved the byte-identity test by mutation) + final whole-branch opus review (READY TO MERGE, 0 Critical/Important). Gate pre-approved with 3 required changes (R1 frozen-literal test, R2 NULL-section skip, R3 smoke no-echo + edu_copy DB) — all folded into the plan before exec.

**Files:** `app/repositories/toc_entries.py` (`get_next_in_book`), `app/services/pipeline.py` (`_inject_lesson_boundary` + `run()` wiring), `prompts/_general/reflection.md`, `app/services/prompts.py` (`_l2_rule` + `_resolve_language_rule`), `scripts/cqa_prompt_smoke.py`, tests (`tests/integration/test_toc_next_in_book.py`, `tests/services/test_pipeline_boundary.py`, `test_reflection_prompt.py`, `test_prompts_output_language.py`). Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.
## [0110] Deterministic content lint (CQ-B: R21.3 error-detection format + R21.4 language) — 2026-07-02 (cq-b-content-lint → Nggaev-v2)

**What:** New module **`app/services/content_lint.py`** — pure-Python, **no-LLM, no-cost** post-phase lint that surfaces the mechanical defect classes the 2026-07-01 5-packet audit found (`docs/research/2026-07-01-content-quality-audit-g8-math.md`). `lint_phase(phase_name, output_md, *, subject, output_language) -> list[LintFinding]` + `findings_to_warnings()`.

**Checks:** (language, every non-extract phase) mixed Latin+Cyrillic in one word (`hisoblaniб`); structural English-template tokens (`Mode:`, `Needs Retry`, `red herring`, shipped meta-preamble phrases — deliberately NOT ambiguous bare words like `Scenario`/`Wrong`, so L2/English lessons don't false-positive); calque (`qizil seld`). (flashcards only) misconception cards missing the `source`/`inferred` provenance tag (`flashcards.md:32,95`). (practice-error-detection only) the **EXACTLY-ONE-broken-block** format contract (`practice-error-detection.md:50-54`): zero markers → `errdet_no_broken_marker`, ≥2 distinct marked block ids → `errdet_multiple_broken`, Reveal names a different block than the body → `errdet_reveal_mismatch`. Marker regex handles BOTH real word orders (`Blok 4 noto'g'ri` noun-first + `noto'g'ri blok 4` verb-first) via `finditer` with captured ids.

**Policy = WARN-ONLY (locked with the gatekeeper).** `pipeline._execute_phase` folds `findings_to_warnings()` (`lint:<code>` prefix) into the same `validation_warnings` the LLM judge uses (`pipeline.py` ~1152), defensively try/except-wrapped — never gates a regen, never fails a job. Mirrors the `phase_judge._fidelity_flags` advisory precedent. Rejected regen-once (costs a paid call per fire; contradicts CQ-B's no-cost mandate).

**90%-bar reframe (accepted at the gate):** R21.3 ships the deterministic FORMAT contract only. Both audited error-detection outputs are format-clean — their real defects (job `8f734563` key endorses a wrong `+1` sign; job `3ca0da6f` wrong final result carried forward) are **semantic**, un-catchable without re-solving the algebra → **CQ-C (R21.2)**. The two real outputs are used as no-false-positive fixtures.

**Acceptance:** unit tests only (deterministic — no generation smoke, per CQ-B's no-LLM mandate). 16 tests over the real audited fixtures (`tests/fixtures/content_lint/`, pulled from `edu_copy`) + 3 minimal mutations for the must-flag error-detection cases. Full suite **1285 passed / 182 skipped**. Known limitations documented in the module docstring (reveal-mismatch needs numeric ids; provenance check matches `source`/`inferred` anywhere — both conservative, under- not over-flag).

**Process:** `superpowers:subagent-driven-development` (fixtures + module + wiring; controller stress-tested every commit — read diff + re-ran tests + verified real fixtures byte-untouched). Base `origin/Nggaev-v2 @ 23310a0`, no rebase needed. Worklog 0109 reserved for the parallel CQ-A session. **Files:** `app/services/content_lint.py` (new), `app/services/pipeline.py` (import + warn-only wiring), `tests/services/test_content_lint.py`, `tests/fixtures/content_lint/*` (5 real + 3 mutated). Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.
## [0111] CQ-D extract-quality guards — 2026-07-02 (cq-d-extract-guards → Nggaev-v2)

**What (Cluster 10 / CQ-D code pair, ROADMAP R21.6 + R10):** two independent extract-quality guards in one PR.

**Item 2 — garbled-text detection (R10/`extract-1`).** New `agent._alpha_plausibility_ratio(text)` = fraction of alphabetic chars in a real alphabet (ASCII Latin ∪ Cyrillic ∪ Uzbek modifiers ʻʼ). Below `settings.extract_min_alpha_ratio` (0.70) the text layer is garbled. Wired into **Gate A** (`validate_extract_text`) and the **TOC usable-gate** (extracted predicate `_toc_text_is_usable`); a failure returns a reason → the pipeline routes to the existing **vision** path (loud-fail only if vision also fails). Below `_ALPHA_RATIO_MIN_SAMPLE` (200) alphabetic chars it returns 1.0 (can't judge → never false-fires).

**Re-scoped from the cluster-doc framing by fact-over-theory:** verified `_decode_glyph_text` (shipped [0035]/[0036]) **already recovers all four local `/Gxx` books perfectly** (plausibility 0.999), so the pure-glyph case is NOT the live gap. The actually-unguarded poison is text that is letter-DENSE but the wrong alphabet — `f20db30c` (RU cp1251 mojibake) passed the old letter-density Gate A at ratio 0.88 but scores plausibility **0.082**. Corpus sweep (26 books): 25 accept (0.998–1.000), only `f20db30c` rejects — the guard fires on exactly the known-garbage book.

**Item 1 — extract-example fidelity (R21.6).** The drift is IN the extract (`summarize_lesson`), and the judge is blind to it by construction (`_FIDELITY_RULE` exempts worked-example arithmetic; it grades downstream phases against the *extract*, never the book). Hybrid guard, all in the pipeline's local-text extract branch after Gate B:
- **Free** `agent.extract_fidelity_candidates(summary, book_text)` — fraction/equation expressions in the summary absent from the source. Regex captures `/`-or-`=` AND (digit OR paren) — the paren arm surfaces digitless invented examples like `(a−b)/(a+b)` (audited drift #2); prose slashes (`va/yoki`) excluded; strips trailing sentence punctuation so `21/120.` grounds against `21/120`.
- **Paid-on-hits** `agent.verify_extract_fidelity(...)` — one gemini-flash structured call (`ExtractFidelityVerdict`), **fail-open** (any error → `[]`, never blocks/corrupts). Source is **lesson-scoped** (`read_page_range_text(pdf, page_start, page_end, ±1)`, fallback full book) so the one paid call is bounded to lesson-sized input, not the whole book (money-rule guard).
- **Regen-once** on confirmed mismatch: re-run `summarize_lesson(correction_hint=...)`; accept the corrected output if it passes Gate B, else keep the original. Extract is cached cross-job → the guard runs on first production only (amortized).

**Key decisions (gate-locked):** char-plausibility → route-to-vision (not flag-book, not dictionary-hit); hybrid free-precheck → LLM-on-hits → regen-once (not LLM-always, not deterministic-only). Gate adjustments folded pre-execution: R1 (digit-OR-paren breadth) and R2 (lesson-scoped verify source).

**Acceptance:** `scripts/cqd_extract_guards_smoke.py`. CHECK 1 (item-2 detection, no model) **PASSED on real books** (`f20db30c`→0.115→rejected; `5e295cbc`→1.000→accepted). CHECKS 2 & 3 (real gemini-api: vision recovers Cyrillic on `f20db30c`; verify catches a planted drift) require a working Vertex env — **run at the PR gate** (the CLI shell's `.env` had a stale Windows SA-key path). Full suite green (1293 passed / 182 skipped). Item-1 verify is fail-open + unit-tested end-to-end, so the residual risk is bounded to needless-regen cost, not corruption.

**Process:** `superpowers:subagent-driven-development` (fresh subagent per task; controller read every diff + re-ran every test). Subagents caught two genuine plan defects mid-execution (a below-sample-floor mojibake test string; a vacuous ASCII-hyphen assertion vs U+2212 fixtures) and refused to paper over them — both fixed in the plan + code. Rebased onto `e19329f`.

**Files:** `app/config.py` (`extract_min_alpha_ratio`), `app/services/agent.py` (`_is_expected_alpha`/`_alpha_plausibility_ratio`, `_toc_text_is_usable`, `_normalize_expr`/`extract_math_expressions`/`extract_fidelity_candidates`, `ExtractFidelityVerdict`/`verify_extract_fidelity`, `summarize_lesson` `correction_hint`, Gate-A + TOC-gate wiring), `app/services/pipeline.py` (`_verify_source_for_section`, `_verify_and_maybe_regen_extract`, `_extract_run` wiring), `scripts/cqd_extract_guards_smoke.py`, tests (test_alpha_plausibility, test_extract_fidelity, + extends to test_extract_gates/test_toc_source_text/test_summarize_lesson/test_pipeline_extract_dispatch). Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.

## [0112] CQ-C answer-key solver pass (R21.2) — 2026-07-02 (cq-c-key-solver → Nggaev-v2)

**What (Cluster 10 / CQ-C, ROADMAP R21.2 — the campaign-quality core):** a third independent LLM role — the **solver** — that re-solves a content phase's items and flags where the generated answer key is wrong, closing the audit's most damaging defect class ("a correct student graded wrong"). Architecturally a clone of the judge role: `app/services/solver.py::solve()` makes ONE structured `agent.run_phase(schema=SolveVerdict, phase_name="__solver__", operation="solve:<phase>")` call; degrade-never-blocks (any error → `available=False`) EXCEPT an api-transport auth error → re-raise (job-level failure, like the judge). Wired into `pipeline._execute_phase` AFTER the judge/regen block (solves the final output) for `_SOLVER_PHASES = (memory-check, practice-error-detection, practice-rlc)`; on a HIGH-confidence key mismatch it regenerates once (`settings.max_solve_regens`) and re-solves. Records a new `phase_outputs.solver_status` (ok / mismatch_regen / mismatch_shipped / mismatch_regen_failed / unavailable / refused).

**Conservative by design (the validate_toc lesson):** only `confidence=="high"` discrepancies trigger a regen; low/medium are advisory. This buys **zero false positives** (never corrupts a correct packet) at the cost of recall.

**⚠️ RECALL BOUNDARY — stated in numbers (do NOT round up):** the catch is **probabilistic, not reliable**. Gatekeeper-verified across 3 independent smoke runs at the merge gate: the objective sign/arithmetic error (`8f734563` error-detection) flagged in **2/3** runs (missed in run 1 — the gated bar itself is nondeterministic); the expression-equivalence error (`8f734563` practice-rlc) flagged in **1/3**; the conceptual truth-value error (`263d99c5` memory-check symmetry) in **0/3** (and 0 in all characterization runs). **Zero false positives across every run.** Characterization (`scripts/cqc_solver_characterize.py`, committed as evidence) proves this is a **model-capability** miss, not a design/threshold artifact: EXP 1 → `agrees=True, 0 discrepancies` (genuine miss, nothing suppressed by the high-only gate); EXP 2 → a truth-value-directive prompt variant does NOT recover it (×3) while the must-PASS packet stays clean (×3). **The 2 missed classes are covered only by CQ-E's answer-key audit rubric, not by this solver.** (Gemini-only is standing policy; a claude-opus cross-model probe was deliberately NOT run.)

**⚠️ Gemini-only operational footgun (one line, no code):** never set the **content generator** to `gemini-3.1-pro-preview`. The self-grade guard (`model_tiers.resolve_solver`==`resolve_judge`) would then swap the solver (and judge — same mechanism) to the claude peer, and on a keyless-Anthropic fleet Task-8's claim gate correctly leaves those jobs **unclaimed forever**. With content on the seeded `2.5-pro/flash`, the claude path is never resolved and everything stays Vertex-native.

**Key facts (gate-verified):**
- **Per-role columns mirror the judge exactly:** `solver_provider/model/transport` on `launch_defaults`/`homework_jobs`/`batches` + `phase_outputs.solver_status` (**migration 0043**). Model resolved by `model_tiers.resolve_solver` (thin alias of `resolve_judge` — same self-grade guard). Launch-stamped from `launch_defaults` at `/generate` + batch launch (5-file judge-mirror). **Seeded default `gemini / gemini-3.1-pro-preview / inherit`** (Vertex-native, ~$0.10-0.12/job, no ANTHROPIC key on the common path) — vs ~$0.22/job at an opus default (R2).
- **Claim-gate (R1, load-bearing):** `jobs.claim_next_job` adds `solver_ok` mirroring `judge_ok` on the solver columns + `.where(solver_ok)` + `solver_needs_api` in the fleet-pause gate, so a job whose resolved solver is claude/api is never claimed by a keyless-Anthropic worker (bite-proven). **Migration 0043 also backfills `homework_jobs.solver_provider` on pre-existing rows** (COALESCE→gemini, mirrors 0037) — without it, pre-0043 pending/api jobs would strand unclaimable once the gate went live (caught by the Task-8 review; guarded by a biting migration test).
- **Cost:** measured ~$0.02-0.08/call → **~$0.12/job** at the 3 target phases (confirms R2). Total characterization+smoke spend this session ~$0.74.

**Acceptance (gate condition):** `scripts/cqc_solver_smoke.py` — the strict "flag all 3" bar was a FAIL; **amended at the gate** (with evidence) to the honest GATED bar: the objective sign-error MUST flag AND the clean key MUST stay zero-false-positive (both ✅, exit 0); the two conceptual/equivalence misses are reported informationally. `scripts/cqc_solver_characterize.py` is the attached evidence. Full suite 1350 passed.

**Process:** `superpowers:subagent-driven-development` — fresh subagent per task; controller read every diff + re-ran every test; the 3 substantive tasks (solver.py, pipeline wiring, claim-gate) got independent task-reviewers. The Task-8 reviewer caught a real Medium-High strand bug (missing solver_provider backfill) that a "mirrors judge" reading had wrongly accepted — fixed with the biting backfill test. Deferred to a ROADMAP follow-up: solver-config editing (`launch_defaults._MUTABLE` + `/settings` `SettingsIn/Out` backend + FE editor + a `solver_status` badge) — the launch path + seed make the role work config-free, editing it is the follow-up.

**Files:** `app/schemas/solver.py` (SolveVerdict/Discrepancy), `app/config.py` (solver_enabled, max_solve_regens), `alembic/versions/0043_solver_role_columns.py` (+seed +job backfill), `app/models/{launch_defaults,homework_job,batch,phase_output}.py`, `app/services/model_tiers.py` (resolve_solver), `app/services/agent_models.py`, `app/schemas/job.py`, `app/repositories/{jobs,batches,phase_outputs}.py`, `app/api/v1/{jobs,batch}.py`, `app/services/solver.py`, `app/services/pipeline.py` (_SOLVER_PHASES + solve-and-regen wiring), `scripts/cqc_solver_smoke.py`, `scripts/cqc_solver_characterize.py`, tests (test_solver, test_pipeline_solver, test_model_tiers_solver, test_solver_columns, test_launch_stamps_solver, migration 0043 + claim-gate solver cases). Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.

## [0113] CQ-E — R20 golden-eval harness (standalone, offline) — 2026-07-02 (cq-e-golden-eval)

**What:** The R20 quality-regression harness (Cluster 10 CQ-E, the last CQ item). A **standalone, offline** scorer — no pipeline/worker/schema coupling (collision map: CQ-C in flight). New `app/services/golden_eval.py` scores one packet's phase markdowns across the 6 audit dimensions; `scripts/golden_eval.py` runs it, diffs vs a committed baseline, exits non-zero on a **gated** regression. `tests/golden/` holds the frozen golden set (the 5 audited packets), committed source fixtures (pdftotext of the real textbook pages), and the tests. **No migration.**

**Hybrid rubric (gate-confirmed):** deterministic dims run FREE in pytest — `language` (reuses CQ-B `content_lint` + a stricter harness-owned mixed-script + English-scaffolding blacklist, non-English-lessons only), `reflection` (keys on **past-tense fabrication verbs** `kuzatildi`/`sezdingiz`/`baholandi`, NOT the bare "Needs Retry" label, so a conditional redo-route is a legitimate `pass`), and the error-detection format check. LLM-rubric dims (`boundary`/`answer_key`/`broken_question`/`extract_fidelity`) each make ONE `agent.run_phase(schema=RubricVerdict, transport=api)` call; degrade-to-`pass` (never crash) with "unavailable" in detail. `answer_key` also reads CQ-C's `solver_status` via `getattr(po,"solver_status",None)` — works pre- and post-CQ-C.

**Gate conditions folded before exec:** E1 `broken_question`=det-OR-llm; E2 reflection verdicts set from the REAL rows (per-packet `reflection_evidence` quote), not the audit hedge; E3 baseline cost estimated FREE from `agent_usages`; E4 `--emit-baseline` refuses to freeze an "unavailable" (degraded) score; E5 golden-set-expansion ROADMAP follow-up.

**Acceptance (fact over theory):** deterministic dims reproduce the audit **10/10** on the 5 real `edu_copy` packets (`language`/`reflection` via the real DB loader). A **paid** LLM audit-check over 4 packets (gemini-2.5-pro/Vertex, **$1.20**) measured the single-pass LLM dims at ~40% agreement with the human audit (answer_key 1/4, broken_question 1/4, boundary 2/4) — an empirical finding that **confirms the audit's thesis** (LLM verdicts can't reliably grade answer-key correctness; that's CQ-C's solver's job). **Consequence, shipped:** the exit-code gate is authoritative ONLY on the deterministic `GATED_DIMENSIONS` (`language`,`reflection`); LLM-verdict dims are reported but advisory (they'd flap the baseline-diff). `answer_key` rejoins the gate once `solver_status` drives it. Full suite green (golden 45 passed/1 skip; 1364+ overall).

**⛔ Task 6 (baseline-freeze) NOT run — blocked on CQ-C merge (0112) + head restart + user go.** Baselines must freeze the FIXED system; freezing pre-CQ-C would bake the answer-key bug in. So R20 stays OPEN for the freeze step; the harness + rubric + deterministic acceptance are shipped.

**Process:** `superpowers:subagent-driven-development`, 5 tasks + 2 fix commits (runner-test + the deterministic-gate refinement), controller stress-tested every commit (incl. catching that an early "language 1/5" scare was my own fragile psql-parse artifact — 5/5 via the real loader). Final opus review: READY TO MERGE, 0 Critical/Important.

**Files:** `app/services/golden_eval.py`, `scripts/golden_eval.py`, `tests/golden/{manifest.json,sources/*.txt,test_manifest,test_deterministic_scorers,test_llm_scorers,test_golden_gate,test_reproduces_audit,test_runner}.py`. Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.

## [0114] notion — refresh stale archive on regen (notion-archive-stale-on-regen-1) — 2026-07-03 (fix/notion-stale-on-regen)

**What:** Force-regenerated homework never refreshed Notion. `_write_leaf` is skip-if-populated (`notion_archive.py:163`), and a force-regen mints a **fresh** job row (`batch.py:308,349`, `notion_archived_at=NULL`) whose auto-archive stamps "archived" but leaves the OLD content on the still-populated leaf. Live 2026-07-03: the Parallelogramm lesson served June (pre-CQ, audit-flagged) content while the dashboard said "✓ archived". Fix: an operator **`force`** flag that clears+rewrites the stale machine leaf pages. **No migration.**

**Design (GK2-approved, decisions ①②③):** ① **operator force, manual endpoints only** — the automatic pipeline archive stays skip-if-populated so it **never auto-clobbers**. Rejected auto-newer-wins (a teacher edit bumps Notion `last_edited_time` → clobber-or-block dilemma) and a content-hash trigger (needs a migration). ② force on **both** endpoints (batch = the regen-wave lever). ③ clear = delete **non-`child_page`** blocks on the machine leaves, preserving structure, container pages, and human sub-pages; a manual annotation added *as a block on a generated leaf* is replaced (block provenance isn't stored — deletion only on explicit operator action, documented).

**How (5 tasks):** (1) `NotionClientWrapper.delete_block` + `clear_content_blocks(page_id)->int` (SDK `blocks.delete`; skips `child_page`). (2) `_write_leaf` **replace mode** threaded via `_push_to_notion(replace=)`/`_push_with_retry(replace=)` — clear-then-rewrite a populated leaf; `replace=False` byte-identical to before. (3) `archive_job(job_id, *, force=False)` bypasses the already-archived early-return and pushes with `replace=force`; success write still clears `notion_skip_reason` (N3). (4) `?force=true` on `POST /jobs/{id}/retry-archive` (overrides the 409; non-done still 409s). (5) `batches_repo.done_job_ids` (mirrors `done_unarchived_job_ids` minus the archived filter) + `?force=true` on `POST /jobs/batch/{id}/retry-archive` sweeping all `done` incl. archived. Fold-ins: N1 worklog next-free (0114), N2 operational-ordering note (force AFTER the wave — the sweep takes the latest *done* job; a running replacement re-goes-stale on its own skip-archive), N3 skip-reason assertion.

**Acceptance:** full suite green (1404 passed / 195 skipped, no `RUN_DB_INTEGRATION`); the batch DB-integration tests (`done_job_ids` incl-archived, force-sweeps-all-done queued==2, `_fake_archive` force-threaded) pass on a fresh scratch DB `edu_scratch_notion`@head. Guards bite: `replace=False` control skips, `?force` without done still 409s, non-force sweep still excludes archived. **Live-Notion verify (real Parallelogramm page) held for the user's go** at the PR gate — no automated test writes to Notion. FE "Re-archive (force)" button flagged out-of-scope.

**Process:** `superpowers:subagent-driven-development`, 5 tasks, controller stress-tested every commit (read diff == plan + re-ran tests). Note: Task-1's commit landed via a background continuation of an interrupted dispatch (the bg-continue-after-blocked race) — verified byte-correct before proceeding.

**Files:** `app/services/notion/client.py`, `app/services/notion_archive.py`, `app/api/v1/jobs.py`, `app/api/v1/batch.py`, `app/repositories/batches.py` + their tests (`tests/services/test_notion_client.py`, `test_notion_archive.py`, `test_notion_archive_force.py`, `tests/api/test_retry_archive_endpoint.py`, `test_batch_rearchive.py`). Docs de-staled: `HOW_IT_WORKS.md`, `CODE_MAP.md`. Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.

## [0115] toc — order_index by page_start, not LLM emission order — 2026-07-06 (Nggaev-v2, controller-direct)

**What:** `toc_entries.bulk_create` assigned `order_index` from the extractor model's emission order (`order_index=idx` over the raw list). Two-column mundarija pages come back interleaved, so books landed with scrambled reading order — hit live twice: G10 algebra `7e86f398` (2-BOB rows before 1-BOB) and G5 matematika part 2 `1d524d6c`, where the scramble also **corrupted three seam `page_end`s** (a row spanning 156→89). Not cosmetic: `order_index` drives `toc_entries.get_next_in_book` — the CQ-A curriculum-boundary note — so a scrambled book pins the boundary to the wrong "next lesson".

**Fix (one-liner + tests, TDD):** stable sort in `bulk_create` before index assignment — key `(page_start is None, page_start, emission_idx)`; same-page ties keep emission order, page-less entries go last. `tests/repositories/test_toc_entries_order.py` (2 tests, RED-proven against the unfixed code). Suite: 1404 passed (the 2 `test_failover_api` fails are pre-existing on clean HEAD, verified via stash).

**Data remediation (already applied, SQL in-place):** G10 algebra + G5-part-2 renumbered by `page_start`; G5-part-2's 3 corrupt seam `page_end`s fixed against the real PDF (89→89, 156→160, 109→109; book is 160pp). Fleet-wide sweep: **0 non-monotonic books remain**. Commit `b187d7d`.

**Context (same day, unlogged elsewhere):** G11 unified Matematika (2 parts, dual-mundarija layout — algebra mundarija p.112 + geometry mundarija p.191) defeats both the extractor and the vision validator (each roll captures one half; validator blessed a half-missing TOC = first live `toc-reextract-override-1` trigger case). Both parts' TOCs hand-curated from the real mundarijas (24+21 rows, `toc_validation_detail` stamped). Full G5–11 UZ math track now TOC-verified: **386 lessons ≈ $226** at the measured $0.585/hw.

## [0116] small-fix pair — queue backpressure vs paused batches + backgrounded force re-archive — 2026-07-06 (fix/queue-and-force-timeout)

**What (2 verified small fixes, controller-direct per the CLAUDE.md exception):**
1. **`queue-backpressure-paused-1`** — `jobs_repo.queue_depth` counted a paused batch's dormant pending jobs (unclaimable by design), so 57 paused RU-geometry jobs filled `queue_backpressure_limit` (50) and 503'd an unrelated 1-job `/generate` (hit live 2026-07-03). Fix: `queue_depth` LEFT JOINs `batches` and filters `Batch.paused_at IS NULL` — mirrors the claim gate's pause predicate (`jobs.py:411-416`) including its critical batchless arm (LEFT JOIN keeps `batch_id IS NULL` jobs counted via NULL `paused_at`).
2. **`job-force-rearchive-inline-timeout-1`** — `POST /jobs/{id}/retry-archive?force=true` ran the >5-min rate-limited Notion clear+rewrite INLINE; a client disconnect made starlette CANCEL it mid-clear → half-written page (hit live during the #81 verify: Boss Arena 52→23 blocks). Fix: backgrounded exactly like the batch sweep — `_FORCE_REARCHIVE_TASKS` in-flight guard keyed by job id + `_force_rearchive_one` task (pops the guard in `finally`); the endpoint returns an immediate `{job_id, queued, already_running}` receipt; a double-POST no-ops. **Non-force path unchanged** (inline, returns `JobOut`) — the FE's `retryArchiveJob(): Promise<Job>` consumes that shape, so backgrounding both was NOT simpler; the endpoint's `-> JobOut` annotation dropped (mixed return shapes, mirrors the batch endpoint).

**Proof:** RED→GREEN both fixes. queue_depth: DB-gated test (`tests/repositories/test_queue_depth_pause.py`, scratch `edu_scratch_smallfix`@127.0.0.1) — pause excludes / unpause restores / batchless always counted (a naive INNER JOIN fails it). Force: async AsyncClient tests — immediate `{queued:1}` receipt + background task awaited with `force=True` + guard released; double-POST `{queued:0, already_running:true}` with archive never called; non-force happy path (inline JobOut + `expire_all`) untouched. Full suite: 1403 passed / 196 skipped + **2 PRE-EXISTING failures** in `tests/services/test_failover_api.py` (`cli_failover_still_crosses_providers` + `api_failover_default_transport_is_cli`) — reproduced at pristine base `211058f` in a clean detached worktree, so not this branch's regression; the cli failover chain isn't crossing providers (env/DB-dependent; needs its own investigation — flagged to GK2).

**Files:** `app/repositories/jobs.py` (queue_depth), `app/api/v1/jobs.py` (force backgrounding), `tests/repositories/test_queue_depth_pause.py` (new), `tests/api/test_retry_archive_endpoint.py`.

## [0117] Round-2 prompt/lint localization polish — 2026-07-06 (fix/round2-localization-polish)

**What:** the non-blocking localization defects from the 2026-07-03 CQ re-audit (`docs/research/2026-07-03-cq-reaudit-g6-10.md` §"Round-2 polish list"). RU-medium output leaked Uzbek/English template strings because the shared `prompts/_general/*.md` bodies hardcode literals the model emits verbatim (they **override** the medium `{{LANGUAGE_RULES}}`), and the bilingual subject label `"Mathematics (Matematika)"` fed the "Matematika" RU title. **Load-bearing FE check:** the live packet view (`web/src/routes/preview.tsx`) renders `output_md` **verbatim** through ReactMarkdown; the structured game components (`BossFight`, etc.) are unmounted legacy — so English scaffold headers are **student-visible, not structural** → they must localize (settled item 6 without guessing).

**Two user-locked decisions:** (1) **scope = systemic + flagged** — one governing `_LOCALIZE_HEADINGS_CLAUSE` appended to the **en/ru** medium rules localizes ALL scaffold headers + phase title + subject name at once (uz left byte-identical), plus reframe the ~5 prescribed literals. (2) **mechanism = inline language-relative** — reframe literals in the shared `.md` as *intent + per-language examples* (`open with a "not yet" opener in the output language — Uzbek «Hali emas», Russian «Пока нет», English «Not yet»`); no new template machinery; uz keeps «Hali emas» via the example.

**How (7 code tasks, SDD, controller stress-tested every commit — read diff==plan + re-ran tests):**
1. `prompts.py`: `_LOCALIZE_HEADINGS_CLAUSE` appended in `_resolve_language_rule` for en/ru only (uz byte-identical; frozen `test_prompts_output_language.py` stays green).
2. `practice-rlc.md`: «Hali emas» opener + `red herring` → language-relative (uz `chalg'ituvchi ma'lumot` / ru «отвлекающий факт»).
3. `boss-arena.md`: «Hali emas» opener → language-relative.
4. `case-based-preview.md`: **de-assert completion** (app owns pass/redo — no decided `passed`/`Needs Retry`, mirrors CQ-A reflection fix) + names the **two approved opening shapes** (storytelling / question-first, fun-fact hook) — closes WISHLIST `cbp-real-life-contract-1`.
5. `reflection.md`: Kuchli/Zaif headings + example strings → output-language (uz examples retained so `test_reflection_prompt.py` stays green; ru «Сильные/Слабые стороны» offered).
6. `content_lint.py`: broken-marker vocab now recognizes `N-yorliq`, `(BU BLOK XATO)`/`(Broken)`, `oshkor` reveal header (the two audited `errdet_no_broken_marker` **false-positives**); new narrow `ru_uzbek_leak` guard flags Uzbek tokens in ru output (regression guard for THIS fix). Docstring en-gate bullet (b23b988) left verbatim.
7. `scripts/check_prompt_render.py`: extended to uz/en/ru × 5 subjects = 165 combos, asserting no leftover `{{...}}` + the directive present for en/ru and absent for uz.

**Typos out of scope (verified):** Szenariy/keys-stadi/Shubham/davmidagi/chiqanda are model-generated, absent from `prompts/` — the brief's "fix only prompt-sourced typos" makes them a no-op; only `red herring` was prompt-sourced (fixed).

**Proof:** RED→GREEN each task; new suite `test_round2_localization.py` (8) + content_lint (+5, 36 total) + prompts_output_language (+3, 10 total); render sweep 165 combos OK; **full suite 1419 passed** (the 2 `test_failover_api` failures are PRE-EXISTING — reproduced on the clean base). **Paid api acceptance smoke (1 UZ + 1 RU lesson, human-read) HANDED TO GK2** — this host's `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` points to a missing (fleet) path, no GEMINI/ANTHROPIC keys; static render proof done, behavioral proof is the gate's (CQ-C/CQ-D precedent). **Worker/head restart required** (prompts cached at startup). No migration.

**Lane collision (GK2 P1):** shares `content_lint.py` + `tests/services/test_content_lint.py` with the `feat/extract-coverage-contract` lane; edits are append-style in different regions — whoever merges second rebases + hand-merges those two files.

Docs de-staled: `HOW_IT_WORKS.md`, `CODE_MAP.md`. Plan `git mv`'d to `docs/superpowers/plans/shipped/`.

## [0119] Extract coverage-contract — enumerated contract + structural Gate B + warn-only coverage check — 2026-07-06 (feat/extract-coverage-contract)

**Why (Phase-0 audit, `docs/research/2026-07-06-coverage-audit.md`):** nothing in the quality stack verified *lesson-core ⊆ packet*; every check grades the packet against the EXTRACT, so an under-summarizing extract is invisible downstream. Audit of 9 real edu_copy packets (5 subject families, 2 langs, one bounded gemini-3.1-pro judge each, ~$0.72): **91% coverage; 5/8 misses are EXTRACT-loss, 3/8 PHASE-loss.** Dominant, hand-verified: the extract systematically **drops the lesson's worked-example/problem types** (kimyo §13 lost BOTH → 71%; source `3-misol`/`4-misol` present, extract had none). And length ⊥ coverage — the FP §5 was 440c/100%.

**What (6 tasks, TDD, one plan/PR):**
1. `content_lint.parse_extract_contract` + `contract_has_items` — pure parser of the enumerated contract (fixed English headers, ordered `any()`-substring `_canonical_section`, lenient on `##/###`/case).
2. **Gate B structural** (`agent.validate_extract_summary`): refusal-marker → fail; parseable contract → pass regardless of length; else fall back to `extract_min_summary_chars` (lowered **400→120**, now a fallback floor only). Structure-AWARE not structure-REQUIRING → no new false-fail. **Closes `extract-gateb-short-lesson-fp-1`.**
3. Extract prompts (`_SUMMARIZE_LESSON_PROMPT` + `_SUMMARIZE_VISION_PROMPT`, shared `_CONTRACT_INSTRUCTIONS`) emit the enumerated contract with a required `## Worked-example types`; extract cache **`builtin:extract:v2`→`:v3`** (invalidates cross-job cache — each active book re-extracts once, flash-pinned, organic per-job).
4. `content_lint.lint_coverage` — deterministic warn-only: a contract item is "thin" only when its salient tokens (≥4 chars) are WHOLLY absent from the packet (conservative/under-reports); formulas excluded; aggregated to one `lint:coverage_thin` finding.
5. `pipeline._coverage_warnings_for_job` + a fail-open finalize hook (before `set_status "done"`) appends coverage findings to the extract row's `validation_warnings` (`guard=False` — the done row is frozen by default; idempotent append on tail-resume).
6. De-stale HOW_IT_WORKS + CODE_MAP.

**Decisions locked with user:** enumerated-markdown (not JSON/hybrid); warn-only deterministic (not gating — validate_toc/solver lesson); phase-side nudges deferred (measured first by this check); CQ-E `coverage` dimension deferred (contract parseable so CQ-E can add an offline dim). **No migration.** Composes with CQ-D fidelity guard unchanged.

**Proof (acceptance, real gemini api ~$0.90 lane total, DB-free):** (a) new §13 contract's `## Worked-example types` RECOVERS the isotope→avg-atomic-mass + composition+valence→mass types the old extract dropped; (b) compact §5 contract (1233c) Gate B PASS — the FP RED case; (c) coverage check fired warn-only on the real §13 packet (2/43 thin — sane); (d) flashcards generated from the NEW contract are high-quality Uzbek and pick up the flagged items (enumeration improves downstream coverage). Full suite 1420 pass / 196 skip / **2 PRE-EXISTING failover reds** (`cli-failover-tests-red-1`, reproduced on pristine base); CQ-D fidelity 22/22 UNMODIFIED; golden free tier 45/46. Final opus whole-branch review: all 8 constraints verified, SHIP (after rebase + finish). **Cascade: this merge unblocks the CQ-E baseline-freeze** (freeze now captures post-contract behavior). Residual (accepted, plan-approved): the 120 fallback floor lets an unmarked unstructured 120–400c refusal pass Gate B (structure-first + CQ-D + judge downstream cover it).

**Files:** `app/services/content_lint.py`, `app/services/agent.py`, `app/config.py`, `app/services/pipeline.py`, tests (`test_content_lint.py`, `test_agent.py`, `test_pipeline_coverage.py` new, `test_config_extract_robustness.py`, `test_extract_reuse_key.py`), docs.

## [0118] Solver-config editing + observability (R21.8) — 2026-07-06 (feat/solver-settings-badge → Nggaev-v2)

**Why:** CQ-C (0112) shipped the solver role with per-role columns (mig 0043) + resolution/claim-gate logic, but left it operator-*invisible* and *unadjustable* — the fleet default was settable only via the migration seed, and `solver_status` never reached the FE. R21.8 closes that gap; **all shipped resolution/claim-gate/pipeline logic is read-only to this change** (no `pipeline.py`/`model_tiers.py`/`worker.py`/claim-gate/migration edits).

**What (5 code commits, judge-mirror line-for-line):**
1. **Settings API** — solver joins `launch_defaults._MUTABLE`, `LaunchDefaultsOut`/`Update`, `_serialize`, and BOTH validation loops in `settings.py` (the required-concrete+manifest loop `("judge","solver","extract","content")` and the transport loop `("judge","solver","extract")`). **Required-concrete is not an open choice:** a null solver default stamps null → `_provider_api_ok(NULL)` → SQL NULL → the job is excluded from every worker's claim gate forever (the exact strand class the mig-0043 backfill closed; letting `/settings` reintroduce it would undo a shipped guard). Null provider/model → 422.
2. **`PhaseOut` serializes `solver_status`** (`app/schemas/job.py`) — it previously serialized `judge_status` only, so the FE badge had no data source. `from_attributes` reads the shipped column automatically.
3. **FE Solver settings row** (`settings.tsx` + `types.ts`) — clones the Judge `<RoleRow>` (state, sync effect, save-validation clause, save payload, header caption) + a self-grade hint line under the row.
4. **FE `solver_status` badge** (`preview.tsx`) — parallels the judge chip but with a status→color map (the judge chip is single-amber): **`mismatch_regen` = emerald/green** ("answer-key fixed" — the solver caught a wrong key and regenerated = a *success*), `mismatch_shipped`/`mismatch_regen_failed` = rose, `unavailable`/`refused` = amber, `ok` = no chip.
5. **Footgun warning** near BOTH content-model pickers (`launcher.tsx` inline; `section.tsx` via a `contentWarning` prop threaded into `AgentPicker` like `judgeWarning`) — fires when content = `gemini-3.1-pro-preview`, naming the self-grade-swap → unclaimable-on-keyless-fleet hazard.

**Decisions:** (a) required-concrete solver default (above); (b) **per-job solver override editors deferred** → filed as ROADMAP **R21.10** (pure-FE follow-up; the stamping path already honors per-job `solver_*` if a future UI sets them); (c) `mismatch_regen`=green is deliberate divergence from the judge chip's single style.

**Proof:** 6 new real-DB settings tests (`test_settings_launch_defaults.py`: solver GET-seed / PUT-override-persists / null-provider-422 / null-model-422 / off-manifest-422 / bad-transport-422) + 1 unit (`test_job_serialization.py::test_phaseout_serializes_solver_status`), all green (21 passed real-DB incl. pre-existing judge/extract/content). `test_launch_stamps_defaults.py` (CQ-C) already proves the PUT-default→job-stamp path. `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean after every FE commit. Full suite **1406 passed / 202 skipped + 2 PRE-EXISTING `test_failover_api.py` failures** (reproduced at pristine base `5a29d7c` in the main checkout — not this branch's regression). **Badge acceptance uses a real production catch:** the 2026-07-03 re-audit's G10 `memory-check` phase carries a genuine `solver_status='mismatch_regen'` in `edu_copy` (green chip demonstrates the feature on a real catch — GK2 eyeballs at the gate). No migration.

**Files:** `app/repositories/launch_defaults.py`, `app/api/v1/settings.py`, `app/schemas/job.py`, `web/src/lib/types.ts`, `web/src/routes/settings.tsx`, `web/src/routes/preview.tsx`, `web/src/components/fleet/launcher.tsx`, `web/src/routes/section.tsx`, + tests. Rebased clean onto `origin/Nggaev-v2` @ `5a29d7c`.

## [0122] TOC re-extract FK guard + dedup feedback — 2026-07-06 (fix/toc-reextract-fk)

**What:** closed WISHLIST `toc-reextract-fk-blocked-1`. TOC re-extraction was impossible for any book with jobs — `toc_extractor.run`'s clear-before-insert (`toc_repo.delete_for_book`) hits the NOT-NULL, no-cascade `homework_jobs.toc_entry_id` FK → `ForeignKeyViolationError`, the book flips `failed`, the old TOC survives (hit live on G6 matematika `bbaa260a`). Operator workaround was surgical DB edits.

**Fix (user-locked: refuse-only LOUD 409, no data surgery):** `POST /books/{id}/toc/retry` — the *sole* re-extract entrypoint that can reference a book with jobs (`ingest_pdf`'s sha-dedup returns early; new-book ingest has no jobs yet) — now counts referencing jobs via new `jobs_repo.list_for_book` (index-backed by `ix_homework_jobs_book_toc`) **before** it fires the extractor. Any referencing job (ANY status — the FK blocks `done`/`cancelled` too) → **409** listing the blocking ids+statuses (capped at 20 + a total count, GK2 B1), **book status untouched**. The operator deletes the affected sections (existing cascade flow in `delete_toc_entry`) then retries. `toc_extractor` internals unchanged — comment-only de-stale of the now-false "retry excludes `toc_ready` so no FK" claim. Rejected `?force`/repoint-by-title for v1 (title-drift/attribution risk → `toc-reextract-override-1`).

**Sibling gap — dedup feedback:** the sha-dedup hit returned a `toc_ready` book indistinguishable from a freshly-created one → FE toasted "Preparing" for an extraction that never runs. Added `BookOut.deduplicated` (additive, default `False`), set `True` on the dedup branch; FE `Book.deduplicated` + `upload.tsx` toast ("This book already exists — reusing it."). **Lane-C handoff:** the phantom "Preparing" toast at `launcher.tsx:128` (lane C's `prepare` mutation) branches on the same field — left for lane C, not touched here. Additive + defensive-consume → no merge-order constraint between the lanes.

**Proof (no paid calls):** real-DB FK-reality test (`tests/integration/test_toc_reextract_guard.py`, scratch DB pinned 127.0.0.1) pins the exact `IntegrityError` the guard preempts and `list_for_book` surfacing the blocker; endpoint unit tests RED-prove the 409 block, the pass-through, and the B1 cap; `BookOut.deduplicated` default + dedup-path tests; FE `tsc` clean. Suite **1439 passed** + the 2 pre-existing `test_failover_api` reds (`cli-failover-tests-red-1`). No migration.

## [0123] Notion multi-part subject clobber — clicked-page-authoritative + collision-safe parts — 2026-07-06 (fix/notion-multipart-clobber)

**Problem (hit live 2026-07-06, G6 matematika):** multi-part subjects («Matematika 1-qism»/«2-qism» — separate Notion pages, same `app_subject`) collapsed in `notion_fetch.available_languages` (per-language dict keyed by `app_subject` → **last page wins**), and BOTH FE prepare paths preferred that map over the page the operator actually clicked (`launcher.tsx:124` + **`upload.tsx:126`** — the 2nd consumer, not in the WISHLIST, had the identical bug). Root cause verified sharper than filed: the subject picker is **UZ-sourced** (`list_subjects` → `_SINF_RE`), so the clicked page is always a specific UZ part, yet `map[lang]?.page_id ?? clickedPage` overrode it **even for `lang === "uz"`** → preparing part 1 silently downloaded part 2, dedup returned the existing part-2 book, no extraction ran. Wrong-textbook class.

**Fix (both halves):**
- **Backend** — `available_languages` accumulates every part in `parts:[{page_id,title,has_textbook}]` per language instead of last-page-wins; keeps top-level `page_id` (= first part) + `has_textbook` so all existing consumers/tests stay byte-identical (`setdefault`-based slot). No migration.
- **FE** — extracted the buggy resolution + chip logic into a pure, `npx tsx`-unit-tested helper `web/src/lib/notion-parts.ts` (`partsFor`/`resolveNotionPageId`/`langChipState`) shared by launcher + upload. UZ output → clicked page authoritative (never consults the map); cross-language → translate via the map only when exactly ONE textbook part exists.
- **Locked decision (user, 2026-07-06):** multi-part cross-language switch = **disable chip + hint** ("N parts — pick a specific part / upload directly"). Zero wrong-book risk; a part-picker is deferred → WISHLIST `notion-crosslang-part-picker-1`.

**GK2 gate conditions folded in (approved, no re-gate):**
- **N1 (lane-B split):** honest dedup toast in launcher `prepare.onSuccess` — branches on a **defensively-read** `data.deduplicated` flag (`(data as {deduplicated?:boolean}).deduplicated ?? false`) → "Book already exists — reusing" vs "Preparing…". No `Book`-type edit, so **no ordering dependency** with lane B (whichever merges first, nothing breaks; toast lights up once B's backend flag exists). Only launcher gets the toast; upload's dedup handling is lane B's.
- **N2:** chip availability + resolution derive from **textbook-having parts** (`parts.filter(has_textbook)`), never the backward-compat top-level flag (pinned to first part → would render × for a language whose part-1 lacks a PDF but part-2 has one). Owned in the helper, proven by an explicit mixed-textbook test case (belt-and-suspenders: backend already filters no-PDF parts out of `parts`).

**Acceptance (no LLM calls, no Notion writes):** mocked two-part fixture proves both parts survive + top-level page_id = first part (`TestAvailableLanguagesMultiPart`, 3 tests); endpoint pass-through guard; helper unit test (13 assertions incl. UZ-click-authoritative, cross-lang single/multi/mixed, N2). `tsc` clean + `vite build` OK. Full suite **1439 passed** + 2 PRE-EXISTING failover reds (`test_failover_api.py`, `cli-failover-tests-red-1` — unrelated; this lane touches only notion_fetch + FE). Rebased onto `origin/Nggaev-v2` (3 docs-only base commits) before finish.

**Deploy note:** Notion-fetch + FE only → workers need no restart, but the running server's **FE bundle must be rebuilt/redeployed** to pick up the launcher/upload fix. Live G6 part-1 prepare = user-gated post-merge verify (GK2 drives). Collision map matched lanes A (ReadyCard region, disjoint) + B (dedup toast — N1 now consumes B's flag; second-to-merge rebases).

## [0124] cli-failover-tests-red-1 root-caused: host-PATH-dependent tests made hermetic — 2026-07-07 (Nggaev-v2 direct, GK2)

**Verdict: no code drift, no bug — the 2 "known reds" were environment-dependent tests.** `test_cli_failover_still_crosses_providers` + `test_api_failover_default_transport_is_cli` assert the cli chain reaches `{codex,kimi,opencode}`, but `_run_with_failover` **deliberately skips fallback providers whose CLI isn't on PATH** (`pipeline.py:808`, the fleet-failover-1 skip, checked via `agent.provider_cli_installed` → `shutil.which`). This Mac has only `claude`+`gemini` installed → the chain correctly stops at `{gemini}`. The tests passed in the CQ era only because the host then satisfied the PATH assumption — they were asserting host state, not code contract.

**Fix (test-only, 3 lines + fixture):** `all_clis_installed` fixture monkeypatches `agent.provider_cli_installed → True` in `tests/services/test_failover_api.py`, applied to the two cross-provider tests. Tests now exercise the chain logic hermetically; the production skip behavior is unchanged and remains correct (cli path stays kept-working per standing policy — tests kept, not deleted, despite cli being operationally retired).

**Canonical bar restored: full suite `1445 passed, 204 skipped, 0 failed`** — first fully-green suite since the CQ-era 1350–1404 runs. The "N passed + 2 known reds" caveat is dead; drop it from future gate reports. Closes WISHLIST `cli-failover-tests-red-1`.

## [0125] Stale fleet worker "Oliver" served the baseline freeze + all post-#84 jobs — 0120 correction — 2026-07-07 (GK2)

**Found while exporting an extract for the user:** production `edu_copy` has **zero `builtin:extract:v3` rows** — every extract since #84 merged (2026-07-06 14:37 EDT) is still `v2` flat-paragraph, including a row written **2026-07-07 09:44**, after the Mac server's restart on current code. Attribution via `homework_jobs.claimed_by`: the Windows fleet worker **`Oliver` (192.168.1.16, 6 live DB connections)** is claiming jobs while running **stale code (≥#80 — solver ran — but pre-#83/#84)**. The Mac server (::1, main checkout, v3) is current; Oliver outraces it on claims.

"Zero coverage_thin" was **vacuous** — the stale worker has no coverage hook at all. **The frozen baselines represent the pre-#84 extract system → re-freeze needed** (paid ~$3.70, user-gated) once the fleet is consistent.

## [0126] Solver → Boss-Arena coverage + live /settings toggle — 2026-07-07 (feat/solver-boss-arena)

**What:** closed WISHLIST `solver-boss-arena-1`. Extended the CQ-C answer-key solver to the boss-arena phase — the 2026-07-06 audit's only FLAG (UZ-G8-GEO `19f32884` Q3: "140° regular polygon impossible" graded correct; truth: exterior 40° → n=9, a nonagon exists) sat outside `_SOLVER_PHASES`, and both post-CQ live escapes were boss-arena key errors.

**Boss-aware contract (not a naive add-to-tuple):** boss-arena emits OPEN Why/How/What questions with no marked-correct option — the generic solver instructions don't fit. Added `solver._BOSS_ARENA_ADDENDUM` (threaded via `_build_solver_prompt(phase_name=)`): check each question's EMBEDDED objectively-decidable claim (computable value / math possibility / lesson-settled fact stated in Scenario·What·Feedback), flag `high` only on an objective error, and SKIP genuinely open/interpretive questions (zero-FP). `_SOLVER_PHASES += boss-arena`.

**Live operator toggle (user request — no more .env edits):** `launch_defaults.solver_boss_arena_enabled` (BOOL NOT NULL default true, **mig 0044**), operator-editable on `/settings` (checkbox), exposed via the settings API (`_MUTABLE` + Out/Update/`_serialize` + explicit-null-drop guard for the NOT NULL column). **Read live** off the singleton `pipeline.run` already loads (`_ld`), threaded through `_execute_one_phase`/`_run_content_phases_parallel`/`_execute_phase` (the call graph has two wrapper hops — the plan under-specified this; mirrored the `solver_model_ov` pattern) into the gate: `solver_enabled AND phase in _SOLVER_PHASES AND (phase != boss-arena OR toggle)`. Flip at `/settings` → every job started after honors it; no redeploy.

**Auto-regen (user-locked) + regen-adoption risk (GK2 C3, stated explicitly):** boss-arena inherits the existing solver-regen loop. The regen adopts the rewritten output WITHOUT re-judging; for boss-arena that re-rolls the entire boss fight (all questions + Correct/Partial/Wrong feedback ladders) — a materially larger un-re-judged surface than memory-check's key fix. Accepted on the solver's zero-FP history + high-conf-only gate. **Recall is probabilistic** (CQ-C ~1/3 on the hard classes); this lane adds *coverage* of the objective class, not a general guarantee — no "reliably catches" wording anywhere.

**Acceptance (GK2 C2 — IN-PROCESS, no queue; the stale worker Oliver still claims per 0125):** real gemini-3.1-pro-preview, `solver.solve()` called directly on boss outputs stored in `edu_copy` (usage redirected to a scratch DB so `edu_copy` is never written). Recall: `19f32884` Q3 → HIGH, solver independently derived "a regular polygon with interior angle 140° exists — a regular nonagon (n=9)" and located the claim in the Feedback line. Zero-FP: 4 human-audited golden-freeze boss packets (`17b8ccac`/`00e33c06`/`13939df2`/`031f5347`) → all `agrees`. (Those packets were generated by the stale worker per 0125 — irrelevant here: the smoke tests the NEW solver against stored outputs, not generation.) **Measured cost: 5 solve calls, 31,245 in / 7,333 out tok, $0.1505 total ($0.0301/call)** — matches the ~$0.03–0.05/job estimate. Suite 1454 passed (0 reds, post-0124). No `homework_jobs` change.

## [0127] TOC lesson filter — classify rows + LESSON-only batch default — 2026-07-07 (feat/toc-lesson-filter)

**What:** closed WISHLIST `batch-launch-lesson-filter-1`. Batch launch used to fire one job per TOC row with **no** lesson/non-lesson discrimination (≈ +$80/10-books + junk packets from `N-§` chapter umbrellas that page-swallow their children, answer keys, tests, biographies). Added a pure deterministic **TOC-row classifier** (`app/services/toc_classifier.py` — `classify_entries(entries)->list[str]`, classes `lesson`/`header`/`recall`/`revision`/`test`/`other`) and made `POST /jobs/batch` default to **LESSON-only** when no `toc_entry_ids` are sent; a new `include_classes` field widens the set; **explicit `toc_entry_ids` picks AND single-section `/generate` stay UNFILTERED** (operator override sacred).

**Design divergence (user-approved, diverges from the dispatch's directed persist-a-column shape):** the class is **computed on-the-fly, no DB column, no migration** (slot 0045 stays free) — HEADER detection is whole-book (page-containment vs siblings) and TOC rows are user-editable/deletable, so a persisted class would go stale on any edit and require whole-book recomputes on ≥3 mutation hooks. One pure classifier called at the read points self-heals and is trivially cheap (<100 rows/read, no LLM). Called at **three** serving/target points, all consistent: `books._enriched_toc_entries` (REST + SSE reconnect replay), `toc_extractor.run` (live `toc_ready`/`toc_review` SSE push — enriched so it doesn't emit `entry_class:null`), and the `batch.py` `targets` filter.

**Classifier:** precedence keyword → page-containment HEADER (candidate must be **multi-page** `page_end>page_start`, counts ALL other rows as potential children but only non-keyword rows are eligible to BE a header) → ALL-CAPS+single-page → default `lesson`. Apostrophe-normalize covers `ʼ ' ` ‘ ʻ` (real Uzbek titles use U+2018/U+02BB for o'/g' — without this, apostrophe keywords silently miss). Keyword tables carry UZ **and RU** back-matter (RU `упражнения к главе`→revision, `тестовые задания`→test, `исторические`/`межпредметные`→other) since RU books were the biggest recall gap.

**Accuracy gate (acceptance — no paid generation; this is launch plumbing):** `tests/services/test_toc_classifier_accuracy.py` runs against a **252-row hand-labeled fixture built from real `edu_copy` rows** (`tests/services/fixtures/toc_classifier_labels.json` — G8-Geometriya, G8-Algebra, G7-Algebra, G5-Matematika, G8-Algebra-RU; controller inspected + labeled every row, labels independent of the classifier). Result: **251/252 = 99.6%, ZERO false-exclusions** (no real lesson ever dropped — the critical asymmetric gate) and **1 accepted false-inclusion** (G7 «al-Xorazmiy» person-name biography → lesson; biographies aren't deterministically keyword-detectable — documented in the fixture's `accepted_false_inclusions` allowlist; tolerable direction, visible in the packet list, operator deselects). The test hard-asserts the false-inclusion set ⊆ allowlist so a new leak fails.

**⚠️ Number correction:** the WISHLIST/dispatch "~62 mavzu rows / 282-of-415 real lessons" **conflated mavzu-topic-numbers with TOC rows**. G8-Geometriya has 62 mavzu *numbers* but only **44 launchable lesson ROWS** (rows bundle pairs like «5–6-mavzu»; some `[N-mavzu]`-numbered rows are actually nazorat/tests). The RED-provable gate asserts G8-Geo targets **44, not 75** (a naive `targets=lessons` fails it). Same for the corpus: the ~282 figure counts topics, not launchable rows.

**FE:** launcher shows a per-row **class chip** (rendered from the server-served `entry_class` only — never re-derived), **per-class opt-in toggles** (lesson on by default; toggling drives `include_classes` + the counts), a class-filtered "remaining" count, and an **excluded-by-class breakdown** ("N rows hidden — X header · Y test …") as the operator's audit trail. Explicit checkbox picks stay unfiltered. `types.ts`/`api.ts` gained `entry_class` + `include_classes` + preview `target_count`/`excluded_by_class`.

**Proof:** full suite **1480 passed, 0 failed**; classifier structural 17, accuracy 1 (99.6%), batch class-filter API tests (default lesson-only / include_classes widen / explicit-override unfiltered / 422), SSE-carries-entry_class test; tsc exit 0 + vite build clean. **No migration, no enqueued jobs** (preview + DB-assertion acceptance only — the stale Windows worker Oliver [0125] would poison any queued acceptance). SDD: 5 tasks (one fresh subagent each, controller stress-tested every commit), Task-1 review caught a real containment-undercount bug, final whole-branch review (opus) READY-TO-MERGE → 2 latent Minors fixed (SSE enrich + multi-page header guard).

## [0128] Cross-process SSE bus — Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY — 2026-07-07 (feat/sse-multipod-bus)

**What:** closed WISHLIST `sse-multipod-1`. The event bus (`app/services/events_bus.py`) was an in-process `dict` of subscriber queues — fine while the embedded worker shares the API process, but a fleet-scale deploy (`WORKER_CONCURRENCY=0` API pods + dedicated worker pods) meant a publish from a worker pod never reached a browser connected to a different API pod: the SSE stream replayed initial state from the DB, then froze. Rewrote the bus onto Postgres `LISTEN/NOTIFY` on a single `hw_events` channel so any process's publish reaches every process's subscribers.

**Design:** `publish()`/`close()` are NOTIFY-only — no queue, no fan-out logic in-process — fired via the pooled engine on their OWN short-lived committed transaction (`pg_notify` is transactional; a publish inside the caller's still-open transaction would sit invisible until that transaction commits, so publish always opens+commits its own — C1 in progress.md). `close()` travels as a reserved `__close__` event so a subscriber's stream ends cleanly regardless of which process fired it. Payloads over 7000 encoded bytes (NOTIFY's practical size limit) collapse to a `__refetch__` marker — small hint fields survive individually capped at 512 bytes — and the SSE endpoints rebuild the full event from the DB on receipt: jobs rebuild `phase_completed` from `phase_outputs` (the same shape as the initial replay) and `error` from `job.error_message`; books rebuild `toc_ready`/`toc_review` through the shared `_enriched_toc_entries` helper (via `books_repo.get_with_toc` — `books_repo.get` alone lazy-loads the TOC relationship outside the session and throws `MissingGreenlet`, a reviewer-caught Critical against the plan's own draft code). One raw-asyncpg LISTEN connection per process is opened in `main.lifespan` (raises loudly on failure — no silent degrade to polling) with a 5s SELECT-1 watchdog and 1→30s backoff reconnect logged at `error` level. `tests/conftest.py` gained an autouse loopback (`_notify` → `_dispatch` in-process) so the unit suite stays DB-free; a `real_events_bus` marker opts a test out when it needs the genuine Postgres path.

**Proof:** a cross-process integration test (two separate DB connections/processes) failed with `TimeoutError` against the old in-process bus at commit `c1120ae`, passes now (2/2, 0.46s, scratch DB `edu_scratch_sse`) — including the C1 transactional-isolation case (publish inside an open transaction only becomes visible after that transaction's own commit). Canonical suite **1478 passed / 207 skipped / 0 failed**. No migration (schema slot 0045 stays free). No FE changes. $0 — no model calls, this is transport plumbing only.

**Review-gate catches (all fixed before merge):** (1) `_dispatch` raised on non-dict `data` — guarded; (2) the `__refetch__` marker key could collide with a legitimate event field of the same name — namespaced away; (3) **Critical** — the plan's own refetch code used `books_repo.get` for the book-refetch path, which `MissingGreenlet`s on the live (non-test) path because the TOC relationship is lazy-loaded outside the request's async session — fixed to `books_repo.get_with_toc`, the same helper the rest of the books surface already uses.

**DEPLOY NOTE:** the publishers live in the **worker processes**, not just the head — a head-only deploy changes nothing visible; the rollout is a **fleet pull + restart on every worker host**, same stale-worker discipline as the "Oliver" incident (worklog 0125) — a worker still running the old in-process bus publishes into a dict nobody else reads, and its jobs' live progress silently freezes for any browser not attached to that exact process. Also: **listener failure now blocks API startup by design** (no silent fallback) — when a stream looks frozen fleet-wide, grep worker/head logs for `events_bus: LISTEN connection DOWN`.

## [0129] Notion archive stale detection + auto-replace-own-output — 2026-07-07 (feat/notion-archive-stale-detect)

**What:** closed WISHLIST `notion-archive-stale-detect-1` (2nd live hit: G7-alg chip said 47/47 archived while 4 pages held pre-regen content — `archive_job`'s skip-if-populated still stamped `notion_archived_at`, so the rollup counted "has page", never "page matches latest job"). Root-cause fix + visibility, per the user-locked "Both" scope.

**Stamp (mig 0045):** `toc_entries.notion_archived_job_id` UUID NULL (no FK) — which job's content is actually on the lesson's Notion page. Written via `toc_repo.set_notion_archived_job` iff `first_archive or do_replace` (i.e. only when the push actually wrote); a populated page under our own "Generated Homeworks" container is always our own output (no human-page adoption), which is what makes auto-replace safe.

**Auto-replace (direction-guarded — GK2 C1):** `archive_job` now computes `auto_replace` inside the first session block: stamp non-NULL AND ≠ this job AND **this job strictly newer by `created_at`** (one extra `jobs_repo.get`). A regen self-heals its own stale page at the automatic post-job archive; an OLDER job re-archiving over a newer stamp (retried pre-regen push) keeps today's skip + logs loudly — without the guard it would have actively rewritten stale content over fresh. Missing stamped-row also keeps the skip. `force=True` stays the only direction-blind path. Stamp-NULL populated pages (pre-0129 husks) keep the safe skip — remediate once with the existing `?force=true` blanket sweep.

**Visibility:** `archive_rollup_for_batch` → `{archived, unarchived, stale}` (strict: archived AND stamped AND stamp ≠ latest done job; NULL stamp never stale — no «all-N stale» scream at deploy); `done_stale_job_ids` targeted worklist; `POST /jobs/batch/{id}/retry-archive?stale=true` sweeps only the husks with force; FE funnel shows amber "· N stale" + a "Refresh stale (N)" button (batch-actions). `archived`/`unarchived` byte-identical (1:1 inner join on the NOT-NULL FK; re-extract can't orphan it thanks to #87's 409).

**Proof:** RED→GREEN per task; controller bite-verified the C1 guard (breaking it fails exactly the 2 direction tests); real-DB slices re-run on fresh scratch DBs (mig test, 10/10 batch-rearchive incl. the stale-sweep endpoint proving `force=True` reaches `archive_job`); full suite **1509 passed, 0 failed** on the rebased head (over #89+#90); tsc 0 errors + vite build clean. No paid model calls (archive plumbing). Final whole-branch review (opus): **Ready to merge, 0 findings**; 2 Minors accepted (pre-existing per-leaf partial-write corner; log wording). SDD: 5 tasks, fresh subagent each, controller stress-tested every commit.

Enforcement lint for the measured compliance classes (setup-pre-answers-checkpoint 3/9, giveaway distractors 5/9 — n=9 audit, both classes violate rules the prompt ALREADY had) **deferred** — revisit only if the next audit shows persistence.

## [0131] TOC classifier de-math-calibration — practice class + subject vocab + homoglyph fold — 2026-07-08 (fix/toc-classifier-subject-vocab)

**What:** closed WISHLIST `toc-classifier-subject-vocab-1`. The #89/0127 classifier was math-calibrated: on the 6 generatable G8 non-math books it passed labs/practicals/problem-solving/English-review rows as LESSON. Added a 7th class **`practice`** (user-locked; operator can opt it back in via `include_classes`), subject vocabulary, a Cyrillic-homoglyph fold, and extended the accuracy fixture 252→**551 rows** (all 6 G8 non-math books + 3 targeted math decision-pin subsets).

**Wishlist corrections (verified by running the shipped classifier over all 293 stored G8 rows):** the filing over-counted — «…bobni takrorlash uchun test topshiriqlari» ×5, «Umumlashtiruvchi takrorlash», «Ilovalar», «…javoblari» were ALREADY caught by existing keywords. True misses: physics ×23 (11 «Masalalar yechish» + 5 «Laboratoriya ishi.» + 2 «Amaliy mashg'ulot.» + 5 «…yuzasidan muhim xulosalar»), biology ×7 (6 «N-laboratoriya mashg'uloti.» + usage-rules), english ×12 (7 keyword-detectable + 5 undetectable), geografiya ×2. The geografiya homoglyph claim did NOT reproduce (0 mixed-script titles stored) — fold kept as cheap defensive hardening, applied symmetrically to titles AND keyword tables (RU keywords keep matching). Wishlist's `life skills`/`culture` keywords DROPPED — stored english rows lost the printed prefixes at extraction ("Scotland", "ICT Literacy: …") → labeled `other` + 5-row `accepted_false_inclusions` allowlist (junk→lesson direction, operator-visible).

**Anchoring (the load-bearing subtlety):** `masalalar yechish` is **whole-title-anchored** — as a substring it false-EXCLUDES real math lessons («Kvadrat tenglamalar yordamida masalalar yechish» g8alg, «…masalalar yechishning koordinatalar usuli» g8geo). `amaliy mashg'ulot` exact (g8geo lesson «Amaliy mashq va tatbiq» is a near-miss). English `^review\b` anchored at title start.

**C1 (GK2 blocking fold-in) — intended math behavior change, pinned:** bare whole-title «Masalalar yechish» flips lesson→practice on MATH books too — G9-Geometriya `1bc43831` #22/25/32 (§23/26/33, printed-mundarija-verified) + G11 Matematika P1 `9bc1ad5e` #6/#11, P2 `bd7b2d6b` #2. Pinned in the fixture as 3 targeted subset books (`g9geo_my`/`g11mat_my_p1`/`g11mat_my_p2`) labeled `practice`, so the flip is a decision, not an accident. **⚠️ OPERATOR NOTE: G9-Geometriya default LESSON-only launch drops 48→45 rows (G11 P1 22→20, P2 17→16; physics 65→42, biology 68→61, english 22→15, geografiya 61→57 lessons). Intended — problem-solving/lab sessions are practice, not new-concept lessons; NOT a regression. Re-include via the `practice` checkbox.**

**Proof:** accuracy gate 545/551 = 98.9%, ZERO false-exclusions, practice 32/32 exact; old 5 fixture books byte-identical. Live read-only proof over 9 stored books: old→new classifier diff shows the ONLY math flips are the 6 pinned C1 rows; G8 books match the plan's verification-targets table exactly. Full suite **1518 passed, 0 failed**; tsc + vite build clean. No migration, no model calls, $0. FE: `practice` added to `ALL_ENTRY_CLASSES`+`CLASS_META` (load-bearing — an unlisted class can never be checkbox-included).
